# Plan 5: Goals Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Savings goals end to end: a monthly-savings goal with a Strict or Flexible mode chosen in setup, deadline goals with the reality check, planned contributions every payday, milestones and completion, confirmed savings transfers, cycle close (leftover to savings, rollover, Strict debt, Flexible shortfall), the catch-up plan (tighten, move, lower, take) with undo, and the app's Goals tab, New goal sheet, goal detail and Home's over-budget catch-up card.

**Architecture:** A new `goals` feature package on the server owns the `goals`, `goal_contributions`, `catch_up_actions` and `goal_milestones` tables. The money rules live in pure calculators (`GoalMath`, `PaydayCalendar`, `GoalProgress`, `RealityCheck`, `CatchUpMath`, `Milestones`, `CycleClose`) tested without Spring. `budget` never depends on `goals`: it gains three small hook interfaces that `goals` implements (`BudgetAdjustment` for pool changes and the catch-up card on `GET /budget/today`, `CycleOpenedListener` for payday work), plus `cycles.carried_paise` for rollover and debt; `profile` gains a `SavingsFloor` hook so savings can't drop below what goals need. In the app, a `features/goals` folder holds the client, hooks, the Goals tab, the New goal and edit form sheets, goal detail and the catch-up card, which Home also renders in its new over-budget state.

**Tech Stack:** Server: Java 21, Spring Boot 4.1.1, Spring Data JPA, Flyway, PostgreSQL 17, Testcontainers, JUnit 5, AssertJ, MockMvc. App: Expo SDK (Plan 2's version), Expo Router (form sheets), TypeScript strict, TanStack Query v5, Jest with React Native Testing Library, Maestro for E2E-06.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-gullak-v1-design.md` sections 2.1 (cycle close rules), 2.2 (savings and goals), 2.7 (off-track is Plan 8), 3.2 #3 and #5, 4.5, 4.6 (Goals and Today rows); test cases in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-gullak-v1-test-cases.md`; rules in `docs/engineering-standards.md` and `.claude/design.md`. Mockups quoted verbatim from the design canvas: `Goals`, `NewGoal`, `HomeOver`, `Setup` (savings step) and `Badges`.

**Builds on:** Plan 1 (implemented: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/**`), Plan 2 (app foundation and the first-cycle pool: `CurrentCycle`, `FirstCycle`, `BudgetService.compute`, `ProfileService.lockForGuard`, `SavingsScreen`, `useFinishSetup`, `HomeScreen`, `Hero`, `Stepper`, `Chip`, `RadioCard`), Plan 3 (`XpService.award(UUID userId, XpKind kind, String refType, UUID refId) : int`, `XpKind(int xp, Integer dailyCap)`, the `xp_events` table, and `SpendTotalsRepository.totals(...) : SpendTotals` with `spentBeforeToday()`, `spentToday()`, `extraPoolPaise()`) and Plan 4 (`in.gullak.ingestion.imports.SavingsTransferListener#onSavingsTransfer(SavingsTransfer)` called for MAT-19 inside the import commit, and `in.gullak.gamification.BadgeTrigger#fire(UUID userId, String trigger, UUID refId)`). Where those plans changed a file this plan edits, keep their additions and apply only the changes shown here; each such spot says so.

## Global Constraints

- Migrations for this plan use V020–V039; if an earlier plan was executed with more migrations than planned, renumber to the next free number in this block. This plan uses V020 and V021.
- Money is `long` paise on the server and whole-number paise in the app. No `double`, `float` or `BigDecimal` for amounts. Goal contributions round up to ₹100; expected-by-today and every daily figure round down to whole rupees.
- Day boundaries are `Asia/Kolkata` (`Ist.today(clock)`); paydays come from the profile's payday rule through `CycleCalculator.payday`.
- Every query on user data filters by the user id from the JWT. Another user's goal, contribution or catch-up returns 404 `NOT_FOUND`, never 403.
- Error bodies are `{code, message}`; codes come from `ErrorCode`, which is append-only (add before `INTERNAL`). Each new code gets a message in `app/src/lib/errors.ts` in the same task (`messages` is `Record<ErrorCode, string>`, so a missing one fails `npm run typecheck`).
- Never log amounts, goal names, merchant text, emails or tokens. Business events use `Events` constants with ids, kinds and counts only.
- Package-private `*Entity` and `*Repository` per feature; public records at the service boundary; controllers never touch repositories, entities or `JdbcClient`. Concurrency-sensitive writes use native `on conflict do nothing`, conditional updates or the profile row lock (`ProfileService.lockForGuard`), never read-then-write in Java alone.
- `budget` and `profile` never import `goals`; `goals` may import `budget`, `profile`, `gamification`, `ingestion` and `common`. The ArchUnit slice rule (`featuresAreFreeOfCycles`) must stay green.
- App: routes only under `app/app/`; features never call `fetch`; money only through `lib/money.ts`, dates only through `lib/dates.ts`; every screen renders loading, error (with Retry) and empty states; every control is at least 44×44 with a label; selected chips and radio cards show the 2px ink border.
- Copy follows `.claude/design.md`: sentence case; a single sentence has no full stop; two or more sentences take a full stop after each; ₹ with Indian grouping; dates as "Fri, 23 Oct" (with the year when it is not this year's cycle: "Sun, 1 Nov 2026"); months as "Mar 2027".
- Limits: goal name 1–40 characters on one line; target ₹1 to ₹99,99,999 (`Money.MAX_AMOUNT_PAISE`); deadline at most 120 months after the current month; at most 20 deadline goals that are active or paused.
- Each task's owner is named; after the owner hands back, `qa-engineer` runs, then `red-team` where the task says so, then `manager` reviews (`docs/team.md`). The server suite (`cd server && ./gradlew test`) and the app suite (`cd app && npm test && npm run lint && npm run typecheck`) stay green after every task.
- Commit messages end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

## Review Focus

- Three goals created in parallel that together need more than the monthly savings: only the ones that fit are saved, the rest get 422 `NOT_POSSIBLE`. Pinned in Task 3 (`parallelCreatesNeverPromiseMoreThanSavings`).
- A double-tapped catch-up option (two parallel `POST /goals/{id}/catch-up`) records one action and lowers tomorrow's budget once. Pinned in Task 8 (`doubleTappedTightenRecordsOneAction`).
- Four requests racing on the first day of a new cycle open it once, close the old cycle once and record each goal's planned contribution once. Pinned in Task 6 (`racingFirstRequestsCloseOnceAndPlanOnce`).
- Lowering monthly savings in Me or the Goals tab below what active goals need is refused with the amount they need. Pinned in Task 4 (`savingsBelowGoalCommitmentsIsRefused`).
- Undo after payday (the catch-up's cycle has closed) is refused, and the old cycle's tighten no longer touches the new cycle's budget. Pinned in Task 8 (`undoAfterTheCycleClosedIsRefused`).

## Decisions made in this plan

| Decision | Why | Cost if wrong |
|---|---|---|
| A goal's paydays are the paydays strictly after the day it was created, up to the payday in its deadline month. The current cycle's savings are not touched | The New goal mockup counts "10 paydays from 1 Nov 2026 to 1 Aug 2027" on 23 Oct; this cycle's savings came out on 1 Oct | a goal made the day after payday waits almost a month for its first contribution |
| Deadlines are months. The API sends `"2027-03"`; the column stores the month's last day; copy says "Mar 2027" | The New goal mockup picks a month; "15 Mar 2027" in the Goals and HomeOver mockups is illustrative | no mid-month deadlines |
| Each payday, when the cycle opens, every active deadline goal gets one `PLANNED` contribution (amount left ÷ paydays left including this one, rounded up to ₹100, capped at the amount left). It counts as saved at once ("savings come out first"). The monthly goal's free share is computed, not stored | One row per goal per cycle, guarded by a unique index, is easy to audit and to undo; storing the free share would go stale when savings change mid-cycle | a user who never opens the app for a whole cycle has no cycle row, so no planned row, and the goal shows behind until Plan 6's daily job opens cycles for everyone |
| Confirmed transfers (MAT-19) are evidence, not money: they appear on the goal as "Confirmed by bank" and do not add to saved | Saved already includes the planned contribution; adding the transfer would count the same money twice | a user who transfers more than planned does not see the extra in saved (they can raise the target) |
| Monthly savings is one `MONTHLY` goal per user whose target is always the profile's savings (`target_paise` null). Setup creates it with the chosen mode; it is created Flexible on first use for anyone without one; it cannot be paused or deleted | One source of truth for the savings amount; spec 2.2's "monthly target" and its mode need a home | none |
| "Tighten" spreads the gap over the days after today and takes effect tomorrow; today's budget is unchanged. It is offered only with at least two days left and when the gap fits tomorrow's pool. The gap is recorded on the goal at once as an `EXTRA` contribution | GOAL-11 ("8 days left") and the HomeOver mockup ("tomorrow's budget ₹577 → ₹352") both describe it from tomorrow; changing a half-spent day surprises people | someone who tightens at 8 am does not see today's number change (the card and Home show tomorrow's) |
| A fourth catch-up kind, `TAKE`, covers spec 2.2's Flexible "take the shortfall from this cycle's contribution" when the pool is below zero. It takes from unassigned savings first, then Flexible goals (largest share first), never Strict ones | Spec 2.2 names the behaviour but the numbered catch-up list covers only a goal that is behind | none |
| At cycle close the default is leftover → extra saved on the monthly goal, and a shortfall → carried into the next pool as debt, for both modes. Only a user's pick (Plan 7's cycle summary, or `TAKE` during the cycle) takes it from savings. The carry lives in `cycles.carried_paise`; `leftover_choice` stays null until the user picks | "Goals can slip, but only when the user picks that" (2.2); BUD-24 and BUD-25 | none |
| A cycle that ended is closed when the next one opens (lazily) as well as by Plan 6's job; both call `CycleCloseService.closeEndedBefore` | Until Plan 6 ships, nobody's leftover would otherwise be recorded | a spend logged late for a closed cycle changes its history (BUD-29) but not its recorded leftover |
| `catch_up_actions` stores typed columns (`gap_paise`, `per_day_paise`, `days`, `previous_deadline`, `new_deadline`, `previous_target_paise`, `new_target_paise`, `amount_paise`, `effective_from`) instead of spec 4.5's `details` jsonb | Undo reads these values back; typed columns get check constraints and map through JPA without JSON plumbing | a schema change per new kind |
| One active catch-up per goal per cycle (partial unique index). After a pick the card shows "catching up" with Undo for the rest of the cycle | The mockup fix in `ux-test-findings.md` ("kept saying ₹1,800 behind after a plan was picked") | a user who wants two options must undo first |
| The Lower result reads "Done. Goa trip's target is now ₹28,200." without the mockup's "and you're on track" | Target minus gap (spec) still leaves the goal behind by the formula (₹28,200 × 2/5 = ₹11,280 > ₹10,200) | a copy change |
| Milestones at 25, 50 and 75% give +50 XP each; 100% is completion, +1,000 XP and the Goal crusher trigger | Spec 2.2 promises XP for milestones; the XP table in 2.6 has no row for them | a number in `XpKind` |
| Goal crusher fires through Plan 4's `BadgeTrigger` interface with a new constant `GOAL_CRUSHER = "goal_crusher"`. Production has no implementation until Plan 6, whose `BadgeService` implements `BadgeTrigger` (the call stays as written) and can backfill from `goals.completed_at` | Plan 6 owns badges; Plan 4 already set the seam; the durable fact (the goal is done) is stored | a goal finished before Plan 6 ships earns its badge only through Plan 6's backfill |
| `PUT /me/profile` refuses savings below the sum of active goals' monthly contributions (422 `NOT_POSSIBLE`, "Your goals need ₹10,300 a month. Pause or change a goal first.") through a `SavingsFloor` hook in `profile` | GOAL-20's "free savings never negative" must hold from both sides | none |
| The deadline picker is year chips over a grid of month chips, not a native picker | A month-only native picker does not exist on Android, the `@expo/ui` `Picker` API is not confirmed for this SDK and would put 120 rows in a menu, and chips work on web and in Jest. Plan 2 made the same call for message times | a less native feel than a wheel |
| Nine error codes are appended: `DEADLINE_PASSED`, `ALREADY_PAUSED`, `NOT_PAUSED`, `STRICT_GOAL`, `NOT_BEHIND`, `CATCH_UP_ACTIVE`, `NOTHING_TO_UNDO`, `GOAL_DONE`, `MONTHLY_GOAL` | Each 409 or 422 in the goals API needs its own message; API-GL-24's "409" gets `NOTHING_TO_UNDO` | none |
| The Goals header's "+₹1,700 more expected" line is left out until Plan 7's forecast exists | It is a forecast, and Plan 7 owns forecasts | none |
| Home and the Goals tab refetch their data each time their tab gains focus (route-level `useFocusEffect` invalidation) | Goal changes made on other screens, by the cycle close or by E2E-06's test seam must show without a restart; `staleTime` alone would hide them for 30 seconds | one extra request per tab switch |
| E2E-06 forces a goal behind through `POST /api/v1/dev/goals/{id}/fall-behind`, which exists only under the `local` profile and moves the goal's creation date back two months | "Force behind (test data)" needs a seam the Maestro flow can reach; Plan 2's dev OTP endpoint set the pattern | none |
| Test-case updates (done by the executor in Task 14): API-GL-01's contribution becomes 600000; API-GL-24 names `NOTHING_TO_UNDO`; API-GL-27 checks the milestone through `GET /goals/{id}` and `xp_events` (Plan 6 adds `/me/progress`); GOAL-19's badge half becomes "Goal crusher trigger fired"; API-TDY-06 gains "situation POOL_NEGATIVE"; new rows GOAL-22, GOAL-23, BUD-33, BUD-34, API-GL-28 to API-GL-32, APP-34 to APP-36 | The example user creating Goa on 23 Oct has 5 paydays and nothing saved, so ₹30,000 ÷ 5 = ₹6,000, not ₹3,600 | none |

### Task 1: Pure goal rules and the cycle-close calculator

**Owner:** backend-engineer. **Test IDs:** GOAL-01 to GOAL-14, GOAL-18 (rule half), GOAL-20, GOAL-21 (rule half), BUD-22 to BUD-25 (rule half), GOAL-23 (new: shortfall order). **Red team:** no.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/CatchUpKind.java`
- Create: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalType.java`, `GoalMode.java`, `GoalStatus.java`, `GoalMath.java`, `PaydayCalendar.java`, `GoalProgress.java`, `Verdict.java`, `VerdictReason.java`, `RealityCheck.java`, `CatchUpMath.java`, `Milestones.java`, `LeftoverChoice.java`, `CycleClose.java`
- Test: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalMathTest.java`, `RealityCheckTest.java`, `CatchUpMathTest.java`, `CycleCloseTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CycleCalculator.payday(YearMonth, PaydayRule, Integer)` (Plan 1), `PaydayRule` (Plan 1).
- Produces: `GoalMath.contribution(long amountLeftPaise, int paydaysLeft) : long`, `GoalMath.expectedByToday(long targetPaise, int elapsed, int total) : long`, `GoalMath.behind(long, long) : long`, `GoalMath.percent(long saved, long target) : int`, `GoalMath.freeSavings(long, long) : long`, `GoalMath.RUPEE`, `GoalMath.HUNDRED_RUPEES`; `PaydayCalendar.between(LocalDate after, YearMonth lastMonth, PaydayRule, Integer) : List<LocalDate>`, `PaydayCalendar.next(LocalDate after, PaydayRule, Integer) : LocalDate`; `GoalProgress.of(long targetPaise, long savedPaise, LocalDate createdOn, YearMonth deadline, boolean active, LocalDate today, PaydayRule, Integer) : GoalProgress` with `paydaysTotal, paydaysElapsed, paydaysLeft, expectedPaise, behindPaise, percent, expectedPercent, contributionPaise`; `RealityCheck.check(long targetPaise, long savedPaise, int paydays, long freePaise) : RealityCheck.Result(Verdict, VerdictReason, contributionPaise, freePaise, freeAfterPaise, maxTargetPaise)`, `RealityCheck.TIGHT_LINE_PAISE`; `CatchUpMath.tightenPerDay(long gap, int days)`, `CatchUpMath.movedDeadline(YearMonth, long gap, long contribution)`, `CatchUpMath.loweredTarget(long, long)`, `CatchUpMath.allowed(GoalMode, GoalType) : Set<CatchUpKind>`; `Milestones.reached(long saved, long target) : List<Integer>`; `CycleClose.compute(long poolPaise, LeftoverChoice, List<CycleClose.Slice>) : CycleClose.Result`, `CycleClose.take(long shortfallPaise, List<Slice>) : List<CycleClose.Reduction>`, `CycleClose.defaultChoice(long poolPaise)`; enums `GoalType {MONTHLY, DEADLINE}`, `GoalMode {FLEXIBLE, STRICT}`, `GoalStatus {ACTIVE, PAUSED, DONE, DELETED}`, `Verdict {DOABLE, TIGHT, NOT_POSSIBLE}`, `VerdictReason {DEADLINE_PASSED, OVER_FREE_SAVINGS}`, `LeftoverChoice {SAVINGS, ROLLOVER, CARRY, TAKE}`, `CatchUpKind {TIGHTEN, MOVE, LOWER, TAKE}` (in `budget`). Plans 6 and 7 call `CycleClose` through Task 6's service.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`server/src/test/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalMathTest.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import in.gullak.profile.PaydayRule;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class GoalMathTest {

    @Test
    void contributionIsAmountLeftOverPaydaysLeft() { // GOAL-01: ₹30,000 goal, ₹12,000 saved, 5 paydays left
        assertThat(GoalMath.contribution(3_000_000 - 1_200_000, 5)).isEqualTo(360_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void contributionRoundsUpToHundredRupees() { // GOAL-02: ₹10,000 over 3 paydays
        assertThat(GoalMath.contribution(1_000_000, 3)).isEqualTo(340_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void contributionIsZeroWithNothingLeftOrNoPayday() {
        assertThat(GoalMath.contribution(0, 3)).isZero();
        assertThat(GoalMath.contribution(-500, 3)).isZero();
        assertThat(GoalMath.contribution(1_000_000, 0)).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void expectedByTodayIsTheElapsedShareOfTheTarget() { // GOAL-03: Goa, 2 of 5 paydays elapsed
        assertThat(GoalMath.expectedByToday(3_000_000, 2, 5)).isEqualTo(1_200_000L);
        assertThat(GoalMath.expectedByToday(3_000_000, 0, 5)).isZero();
        assertThat(GoalMath.expectedByToday(3_000_000, 5, 5)).isEqualTo(3_000_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void expectedByTodayRoundsDownToWholeRupees() {
        assertThat(GoalMath.expectedByToday(1_000_000, 1, 3)).isEqualTo(333_300L);
    }

    @Test
    void behindIsExpectedMinusSaved() { // GOAL-04
        assertThat(GoalMath.behind(1_200_000, 1_020_000)).isEqualTo(180_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void onTrackIsNeverNegativeBehind() { // GOAL-05
        assertThat(GoalMath.behind(1_200_000, 1_200_000)).isZero();
        assertThat(GoalMath.behind(1_200_000, 1_500_000)).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void percentIsFlooredAndCapped() {
        assertThat(GoalMath.percent(1_020_000, 3_000_000)).isEqualTo(34);
        assertThat(GoalMath.percent(4_000_000, 3_000_000)).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(GoalMath.percent(0, 3_000_000)).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void freeSavingsAreSavingsMinusPromises() { // GOAL-20, GOAL-21
        assertThat(GoalMath.freeSavings(2_000_000, 670_000 + 360_000)).isEqualTo(970_000L);
        assertThat(GoalMath.freeSavings(2_000_000, 0)).isEqualTo(2_000_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void paydaysAreStrictlyAfterCreationUpToTheDeadlineMonth() {
        assertThat(PaydayCalendar.between(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23), YearMonth.of(2027, 3), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 1))
            .containsExactly(LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 1), LocalDate.of(2026, 12, 1), LocalDate.of(2027, 1, 1),
                LocalDate.of(2027, 2, 1), LocalDate.of(2027, 3, 1));
    }

    @Test
    void deadlineThisPaydayIsOnePayday() { // GOAL-10
        assertThat(PaydayCalendar.between(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23), YearMonth.of(2026, 11), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 1))
            .containsExactly(LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 1));
        assertThat(GoalMath.contribution(3_000_000, 1)).isEqualTo(3_000_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void aMonthWhosePaydayHasPassedHasNoPayday() { // GOAL-09 input
        assertThat(PaydayCalendar.between(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23), YearMonth.of(2026, 10), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 1)).isEmpty();
        assertThat(PaydayCalendar.between(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23), YearMonth.of(2026, 8), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 1)).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void laterPaydayInTheSameMonthStillCounts() {
        assertThat(PaydayCalendar.between(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23), YearMonth.of(2026, 10), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 25))
            .containsExactly(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 25));
    }

    @Test
    void lastWorkingDayRuleSkipsWeekends() {
        assertThat(PaydayCalendar.between(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23), YearMonth.of(2026, 10), PaydayRule.LAST_WORKING_DAY, null))
            .containsExactly(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 30));
    }

    @Test
    void nextPaydayIsTheFirstOneAfterTheDay() {
        assertThat(PaydayCalendar.next(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 1)).isEqualTo(LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 1));
        assertThat(PaydayCalendar.next(LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 1), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 1)).isEqualTo(LocalDate.of(2026, 12, 1));
    }

    @Test
    void calendarStopsAfterTenYears() {
        assertThat(PaydayCalendar.between(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23), YearMonth.of(2126, 10), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 1))
            .hasSize(PaydayCalendar.MAX_MONTHS);
    }

    @Test
    void progressOfTheMockupGoa() { // Goals mockup: Goa ₹10,200 of ₹30,000, 34%, tick at 40%, behind ₹1,800
        GoalProgress p = GoalProgress.of(3_000_000, 1_020_000, LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 20), YearMonth.of(2027, 3), true,
            LocalDate.of(2026, 12, 23), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 1);
        assertThat(p.paydaysTotal()).isEqualTo(5);
        assertThat(p.paydaysElapsed()).isEqualTo(2);
        assertThat(p.paydaysLeft()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(p.expectedPaise()).isEqualTo(1_200_000L);
        assertThat(p.behindPaise()).isEqualTo(180_000L);
        assertThat(p.percent()).isEqualTo(34);
        assertThat(p.expectedPercent()).isEqualTo(40);
        assertThat(p.contributionPaise()).isEqualTo(660_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void pausedGoalPromisesNothing() { // GOAL-16 rule half
        GoalProgress p = GoalProgress.of(3_000_000, 0, LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23), YearMonth.of(2027, 3), false,
            LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23), PaydayRule.FIXED_DAY, 1);
        assertThat(p.contributionPaise()).isZero();
        assertThat(p.paydaysLeft()).isEqualTo(5);
    }

    @Test
    void milestonesReachedAreEveryQuarterAtOrBelowTheShare() { // GOAL-18 rule half
        assertThat(Milestones.reached(700_000, 3_000_000)).isEmpty();
        assertThat(Milestones.reached(750_000, 3_000_000)).containsExactly(25);
        assertThat(Milestones.reached(1_600_000, 3_000_000)).containsExactly(25, 50);
        assertThat(Milestones.reached(2_250_000, 3_000_000)).containsExactly(25, 50, 75);
        assertThat(Milestones.reached(3_000_000, 3_000_000)).containsExactly(25, 50, 75, 100);
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class RealityCheckTest {
    private static final long FREE = 970_000; // ₹20,000 savings − Emergency ₹6,700 − Goa ₹3,600

    @Test
    void doableLeavesAtLeast1500Free() { // GOAL-06: ₹60,000 over 10 paydays
        RealityCheck.Result r = RealityCheck.check(6_000_000, 0, 10, FREE);
        assertThat(r.verdict()).isEqualTo(Verdict.DOABLE);
        assertThat(r.contributionPaise()).isEqualTo(600_000L);
        assertThat(r.freeAfterPaise()).isEqualTo(370_000L);
        assertThat(r.reason()).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void tightLeavesLessThan1500Free() { // GOAL-07: ₹85,000 over 10
        RealityCheck.Result r = RealityCheck.check(8_500_000, 0, 10, FREE);
        assertThat(r.verdict()).isEqualTo(Verdict.TIGHT);
        assertThat(r.freeAfterPaise()).isEqualTo(120_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void exactly1500FreeIsStillDoable() {
        assertThat(RealityCheck.check(8_200_000, 0, 10, FREE).verdict()).isEqualTo(Verdict.DOABLE); // ₹8,200 a month leaves ₹1,500
    }

    @Test
    void notPossibleWhenTheContributionExceedsFreeSavings() { // GOAL-08: ₹1,00,000 over 10
        RealityCheck.Result r = RealityCheck.check(10_000_000, 0, 10, FREE);
        assertThat(r.verdict()).isEqualTo(Verdict.NOT_POSSIBLE);
        assertThat(r.reason()).isEqualTo(VerdictReason.OVER_FREE_SAVINGS);
        assertThat(r.contributionPaise()).isEqualTo(1_000_000L);
        assertThat(r.maxTargetPaise()).isEqualTo(9_700_000L); // ₹9,700 × 10
    }

    @Test
    void deadlinePassedIsNotPossible() { // GOAL-09
        RealityCheck.Result r = RealityCheck.check(3_000_000, 0, 0, FREE);
        assertThat(r.verdict()).isEqualTo(Verdict.NOT_POSSIBLE);
        assertThat(r.reason()).isEqualTo(VerdictReason.DEADLINE_PASSED);
    }

    @Test
    void savedMoneyLowersTheContribution() {
        assertThat(RealityCheck.check(3_000_000, 1_200_000, 5, FREE).contributionPaise()).isEqualTo(360_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void thirdGoalPastTheSavingsIsRejected() { // GOAL-20: three goals of ₹7,000 a month against ₹20,000
        long free = GoalMath.freeSavings(2_000_000, 700_000 + 700_000);
        assertThat(RealityCheck.check(700_000, 0, 1, free).verdict()).isEqualTo(Verdict.NOT_POSSIBLE);
        assertThat(RealityCheck.check(600_000, 0, 1, free).verdict()).isEqualTo(Verdict.TIGHT);
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import in.gullak.budget.CatchUpKind;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class CatchUpMathTest {

    @Test
    void tightenSpreadsTheGapOverTheDaysLeft() { // GOAL-11: gap ₹1,800, 8 days
        assertThat(CatchUpMath.tightenPerDay(180_000, 8)).isEqualTo(22_500L);
    }

    @Test
    void tightenRoundsUpToAWholeRupee() {
        assertThat(CatchUpMath.tightenPerDay(180_000, 7)).isEqualTo(25_800L); // ₹257.14 → ₹258
        assertThat(CatchUpMath.tightenPerDay(180_000, 0)).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void moveAddsThePaydaysThatCloseTheGap() { // GOAL-12: gap ₹1,800 at ₹3,600 a month
        assertThat(CatchUpMath.movedDeadline(YearMonth.of(2027, 3), 180_000, 360_000)).isEqualTo(YearMonth.of(2027, 4));
        assertThat(CatchUpMath.movedDeadline(YearMonth.of(2027, 3), 800_000, 360_000)).isEqualTo(YearMonth.of(2027, 6));
    }

    @Test
    void lowerTakesTheGapOffTheTarget() { // GOAL-13
        assertThat(CatchUpMath.loweredTarget(3_000_000, 180_000)).isEqualTo(2_820_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void strictGoalsOnlyTighten() { // GOAL-14
        assertThat(CatchUpMath.allowed(GoalMode.STRICT, GoalType.DEADLINE)).containsExactly(CatchUpKind.TIGHTEN);
        assertThat(CatchUpMath.allowed(GoalMode.FLEXIBLE, GoalType.DEADLINE))
            .containsExactly(CatchUpKind.TIGHTEN, CatchUpKind.MOVE, CatchUpKind.LOWER);
        assertThat(CatchUpMath.allowed(GoalMode.FLEXIBLE, GoalType.MONTHLY)).containsExactly(CatchUpKind.TAKE);
        assertThat(CatchUpMath.allowed(GoalMode.STRICT, GoalType.MONTHLY)).containsExactly(CatchUpKind.TAKE);
    }
}
```

`server/src/test/java/in/gullak/goals/CycleCloseTest.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class CycleCloseTest {
    private static final UUID MONTHLY = UUID.fromString("00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a");
    private static final UUID GOA = UUID.fromString("00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000b");
    private static final UUID FUND = UUID.fromString("00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000c");

    @Test
    void leftoverGoesToSavingsByDefault() { // BUD-22
        CycleClose.Result r = CycleClose.compute(170_000, CycleClose.defaultChoice(170_000), List.of());
        assertThat(r.leftoverPaise()).isEqualTo(170_000L);
        assertThat(r.extraSavedPaise()).isEqualTo(170_000L);
        assertThat(r.carriedPaise()).isZero();
        assertThat(r.reductions()).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void rolloverCarriesTheLeftover() { // BUD-23
        CycleClose.Result r = CycleClose.compute(170_000, LeftoverChoice.ROLLOVER, List.of());
        assertThat(r.extraSavedPaise()).isZero();
        assertThat(r.carriedPaise()).isEqualTo(170_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void shortfallIsCarriedAsDebtByDefault() { // BUD-24: Strict, pool −₹400
        CycleClose.Result r = CycleClose.compute(-40_000, CycleClose.defaultChoice(-40_000),
            List.of(new CycleClose.Slice(MONTHLY, true, GoalMode.STRICT, 2_000_000)));
        assertThat(r.leftoverPaise()).isEqualTo(-40_000L);
        assertThat(r.carriedPaise()).isEqualTo(-40_000L);
        assertThat(r.reductions()).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void flexibleShortfallComesOutOfThisCyclesContribution() { // BUD-25
        CycleClose.Result r = CycleClose.compute(-40_000, LeftoverChoice.TAKE,
            List.of(new CycleClose.Slice(GOA, false, GoalMode.FLEXIBLE, 360_000)));
        assertThat(r.reductions()).containsExactly(new CycleClose.Reduction(GOA, 40_000));
        assertThat(r.carriedPaise()).isZero();
        assertThat(r.takenPaise()).isEqualTo(40_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void takeUsesFreeSavingsFirstThenTheLargestFlexibleGoalAndNeverAStrictOne() { // GOAL-23
        List<CycleClose.Slice> slices = List.of(
            new CycleClose.Slice(FUND, false, GoalMode.STRICT, 670_000),
            new CycleClose.Slice(GOA, false, GoalMode.FLEXIBLE, 360_000),
            new CycleClose.Slice(MONTHLY, true, GoalMode.FLEXIBLE, 30_000));
        assertThat(CycleClose.take(100_000, slices))
            .containsExactly(new CycleClose.Reduction(MONTHLY, 30_000), new CycleClose.Reduction(GOA, 70_000));
    }

    @Test
    void whatCannotBeTakenIsStillCarried() {
        CycleClose.Result r = CycleClose.compute(-500_000, LeftoverChoice.TAKE,
            List.of(new CycleClose.Slice(GOA, false, GoalMode.FLEXIBLE, 360_000),
                new CycleClose.Slice(FUND, false, GoalMode.STRICT, 670_000)));
        assertThat(r.takenPaise()).isEqualTo(360_000L);
        assertThat(r.carriedPaise()).isEqualTo(-140_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void choicesMustMatchTheSignOfThePool() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> CycleClose.compute(170_000, LeftoverChoice.TAKE, List.of())).isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> CycleClose.compute(-40_000, LeftoverChoice.ROLLOVER, List.of())).isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class);
        assertThat(CycleClose.compute(0, LeftoverChoice.SAVINGS, List.of()).extraSavedPaise()).isZero();
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalMath.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

/** Spec 2.2 money rules for goals. Pure: no clock, no database. Amounts are paise. */
public final class GoalMath {
    public static final long RUPEE = 100L;
    public static final long HUNDRED_RUPEES = 100_00L;

    private GoalMath() {}

    /** Amount left ÷ paydays left, rounded up to ₹100 (GOAL-01, GOAL-02). Nothing left or no payday left gives 0. */
    public static long contribution(long amountLeftPaise, int paydaysLeft) {
        if (amountLeftPaise <= 0 || paydaysLeft <= 0) {
            return 0;
        }
        long perPayday = Math.ceilDiv(amountLeftPaise, (long) paydaysLeft);
        return Math.ceilDiv(perPayday, HUNDRED_RUPEES) * HUNDRED_RUPEES;
    }

    /** Target × paydays elapsed ÷ paydays in total, rounded down to a whole rupee (GOAL-03). */
    public static long expectedByToday(long targetPaise, int paydaysElapsed, int paydaysTotal) {
        if (paydaysTotal <= 0 || paydaysElapsed <= 0) {
            return 0;
        }
        if (paydaysElapsed >= paydaysTotal) {
            return targetPaise;
        }
        return targetPaise * paydaysElapsed / paydaysTotal / RUPEE * RUPEE;
    }

    /** How far saved is below expected; never negative (GOAL-04, GOAL-05). */
    public static long behind(long expectedPaise, long savedPaise) {
        return Math.max(0, expectedPaise - savedPaise);
    }

    /** Saved as a whole percentage of the target, floored, 0 to 100. */
    public static int percent(long savedPaise, long targetPaise) {
        if (targetPaise <= 0 || savedPaise <= 0) {
            return 0;
        }
        return (int) Math.min(100, savedPaise * 100 / targetPaise);
    }

    /** Monthly savings minus what is already promised to goals (GOAL-20, GOAL-21). */
    public static long freeSavings(long monthlySavingsPaise, long promisedPaise) {
        return monthlySavingsPaise - promisedPaise;
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/PaydayCalendar.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.CycleCalculator;
import in.gullak.profile.PaydayRule;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/** The paydays a goal counts, from the profile's payday rule. */
public final class PaydayCalendar {
    /** Ten years of months plus the current one: the furthest a deadline may be. */
    public static final int MAX_MONTHS = 121;

    private PaydayCalendar() {}

    /** Paydays strictly after {@code after}, up to and including the payday in {@code lastMonth}. */
    public static List<LocalDate> between(LocalDate after, YearMonth lastMonth, PaydayRule rule, Integer day) {
        List<LocalDate> out = new ArrayList<>();
        YearMonth month = YearMonth.from(after);
        for (int i = 0; i < MAX_MONTHS && !month.isAfter(lastMonth); i++, month = month.plusMonths(1)) {
            LocalDate payday = CycleCalculator.payday(month, rule, day);
            if (payday.isAfter(after)) {
                out.add(payday);
            }
        }
        return List.copyOf(out);
    }

    /** The first payday strictly after {@code after}. */
    public static LocalDate next(LocalDate after, PaydayRule rule, Integer day) {
        YearMonth month = YearMonth.from(after);
        LocalDate payday = CycleCalculator.payday(month, rule, day);
        return payday.isAfter(after) ? payday : CycleCalculator.payday(month.plusMonths(1), rule, day);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalProgress.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.profile.PaydayRule;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.List;

/** Where a deadline goal stands on a given day (spec 2.2). */
public record GoalProgress(
    int paydaysTotal,
    int paydaysElapsed,
    int paydaysLeft,
    long expectedPaise,
    long behindPaise,
    int percent,
    int expectedPercent,
    long contributionPaise) {

    /**
     * Paydays run from the first payday after {@code createdOn} to the payday in {@code deadline}. Only an
     * active goal promises a monthly contribution; a paused or done one promises nothing.
     */
    public static GoalProgress of(long targetPaise, long savedPaise, LocalDate createdOn, YearMonth deadline,
                                  boolean active, LocalDate today, PaydayRule rule, Integer day) {
        List<LocalDate> paydays = PaydayCalendar.between(createdOn, deadline, rule, day);
        int total = paydays.size();
        int elapsed = (int) paydays.stream().filter(p -> !p.isAfter(today)).count();
        int left = total - elapsed;
        long expected = GoalMath.expectedByToday(targetPaise, elapsed, total);
        long contribution = active ? GoalMath.contribution(targetPaise - savedPaise, left) : 0;
        int expectedPercent = total == 0 ? 100 : elapsed * 100 / total;
        return new GoalProgress(total, elapsed, left, expected, GoalMath.behind(expected, savedPaise),
            GoalMath.percent(savedPaise, targetPaise), expectedPercent, contribution);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/RealityCheck.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

/** Spec 2.2 reality check: does a goal's monthly contribution fit the user's free savings? */
public final class RealityCheck {
    /** Below ₹1,500 of free savings left over, a goal is "possible, but tight". */
    public static final long TIGHT_LINE_PAISE = 1_500_00L;

    private RealityCheck() {}

    public record Result(Verdict verdict, VerdictReason reason, long contributionPaise, long freePaise,
                         long freeAfterPaise, long maxTargetPaise) {}

    /** {@code freePaise} is monthly savings minus every other goal's contribution. */
    public static Result check(long targetPaise, long savedPaise, int paydays, long freePaise) {
        if (paydays <= 0) {
            return new Result(Verdict.NOT_POSSIBLE, VerdictReason.DEADLINE_PASSED, 0, freePaise, freePaise, 0);
        }
        long contribution = GoalMath.contribution(targetPaise - savedPaise, paydays);
        long perPaydayCap = Math.max(0, freePaise) / GoalMath.HUNDRED_RUPEES * GoalMath.HUNDRED_RUPEES;
        long maxTarget = savedPaise + perPaydayCap * paydays;
        long after = freePaise - contribution;
        if (contribution > freePaise) {
            return new Result(Verdict.NOT_POSSIBLE, VerdictReason.OVER_FREE_SAVINGS, contribution, freePaise, after, maxTarget);
        }
        return new Result(after < TIGHT_LINE_PAISE ? Verdict.TIGHT : Verdict.DOABLE, null, contribution, freePaise, after, maxTarget);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/CatchUpMath.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.CatchUpKind;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.EnumSet;
import java.util.Set;

/** Spec 2.2 catch-up numbers. Pure. */
public final class CatchUpMath {
    private CatchUpMath() {}

    /** Tighten: the gap spread over {@code days} days, rounded up to a whole rupee (GOAL-11). */
    public static long tightenPerDay(long gapPaise, int days) {
        if (gapPaise <= 0 || days <= 0) {
            return 0;
        }
        return Math.ceilDiv(Math.ceilDiv(gapPaise, (long) days), GoalMath.RUPEE) * GoalMath.RUPEE;
    }

    /** Move: the deadline goes out by the paydays the current contribution needs to close the gap (GOAL-12). */
    public static YearMonth movedDeadline(YearMonth deadline, long gapPaise, long monthlyContributionPaise) {
        long extra = Math.max(1, Math.ceilDiv(gapPaise, Math.max(1, monthlyContributionPaise)));
        return deadline.plusMonths(extra);
    }

    /** Lower: target minus gap (GOAL-13). */
    public static long loweredTarget(long targetPaise, long gapPaise) {
        return targetPaise - gapPaise;
    }

    /** GOAL-14: a Strict deadline goal only gets "spend less". Monthly savings only offer TAKE. */
    public static Set<CatchUpKind> allowed(GoalMode mode, GoalType type) {
        if (type == GoalType.MONTHLY) {
            return EnumSet.of(CatchUpKind.TAKE);
        }
        return mode == GoalMode.STRICT
            ? EnumSet.of(CatchUpKind.TIGHTEN)
            : EnumSet.of(CatchUpKind.TIGHTEN, CatchUpKind.MOVE, CatchUpKind.LOWER);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/Milestones.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import java.util.List;

/** Spec 2.2: milestones at 25, 50, 75 and 100% of a goal. */
public final class Milestones {
    public static final List<Integer> STEPS = List.of(25, 50, 75, 100);

    private Milestones() {}

    /** Every step at or below the saved share. The database makes each one happen once per goal. */
    public static List<Integer> reached(long savedPaise, long targetPaise) {
        int percent = GoalMath.percent(savedPaise, targetPaise);
        return STEPS.stream().filter(step -> percent >= step).toList();
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/CycleClose.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Spec 2.1 and 2.2 at cycle close: money left in the pool becomes extra saved or rolls over; a shortfall is
 * carried into the next pool as debt or, when the user picks that, taken from Flexible savings. Pure.
 */
public final class CycleClose {
    private CycleClose() {}

    /** One goal's share of this cycle's savings that a shortfall may come out of. {@code monthly} marks unassigned savings. */
    public record Slice(UUID goalId, boolean monthly, GoalMode mode, long amountPaise) {}

    public record Reduction(UUID goalId, long amountPaise) {}

    /**
     * {@code leftoverPaise} is the pool at close (negative when overspent); {@code extraSavedPaise} goes to the
     * monthly goal; {@code carriedPaise} moves the next cycle's pool (positive rollover, negative debt).
     */
    public record Result(long leftoverPaise, long extraSavedPaise, long carriedPaise, List<Reduction> reductions) {
        public long takenPaise() {
            return reductions.stream().mapToLong(Reduction::amountPaise).sum();
        }
    }

    public static LeftoverChoice defaultChoice(long poolPaise) {
        return poolPaise >= 0 ? LeftoverChoice.SAVINGS : LeftoverChoice.CARRY;
    }

    public static Result compute(long poolPaise, LeftoverChoice choice, List<Slice> slices) {
        if (poolPaise >= 0) {
            return switch (choice) {
                case SAVINGS -> new Result(poolPaise, poolPaise, 0, List.of());
                case ROLLOVER -> new Result(poolPaise, 0, poolPaise, List.of());
                case CARRY, TAKE -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("nothing is short in this cycle");
            };
        }
        return switch (choice) {
            case CARRY -> new Result(poolPaise, 0, poolPaise, List.of());
            case TAKE -> {
                List<Reduction> reductions = take(-poolPaise, slices);
                long taken = reductions.stream().mapToLong(Reduction::amountPaise).sum();
                yield new Result(poolPaise, 0, poolPaise + taken, reductions);
            }
            case SAVINGS, ROLLOVER -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("there is no leftover in this cycle");
        };
    }

    /** Unassigned savings first, then Flexible goals with the largest share first; Strict shares are never touched (GOAL-23). */
    public static List<Reduction> take(long shortfallPaise, List<Slice> slices) {
        List<Slice> order = slices.stream()
            .filter(s -> s.mode() == GoalMode.FLEXIBLE && s.amountPaise() > 0)
            .sorted(Comparator.comparing((Slice s) -> !s.monthly())
                .thenComparing(Slice::amountPaise, Comparator.reverseOrder())
                .thenComparing(s -> s.goalId().toString()))
            .toList();
        long left = shortfallPaise;
        List<Reduction> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Slice s : order) {
            if (left <= 0) {
                break;
            }
            long cut = Math.min(left, s.amountPaise());
            out.add(new Reduction(s.goalId(), cut));
            left -= cut;
        }
        return List.copyOf(out);
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.TestConfiguration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;

@Import(BudgetHooksApiTest.Hooks.class)
class BudgetHooksApiTest extends ApiTest {
    static final Instant EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE = Instant.parse("2026-10-10T06:00:00Z");
    static final Instant TODAY_COFFEE = Instant.parse("2026-10-23T03:42:00Z");

    @Autowired BudgetService budget;

    /** Hooks keyed by user, so the rest of the suite sharing this context is unaffected. */
    @TestConfiguration
    static class Hooks {
        static final Map<UUID, Long> DELTA = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        static final Map<UUID, LocalDate> DELTA_FROM = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        static final Map<UUID, CatchUp> CARDS = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        static final List<UUID> OPENED = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();

        @Bean
        BudgetAdjustment testAdjustment() {
            return new BudgetAdjustment() {
                @Override
                public long poolDeltaPaise(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, LocalDate day) {
                    LocalDate from = DELTA_FROM.get(userId);
                    return from != null && !day.isBefore(from) ? DELTA.getOrDefault(userId, 0L) : 0L;
                }

                @Override
                public Optional<CatchUp> catchUp(UUID userId, TodaySnapshot today) {
                    return Optional.ofNullable(CARDS.get(userId));
                }
            };
        }

        @Bean
        CycleOpenedListener testListener() {
            return (userId, cycleId, cycle, profile) -> OPENED.add(userId);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void tomorrowsBudgetIsOnTheResponse() throws Exception { // BUD-09: ₹720 of ₹593 spent today → tomorrow ₹577
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 966_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE);
        seedSpend(u, 72_000, TODAY_COFFEE);
        budgetToday(u)
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(59_300))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.tomorrowPaise").value(57_700))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp").doesNotExist());
    }

    @Test
    void theLastDayHasNoTomorrow() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-10-31T04:00:00Z"));
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.tomorrowPaise").doesNotExist());
    }

    @Test
    void anAdjustmentFromTomorrowLeavesTodayAlone() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 966_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE);
        Hooks.DELTA.put(u.id(), -180_000L);
        Hooks.DELTA_FROM.put(u.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 24));
        budgetToday(u)
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(59_300))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.tomorrowPaise").value(44_200)); // (5,340 − 1,800) ÷ 8 = 442.5
    }

    @Test
    void anAdjustmentFromTodayChangesTodayAtOnce() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 966_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE);
        Hooks.DELTA.put(u.id(), 40_000L);
        Hooks.DELTA_FROM.put(u.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 23));
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(63_700)); // (5,340 + 400) ÷ 9 = 637.7
    }

    @Test
    void theCatchUpCardComesFromTheHook() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        Hooks.CARDS.put(u.id(), new CatchUp(CatchUpSituation.BEHIND, UUID.randomUUID(), "Goa trip", false, 180_000,
            List.of(CatchUp.Option.lower(2_820_000)), null));
        budgetToday(u)
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.situation").value("BEHIND"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.goalName").value("Goa trip"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[0].kind").value("LOWER"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[0].newTargetPaise").value(2_820_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[0].perDayPaise").doesNotExist())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.applied").doesNotExist());
    }

    @Test
    void aRolloverFromTheClosedCycleRaisesTheNextPool() throws Exception { // BUD-23 budget half
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        closeOctober(u, 170_000);
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-11-01T04:00:00Z"));
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(55_600)); // (15,000 + 1,700) ÷ 30
    }

    @Test
    void aDebtFromTheClosedCycleLowersTheNextPool() throws Exception { // BUD-24 budget half: spendable ₹14,600
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        closeOctober(u, -40_000);
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-11-01T04:00:00Z"));
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(48_600)); // 14,600 ÷ 30 = 486.6
    }

    @Test
    void anOpenPreviousCycleCarriesNothing() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        jdbc.sql("update cycles set carried_paise = 170000 where user_id = ?").param(u.id()).update();
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-11-01T04:00:00Z"));
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(50_000));
    }

    @Test
    void theListenerHearsEachNewCycleOnce() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = newlySetUpUser();
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThat(Hooks.OPENED.stream().filter(u.id()::equals).count()).isEqualTo(1);
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-11-02T04:00:00Z"));
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThat(Hooks.OPENED.stream().filter(u.id()::equals).count()).isEqualTo(2);
    }

    @Test
    void poolAtCloseCountsTheWholeCycle() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 1_330_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE);
        assertThat(budget.poolAtClose(u.id(), cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01"))).isEqualTo(170_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void snapshotCarriesWhatGoalsNeed() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 966_000, EARLIER_THIS_CYCLE);
        seedSpend(u, 72_000, TODAY_COFFEE);
        TodaySnapshot s = budget.snapshot(u.id());
        assertThat(s.cycleId()).isEqualTo(cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01"));
        assertThat(s.daysLeft()).isEqualTo(9);
        assertThat(s.poolPaise()).isEqualTo(534_000L);
        assertThat(s.poolAfterTodayPaise()).isEqualTo(462_000L);
        assertThat(s.tomorrowPoolPaise()).isEqualTo(462_000L);
        assertThat(s.savingsPaise()).isEqualTo(2_000_000L);
    }

    private void closeOctober(TestUser u, long carried) {
        jdbc.sql("update cycles set closed_at = now(), carried_paise = ? where user_id = ? and start_date = '2026-10-01'")
            .params(carried, u.id()).update();
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;

class CycleServiceApiTest extends ApiTest {
    @Autowired CycleService cycles;

    @Test
    void markClosedHappensOnce() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID oct = cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01");
        assertThat(cycles.markClosed(u.id(), oct, Instant.parse("2026-11-01T00:00:00Z"), 170_000, 0)).isTrue();
        assertThat(cycles.markClosed(u.id(), oct, Instant.parse("2026-11-01T00:00:00Z"), 1, 1)).isFalse();
        CycleRecord r = cycles.find(u.id(), oct).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(r.closedAt()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(r.leftoverPaise()).isEqualTo(170_000L);
        assertThat(r.carriedPaise()).isZero();
        assertThat(r.leftoverChoice()).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void aChoiceIsRecordedOnceAndOnlyOnAClosedCycle() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID oct = cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01");
        assertThat(cycles.recordChoice(u.id(), oct, "ROLLOVER", 170_000)).isFalse(); // still open
        cycles.markClosed(u.id(), oct, Instant.parse("2026-11-01T00:00:00Z"), 170_000, 0);
        assertThat(cycles.recordChoice(u.id(), oct, "ROLLOVER", 170_000)).isTrue();
        assertThat(cycles.recordChoice(u.id(), oct, "SAVINGS", 0)).isFalse();
        assertThat(cycles.find(u.id(), oct).orElseThrow().carriedPaise()).isEqualTo(170_000L);
        assertThat(cycles.carriedInto(u.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 1))).isEqualTo(170_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void lookupsAreScopedToTheUser() throws Exception {
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        UUID aOct = cycleIdStarting(a, "2026-10-01");
        assertThat(cycles.find(b.id(), aOct)).isEmpty();
        assertThat(cycles.findAll(b.id(), List.of(aOct))).isEmpty();
        assertThat(cycles.markClosed(b.id(), aOct, Instant.now(), 0, 0)).isFalse();
        assertThat(cycles.covering(a.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 15)).map(CycleRecord::id)).contains(aOct);
        assertThat(cycles.covering(a.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 9, 15))).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void openCyclesEndingBeforeADateAreListedOldestFirst() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedCycle(u, "2026-11-01", "2026-11-30");
        assertThat(cycles.openEndingBefore(u.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 12, 1)))
            .extracting(r -> r.cycle().start()).containsExactly(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 1), LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 1));
        cycles.markClosed(u.id(), cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01"), Instant.now(), 0, 0);
        assertThat(cycles.openEndingBefore(u.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 12, 1))).hasSize(1);
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * A feature outside budget that moves a cycle's pool or explains how to get back on track. Goals implement it;
 * budget never imports goals, so the feature slices stay free of cycles.
 */
public interface BudgetAdjustment {
    /** Paise added to (positive) or taken from (negative) the cycle's pool for the budget of {@code day}. */
    long poolDeltaPaise(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, LocalDate day);

    /** The catch-up card for Home, if one applies today. */
    Optional<CatchUp> catchUp(UUID userId, TodaySnapshot today);
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/CatchUpSituation.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

/** BEHIND: a goal is short of where it should be. POOL_NEGATIVE: the cycle's pool ran out. APPLIED: a plan is in place. */
public enum CatchUpSituation { BEHIND, POOL_NEGATIVE, APPLIED }
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/CatchUp.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

/** The catch-up card on GET /budget/today and on a goal (spec 2.2). Built by goals through {@link BudgetAdjustment}. */
@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
public record CatchUp(
    CatchUpSituation situation,
    UUID goalId,
    String goalName,
    boolean strict,
    long gapPaise,
    List<Option> options,
    Applied applied) {

    /** One choice with the numbers its label shows; only the fields for its kind are set. */
    @JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
    public record Option(
        CatchUpKind kind,
        Long perDayPaise,
        Integer days,
        Long tomorrowPaise,
        YearMonth newDeadline,
        Integer extraPaydays,
        Long newTargetPaise,
        Long takePaise) {

        public static Option tighten(long perDayPaise, int days, long tomorrowPaise) {
            return new Option(CatchUpKind.TIGHTEN, perDayPaise, days, tomorrowPaise, null, null, null, null);
        }

        public static Option move(YearMonth newDeadline, int extraPaydays) {
            return new Option(CatchUpKind.MOVE, null, null, null, newDeadline, extraPaydays, null, null);
        }

        public static Option lower(long newTargetPaise) {
            return new Option(CatchUpKind.LOWER, null, null, null, null, null, newTargetPaise, null);
        }

        public static Option take(long takePaise) {
            return new Option(CatchUpKind.TAKE, null, null, null, null, null, null, takePaise);
        }
    }

    /** The plan picked this cycle; {@code canUndo} is false once the goal is done. */
    @JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
    public record Applied(
        UUID actionId,
        CatchUpKind kind,
        Long perDayPaise,
        Integer days,
        YearMonth newDeadline,
        Integer extraPaydays,
        Long newTargetPaise,
        Long takePaise,
        boolean canUndo) {}
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/TodaySnapshot.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import in.gullak.profile.PaydayRule;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Today's figures for features that build on the budget (the catch-up plan). {@code poolPaise} is the pool at the
 * start of today with today's adjustments; {@code tomorrowPoolPaise} is tomorrow's pool if nothing more is spent
 * today, with tomorrow's adjustments, or null on the cycle's last day.
 */
public record TodaySnapshot(
    UUID cycleId,
    LocalDate cycleStart,
    LocalDate cycleEnd,
    LocalDate date,
    int daysLeft,
    long poolPaise,
    long spentTodayPaise,
    long budgetPaise,
    Long tomorrowPoolPaise,
    PaydayRule paydayRule,
    Integer paydayDay,
    long savingsPaise) {

    /** Negative when today's spending (or earlier overspend) took the pool below zero. */
    public long poolAfterTodayPaise() {
        return poolPaise - spentTodayPaise;
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

/**
 * {@code adjustmentPaise} moves the spendable amount: the previous cycle's rollover or debt and today's catch-up
 * changes (Plan 5). Zero for the six-argument form.
 */
public record BudgetInput(
    long incomePaise,
    long fixedPaise,
    long savingsPaise,
    long spentBeforeTodayPaise,
    long spentTodayPaise,
    int daysLeftIncludingToday,
    long adjustmentPaise) {

    public BudgetInput {
        if (daysLeftIncludingToday < 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("daysLeftIncludingToday must be at least 1");
        }
    }

    public BudgetInput(long incomePaise, long fixedPaise, long savingsPaise, long spentBeforeTodayPaise,
                       long spentTodayPaise, int daysLeftIncludingToday) {
        this(incomePaise, fixedPaise, savingsPaise, spentBeforeTodayPaise, spentTodayPaise, daysLeftIncludingToday, 0);
    }

    /** A cycle whose pool is pinned (the first cycle): the pool is the spendable amount, nothing is subtracted. */
    public static BudgetInput ofSpendable(long spendablePaise, long spentBeforeTodayPaise, long spentTodayPaise, int daysLeftIncludingToday) {
        return new BudgetInput(spendablePaise, 0, 0, spentBeforeTodayPaise, spentTodayPaise, daysLeftIncludingToday, 0);
    }

    public BudgetInput withAdjustment(long adjustment) { // Plan 5
        return new BudgetInput(incomePaise, fixedPaise, savingsPaise, spentBeforeTodayPaise, spentTodayPaise,
            daysLeftIncludingToday, adjustment);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/BudgetCalculator.java` (only the `spendable` line changes):

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import in.gullak.content.Mood;

/** The money rules of spec section 2.1. Pure: no clock, no database. */
public final class BudgetCalculator {
    private BudgetCalculator() {}

    public static DailyBudget compute(BudgetInput in) {
        long spendable = in.incomePaise() - in.fixedPaise() - in.savingsPaise() + in.adjustmentPaise(); // Plan 5
        long pool = spendable - in.spentBeforeTodayPaise();
        long budget = pool > 0 ? pool / in.daysLeftIncludingToday() / 100 * 100 : 0;
        long spent = in.spentTodayPaise();
        long left = Math.max(0, budget - spent);
        long over = Math.max(0, spent - budget);
        long poolOver = Math.max(0, -pool);
        int percent = budget > 0 ? (int) Math.min(999, spent * 100 / budget) : (spent > 0 ? 999 : 0);
        Mood mood = pool < 0 || over > 0 ? Mood.FAINTED
            : budget == 0 ? Mood.NERVOUS
            : percent < 50 ? Mood.HAPPY
            : percent < 80 ? Mood.CALM
            : Mood.NERVOUS;
        return new DailyBudget(spendable, pool, budget, spent, left, over, poolOver, percent, mood);
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;
import in.gullak.content.Mood;
import java.time.LocalDate;

public record TodayResponse(
    LocalDate date,
    long budgetPaise,
    long spentPaise,
    long leftPaise,
    long overPaise,
    long poolOverPaise,
    Mood mood,
    String line,
    int daysLeft,
    LocalDate cycleStart,
    LocalDate cycleEnd,
    /** Tomorrow's budget if nothing more is spent today; absent on the cycle's last day. */
    @JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL) Long tomorrowPaise,
    /** The catch-up card (spec 2.2), absent when there is nothing to catch up. */
    @JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL) CatchUp catchUp) {}
```

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Read-only here: rows are created by {@link CycleRepository#insertIfAbsent} so concurrent first requests make one,
 * and changed only by the conditional updates in {@link CycleRepository}.
 */
@Entity
@Table(name = "cycles")
class CycleEntity {
    @Id
    private UUID id;

    @Column(name = "user_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID userId;

    @Column(name = "start_date", nullable = false)
    private LocalDate startDate;

    @Column(name = "end_date", nullable = false)
    private LocalDate endDate;

    @Column(name = "pool_override_paise")
    private Long poolOverridePaise;

    @Column(name = "closed_at")
    private Instant closedAt;

    @Column(name = "leftover_paise")
    private Long leftoverPaise;

    @Column(name = "leftover_choice", length = 16)
    private String leftoverChoice;

    @Column(name = "carried_paise", nullable = false)
    private long carriedPaise;

    protected CycleEntity() {}

    UUID getId() { return id; }
    LocalDate getStartDate() { return startDate; }
    Long getPoolOverridePaise() { return poolOverridePaise; }
    Instant getClosedAt() { return closedAt; }
    long getCarriedPaise() { return carriedPaise; }

    Cycle toCycle() { return new Cycle(startDate, endDate); }

    CycleRecord toRecord() {
        return new CycleRecord(id, toCycle(), poolOverridePaise, closedAt, leftoverPaise, leftoverChoice, carriedPaise);
    }
}
```

```java
    List<CycleEntity> findByUserIdAndIdIn(UUID userId, Collection<UUID> ids);

    /** The cycle that ended most recently before {@code date}: its carry, once closed, feeds the next pool. */
    Optional<CycleEntity> findFirstByUserIdAndEndDateBeforeOrderByEndDateDesc(UUID userId, LocalDate date);

    @Query("""
            select c from CycleEntity c where c.userId = :userId and c.closedAt is null and c.endDate < :date
            order by c.startDate
            """)
    List<CycleEntity> findOpenEndingBefore(@Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("date") LocalDate date);

    /** Closes a cycle once: a second caller (job and request racing) changes nothing. */
    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true, clearAutomatically = true)
    @Query(value = """
            update cycles set closed_at = :at, leftover_paise = :leftover, carried_paise = :carried, updated_at = now()
            where id = :id and user_id = :userId and closed_at is null
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    int markClosed(@Param("id") UUID id, @Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("at") OffsetDateTime at,
                   @Param("leftover") long leftover, @Param("carried") long carried);

    /** Records the user's leftover choice once, on a closed cycle only. */
    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true, clearAutomatically = true)
    @Query(value = """
            update cycles set leftover_choice = :choice, carried_paise = :carried, updated_at = now()
            where id = :id and user_id = :userId and closed_at is not null and leftover_choice is null
            """, nativeQuery = true)
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/CycleService.java` (Plan 2's file with the listener and the new public methods):

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import in.gullak.common.db.Db;
import in.gullak.common.error.ErrorCode;
import in.gullak.common.error.UnprocessableException;
import in.gullak.common.log.Events;
import in.gullak.profile.Profile;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.ObjectProvider;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
public class CycleService {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(CycleService.class);

    private final CycleRepository cycles;
    /** Looked up lazily: listeners (goals) depend on budget services, so a constructor list would be a bean cycle. */
    private final ObjectProvider<CycleOpenedListener> listeners;

    CycleService(CycleRepository cycles, ObjectProvider<CycleOpenedListener> listeners) {
        this.cycles = cycles;
        this.listeners = listeners;
    }

    /**
     * The cycle covering {@code today}, created on first use. After a payday change the current cycle
     * keeps its end date; the next one starts the day after it and ends the day before the new payday.
     * A user's very first cycle, created mid-cycle, gets a pro-rated pool (spec 2.1). Listeners hear about
     * each cycle this call creates, inside the same transaction.
     */
    @Transactional
    public CurrentCycle current(UUID userId, LocalDate today, Profile profile, long monthlySpendablePaise) {
        Optional<CycleEntity> existing = cycles.findCovering(userId, today);
        if (existing.isPresent()) {
            return toCurrent(userId, existing.get());
        }
        Cycle natural = CycleCalculator.containing(today, profile.paydayRule(), profile.paydayDay());
        LocalDate start = cycles.latestEndBefore(userId, today)
            .map(end -> end.plusDays(1))
            .filter(s -> s.isAfter(natural.start()))
            .orElse(natural.start());
        Cycle cycle = new Cycle(start, natural.end());
        boolean first = cycles.countEndingBefore(userId, cycle.start()) == 0;
        Long pool = first ? FirstCycle.proRate(monthlySpendablePaise, today, cycle) : null;
        UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
        if (cycles.insertIfAbsent(id, userId, cycle.start(), cycle.end(), pool) == 1) {
            log.info("{} user={} days={} proRated={}", Events.CYCLE_OPENED, userId, cycle.days(), pool != null);
            listeners.orderedStream().forEach(l -> l.cycleOpened(userId, id, cycle, profile)); // Plan 5
        }
        return cycles.findCovering(userId, today)
            .map(e -> toCurrent(userId, e))
            .orElseGet(() -> new CurrentCycle(null, cycle, pool, first));
    }

    /** Pins the pool of the caller's first, still-open cycle to what they said they have left. */
    @Transactional
    public void setFirstCyclePool(UUID userId, CurrentCycle current, long poolPaise) {
        if (!current.first()) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.FIRST_CYCLE_OVER, "Your first cycle has ended, so this can no longer be changed");
        }
        if (current.id() == null || cycles.setPoolOverride(current.id(), userId, poolPaise) == 0) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.FIRST_CYCLE_OVER, "Your first cycle has ended, so this can no longer be changed");
        }
        log.info("{} user={} cycle={}", Events.CYCLE_POOL_SET, userId, current.id());
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Optional<CycleRecord> find(UUID userId, UUID cycleId) {
        return cycles.findByIdAndUserId(cycleId, userId).map(CycleEntity::toRecord);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<CycleRecord> findAll(UUID userId, Collection<UUID> cycleIds) {
        if (cycleIds.isEmpty()) {
            return List.of();
        }
        return cycles.findByUserIdAndIdIn(userId, cycleIds).stream().map(CycleEntity::toRecord).toList();
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Optional<CycleRecord> covering(UUID userId, LocalDate date) {
        return cycles.findCovering(userId, date).map(CycleEntity::toRecord);
    }

    /** Cycles that ended before {@code date} and have not been closed, oldest first. */
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<CycleRecord> openEndingBefore(UUID userId, LocalDate date) {
        return cycles.findOpenEndingBefore(userId, date).stream().map(CycleEntity::toRecord).toList();
    }

    /** What the cycle before the one starting on {@code cycleStart} passes on; zero while that cycle is still open. */
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public long carriedInto(UUID userId, LocalDate cycleStart) {
        return cycles.findFirstByUserIdAndEndDateBeforeOrderByEndDateDesc(userId, cycleStart)
            .filter(c -> c.getClosedAt() != null)
            .map(CycleEntity::getCarriedPaise)
            .orElse(0L);
    }

    /** True only for the caller that closed it. */
    @Transactional
    public boolean markClosed(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, Instant at, long leftoverPaise, long carriedPaise) {
        boolean closed = cycles.markClosed(cycleId, userId, Db.ts(at), leftoverPaise, carriedPaise) == 1;
        if (closed) {
            log.info("{} user={} cycle={}", Events.CYCLE_CLOSED, userId, cycleId);
        }
        return closed;
    }

    /** True only the first time, and only on a closed cycle. */
    @Transactional
    public boolean recordChoice(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, String choice, long carriedPaise) {
        return cycles.recordChoice(cycleId, userId, choice, carriedPaise) == 1;
    }

    private CurrentCycle toCurrent(UUID userId, CycleEntity e) {
        return new CurrentCycle(e.getId(), e.toCycle(), e.getPoolOverridePaise(), cycles.countEndingBefore(userId, e.getStartDate()) == 0);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/budget/BudgetService.java` (Plans 2 and 3's file reorganised around one `compute` that returns both the response and the snapshot; Plan 6 adds its own methods next to `today` against this `compute`):

```java
package in.gullak.budget;

import in.gullak.common.error.NotFoundException;
import in.gullak.common.time.Ist;
import in.gullak.content.GullakLines;
import in.gullak.profile.FixedCostService;
import in.gullak.profile.Profile;
import in.gullak.profile.ProfileService;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.ObjectProvider;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
public class BudgetService {
    private final ProfileService profiles;
    private final FixedCostService fixedCosts;
    private final CycleService cycles;
    private final SpendTotalsRepository totals;
    private final ObjectProvider<BudgetAdjustment> adjustments;
    private final Clock clock;

    BudgetService(ProfileService profiles, FixedCostService fixedCosts, CycleService cycles,
                  SpendTotalsRepository totals, ObjectProvider<BudgetAdjustment> adjustments, Clock clock) {
        this.profiles = profiles;
        this.fixedCosts = fixedCosts;
        this.cycles = cycles;
        this.totals = totals;
        this.adjustments = adjustments;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    /** One transaction so every figure comes from the same snapshot; read-write because it may open a cycle. */
    @Transactional
    public TodayResponse today(UUID userId) {
        return compute(userId, open(userId), true).response();
    }

    /** Today's figures for goals (Plan 5), without the catch-up card. */
    @Transactional
    public TodaySnapshot snapshot(UUID userId) {
        return compute(userId, open(userId), false).snapshot();
    }

    /** The caller's current cycle, opened if needed. */
    @Transactional
    public CurrentCycle currentCycle(UUID userId) {
        return open(userId).current();
    }

    @Transactional
    public TodayResponse setFirstCyclePool(UUID userId, long moneyLeftPaise) {
        Opened opened = open(userId);
        cycles.setFirstCyclePool(userId, opened.current(), moneyLeftPaise);
        return compute(userId, open(userId), true).response();
    }

    /** The pool once the whole cycle is counted, with its carry and catch-up changes; negative when overspent. */
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public long poolAtClose(UUID userId, UUID cycleId) {
        CycleRecord c = cycles.find(userId, cycleId).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Cycle"));
        Profile profile = profiles.get(userId);
        LocalDate after = c.cycle().end().plusDays(1);
        // "Today" is the day after the cycle: every spend, spread spend, refund and income share of the cycle is "before".
        SpendTotals t = totals.totals(userId, Ist.startOf(c.cycle().start()), Ist.startOf(after), Ist.startOf(after));
        long base = c.poolOverridePaise() != null ? c.poolOverridePaise() : spendable(profile, fixedCosts.totalFor(userId));
        return base + t.extraPoolPaise() + cycles.carriedInto(userId, c.cycle().start()) + delta(userId, cycleId, after)
            - t.spentBeforeToday();
    }

    private record Opened(Profile profile, LocalDate today, long fixed, CurrentCycle current) {}

    private record Computed(TodayResponse response, TodaySnapshot snapshot) {}

    private Opened open(UUID userId) {
        Profile profile = profiles.get(userId);
        LocalDate today = Ist.today(clock);
        long fixed = fixedCosts.totalFor(userId);
        return new Opened(profile, today, fixed, cycles.current(userId, today, profile, spendable(profile, fixed)));
    }

    private static long spendable(Profile profile, long fixed) {
        return profile.incomePaise() - fixed - profile.savingsPaise();
    }

    private Computed compute(UUID userId, Opened o, boolean withCatchUp) {
        Cycle cycle = o.current().cycle();
        UUID cycleId = o.current().id();
        LocalDate today = o.today();
        SpendTotals t = totals.totals(userId, Ist.startOf(cycle.start()), Ist.startOf(today), Ist.startOf(today.plusDays(1)));
        long before = t.spentBeforeToday();
        long spentToday = t.spentToday();
        int daysLeft = (int) ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(today, cycle.end()) + 1;
        long carried = cycles.carriedInto(userId, cycle.start());                      // Plan 5
        DailyBudget b = BudgetCalculator.compute(input(o, t.extraPoolPaise(), before, spentToday, daysLeft)
            .withAdjustment(carried + delta(userId, cycleId, today)));
        Long tomorrow = null;
        Long tomorrowPool = null;
        if (daysLeft > 1) {                                                            // Plan 5
            DailyBudget next = BudgetCalculator.compute(input(o, t.extraPoolPaise(), before + spentToday, 0, daysLeft - 1)
                .withAdjustment(carried + delta(userId, cycleId, today.plusDays(1))));
            tomorrow = next.budgetPaise();
            tomorrowPool = next.poolPaise();
        }
        Profile profile = o.profile();
        TodaySnapshot snapshot = new TodaySnapshot(cycleId, cycle.start(), cycle.end(), today, daysLeft, b.poolPaise(),
            spentToday, b.budgetPaise(), tomorrowPool, profile.paydayRule(), profile.paydayDay(), profile.savingsPaise());
        CatchUp catchUp = withCatchUp ? catchUp(userId, snapshot) : null;
        TodayResponse response = new TodayResponse(today, b.budgetPaise(), b.spentTodayPaise(), b.leftPaise(), b.overPaise(),
            b.poolOverPaise(), b.mood(), GullakLines.line(profile.personality(), b.mood(), b.leftPaise(), b.totalOverPaise()),
            daysLeft, cycle.start(), cycle.end(), tomorrow, catchUp);
        return new Computed(response, snapshot);
    }

    /** Plan 3's rule: the pool share of extra income is added to income, or to the first cycle's pinned pool. */
    private static BudgetInput input(Opened o, long extraPoolPaise, long before, long spentToday, int daysLeft) {
        return o.current().poolOverridePaise() != null
            ? BudgetInput.ofSpendable(o.current().poolOverridePaise() + extraPoolPaise, before, spentToday, daysLeft)
            : new BudgetInput(o.profile().incomePaise() + extraPoolPaise, o.fixed(), o.profile().savingsPaise(), before, spentToday, daysLeft);
    }

    private long delta(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, LocalDate day) {
        if (cycleId == null) {
            return 0;
        }
        return adjustments.orderedStream().mapToLong(a -> a.poolDeltaPaise(userId, cycleId, day)).sum();
    }

    private CatchUp catchUp(UUID userId, TodaySnapshot snapshot) {
        return adjustments.orderedStream()
            .map(a -> a.catchUp(userId, snapshot))
            .flatMap(Optional::stream)
            .findFirst()
            .orElse(null);
    }
}
```
