- [ ] **Step 1: Migration**

`server/src/main/resources/db/migration/V021__goals.sql`:

```sql
-- Goals (spec 2.2, 4.5). Monthly savings is one MONTHLY goal per user whose target is the profile's monthly
-- savings, so its target_paise and deadline stay null. A deadline goal's deadline is the last day of its month.
create table goals (
    id uuid primary key,
    user_id uuid not null references users (id) on delete cascade,
    name varchar(40) not null,
    type varchar(10) not null check (type in ('MONTHLY', 'DEADLINE')),
    target_paise bigint,
    deadline date,
    mode varchar(10) not null check (mode in ('FLEXIBLE', 'STRICT')),
    status varchar(10) not null check (status in ('ACTIVE', 'PAUSED', 'DONE', 'DELETED')),
    created_cycle_id uuid references cycles (id) on delete set null,
    -- The IST day the goal was made: its paydays are the ones after this day.
    created_on date not null,
    completed_at timestamptz,
    created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    check ((type = 'MONTHLY' and target_paise is null and deadline is null)
        or (type = 'DEADLINE' and target_paise > 0 and deadline is not null))
);
create unique index goals_one_monthly_idx on goals (user_id) where type = 'MONTHLY' and status <> 'DELETED';
create index goals_user_idx on goals (user_id) where status <> 'DELETED';

-- One picked catch-up plan (spec 2.2). Typed columns instead of a details blob: undo reads them back.
create table catch_up_actions (
    id uuid primary key,
    goal_id uuid not null references goals (id) on delete cascade,
    user_id uuid not null references users (id) on delete cascade,
    cycle_id uuid not null references cycles (id) on delete cascade,
    kind varchar(10) not null check (kind in ('TIGHTEN', 'MOVE', 'LOWER', 'TAKE')),
    gap_paise bigint not null check (gap_paise > 0),
    amount_paise bigint check (amount_paise > 0),
    per_day_paise bigint,
    days int,
    previous_deadline date,
    new_deadline date,
    previous_target_paise bigint,
    new_target_paise bigint,
    effective_from date not null,
    undone_at timestamptz,
    created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
-- At most one live plan per goal per cycle: a double tap cannot apply two.
create unique index catch_up_one_active_idx on catch_up_actions (goal_id, cycle_id) where undone_at is null;
create index catch_up_user_cycle_idx on catch_up_actions (user_id, cycle_id);

-- The savings ledger. SHORTFALL rows are negative: savings handed back to the pool.
create table goal_contributions (
    id uuid primary key,
    goal_id uuid not null references goals (id) on delete cascade,
    user_id uuid not null references users (id) on delete cascade,
    cycle_id uuid not null references cycles (id) on delete cascade,
    amount_paise bigint not null,
    kind varchar(20) not null check (kind in ('PLANNED', 'EXTRA', 'MANUAL', 'CONFIRMED_TRANSFER', 'SHORTFALL')),
    catch_up_id uuid references catch_up_actions (id) on delete cascade,
    transaction_id uuid references transactions (id) on delete set null,
    occurred_on date,
    created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    check ((kind = 'SHORTFALL' and amount_paise < 0) or (kind <> 'SHORTFALL' and amount_paise > 0))
);
create unique index goal_contributions_planned_once_idx on goal_contributions (goal_id, cycle_id) where kind = 'PLANNED';
create unique index goal_contributions_transfer_once_idx on goal_contributions (transaction_id) where transaction_id is not null;
create index goal_contributions_user_goal_idx on goal_contributions (user_id, goal_id);
create index goal_contributions_user_cycle_idx on goal_contributions (user_id, cycle_id);

create table goal_milestones (
    id uuid primary key,
    goal_id uuid not null references goals (id) on delete cascade,
    user_id uuid not null references users (id) on delete cascade,
    percent int not null check (percent in (25, 50, 75, 100)),
    reached_at timestamptz not null,
    created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    unique (goal_id, percent)
);
```

```java
package in.gullak.common.error;

import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;

/** 409: the request is valid but the object is in a state that does not allow it (engineering standards 2). */
public class ConflictException extends GullakException {
    public ConflictException(ErrorCode code, String message) {
        super(code, HttpStatus.CONFLICT, message);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Test helpers**

Add to `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/support/ApiTest.java`:

```java
    /** POST /goals for a deadline goal; returns its id. */
    protected String createGoal(TestUser user, String name, long targetPaise, String deadline, String mode) throws Exception {
        String body = mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(user)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("{\"type\":\"DEADLINE\",\"name\":\"" + name + "\",\"targetPaise\":" + targetPaise
                    + ",\"deadline\":\"" + deadline + "\",\"mode\":\"" + mode + "\"}"))
            .andExpect(status().isCreated())
            .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        return JsonPath.read(body, "$.goal.id");
    }

    /** Test data: a deadline goal as if it was made on {@code createdOn}, so its early paydays have passed. */
    protected UUID seedGoal(TestUser user, String name, long targetPaise, String deadlineMonth, String mode, String createdOn) {
        UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
        jdbc.sql("""
                insert into goals (id, user_id, name, type, target_paise, deadline, mode, status, created_on)
                values (?, ?, ?, 'DEADLINE', ?, (?::date + interval '1 month' - interval '1 day')::date, ?, 'ACTIVE', ?::date)
                """)
            .params(id, user.id(), name, targetPaise, deadlineMonth + "-01", mode, createdOn)
            .update();
        return id;
    }

    /** Test data: money already on a goal (an EXTRA row, so it never collides with a planned one). */
    protected void seedContribution(TestUser user, UUID goalId, UUID cycleId, long paise) {
        jdbc.sql("insert into goal_contributions (id, goal_id, user_id, cycle_id, amount_paise, kind) values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'EXTRA')")
            .params(UUID.randomUUID(), goalId, user.id(), cycleId, paise)
            .update();
    }
```

```java
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.type").value("DEADLINE"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.deadline").value("2027-03"))

    @Test
    void checkGivesEachVerdictWithoutSaving() throws Exception { // API-GL-09, GOAL-06 to GOAL-08
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        createGoal(u, "Emergency fund", 6_030_000, "2027-07", "STRICT"); // 9 paydays → ₹6,700
        createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 1_800_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");       // 5 paydays → ₹3,600
        check(u, 6_000_000, "2027-08")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.verdict").value("DOABLE"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.freePaise").value(970_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.contributionPaise").value(600_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.freeAfterPaise").value(370_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.paydays").value(10))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.nextPayday").value("2026-11-01"));
        check(u, 8_500_000, "2027-08").andExpect(jsonPath("$.verdict").value("TIGHT")).andExpect(jsonPath("$.freeAfterPaise").value(120_000));
        check(u, 10_000_000, "2027-08")
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.verdict").value("NOT_POSSIBLE"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.reason").value("OVER_FREE_SAVINGS"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.maxTargetPaise").value(9_700_000));
        check(u, 3_000_000, "2026-08")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.verdict").value("NOT_POSSIBLE"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.reason").value("DEADLINE_PASSED"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.paydays").value(0));
        assertThat(countGoals(u, "DEADLINE")).isEqualTo(2);
    }

            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.monthly.split").isEmpty())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals").isEmpty());

            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals[0].expectedPaise").value(0))

                .content("{\"targetPaise\":100000,\"deadline\":\"2027-03\",\"goalId\":\"" + goa + "\"}"))
            .andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(b))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals").isEmpty());

    @Test
    void detailGroupsContributionsByCycle() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        seedContribution(u, UUID.fromString(goa), cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01"), 500_000);
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.savedPaise").value(500_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cycles[0].cycleStart").value("2026-10-01"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cycles[0].cycleEnd").value("2026-10-31"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cycles[0].extraPaise").value(500_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cycles[0].totalPaise").value(500_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.transfers").isEmpty());

    @Test
    void aThirdGoalPastTheSavingsIsRejected() throws Exception { // GOAL-20
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        createGoal(u, "One", 700_000, "2026-11", "FLEXIBLE");
        createGoal(u, "Two", 700_000, "2026-11", "FLEXIBLE");
        postGoal(u, deadlineJson("Three", 700_000, "2026-11", "FLEXIBLE"))
            .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_POSSIBLE"));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.monthly.freeNextPaise").value(600_000));
    }

    @Test
    void parallelCreatesNeverPromiseMoreThanSavings() throws Exception { // Review focus, API-GL-28
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(3);
        try {
            CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);
            List<Future<Integer>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                String json = deadlineJson("Goal " + i, 900_000, "2026-11", "FLEXIBLE");
                Callable<Integer> call = () -> {
                    start.await();
                    return postGoal(u, json).andReturn().getResponse().getStatus();
                };
                futures.add(pool.submit(call));
            }
            start.countDown();
            List<Integer> statuses = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Future<Integer> f : futures) {
                statuses.add(f.get());
            }
            assertThat(statuses).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(201, 201, 422);
            assertThat(countGoals(u, "DEADLINE")).isEqualTo(2);
        } finally {
            pool.shutdownNow();
        }
    }

    @Test
    void twentyOpenGoalsIsTheLimit() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
            seedGoal(u, "Seeded " + i, 100_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE", "2026-10-20");
        }
        postGoal(u, deadlineJson("One more", 100_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE"))
            .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("You can have up to 20 goals at once. Finish or delete one first."));
    }

    @Test
    void goalsNeedSetupFirst() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = signIn();
        postGoal(u, deadlineJson("Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE"))
            .andExpect(status().isConflict())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("SETUP_INCOMPLETE"));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isConflict());
    }

    private ResultActions postGoal(TestUser u, String json) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content(json));
    }

    private ResultActions check(TestUser u, long target, String deadline) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/check").with(auth(u)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .content("{\"targetPaise\":" + target + ",\"deadline\":\"" + deadline + "\"}"));
    }

    private static String deadlineJson(String name, long target, String deadline, String mode) {
        return "{\"type\":\"DEADLINE\",\"name\":\"" + name + "\",\"targetPaise\":" + target
            + ",\"deadline\":\"" + deadline + "\",\"mode\":\"" + mode + "\"}";
    }

    private long countGoals(TestUser u, String type) {
        return jdbc.sql("select count(*) from goals where user_id = ? and type = ? and status <> 'DELETED'")
            .params(u.id(), type).query(Long.class).single();
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/ContributionKind.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

/**
 * PLANNED: set aside on a payday. EXTRA: leftover at cycle close or a tighten plan. MANUAL: reserved for money
 * added by hand. CONFIRMED_TRANSFER: a statement line proving a transfer (evidence, not added to saved).
 * SHORTFALL: negative, savings handed back to the pool.
 */
public enum ContributionKind { PLANNED, EXTRA, MANUAL, CONFIRMED_TRANSFER, SHORTFALL }
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalEntity.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.EnumType;
import jakarta.persistence.Enumerated;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.PostLoad;
import jakarta.persistence.PostPersist;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import jakarta.persistence.Transient;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.hibernate.annotations.UpdateTimestamp;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Persistable;

@Entity
@Table(name = "goals")
class GoalEntity implements Persistable<UUID> {
    @Id
    private UUID id;

    @Column(name = "user_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID userId;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 40)
    private String name;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 10)
    private GoalType type;

    @Column(name = "target_paise")
    private Long targetPaise;

    /** The last day of the deadline month. */
    @Column(name = "deadline")
    private LocalDate deadline;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 10)
    private GoalMode mode;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 10)
    private GoalStatus status;

    @Column(name = "created_cycle_id")
    private UUID createdCycleId;

    @Column(name = "created_on", nullable = false)
    private LocalDate createdOn;

    @Column(name = "completed_at")
    private Instant completedAt;

    @Column(name = "created_at", insertable = false, updatable = false)
    private Instant createdAt;

    @UpdateTimestamp
    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private Instant updatedAt;

    @Transient
    private boolean isNew;

    protected GoalEntity() {}

    static GoalEntity deadlineGoal(UUID userId, String name, long targetPaise, YearMonth deadline, GoalMode mode,
                                   UUID createdCycleId, LocalDate createdOn) {
        GoalEntity g = new GoalEntity();
        g.id = UUID.randomUUID();
        g.isNew = true;
        g.userId = userId;
        g.name = name;
        g.type = GoalType.DEADLINE;
        g.targetPaise = targetPaise;
        g.deadline = deadline.atEndOfMonth();
        g.mode = mode;
        g.status = GoalStatus.ACTIVE;
        g.createdCycleId = createdCycleId;
        g.createdOn = createdOn;
        return g;
    }

    String name() { return name; }
    GoalType type() { return type; }
    Long targetPaise() { return targetPaise; }
    YearMonth deadlineMonth() { return deadline == null ? null : YearMonth.from(deadline); }
    GoalMode mode() { return mode; }
    GoalStatus status() { return status; }
    LocalDate createdOn() { return createdOn; }
    Instant createdAt() { return createdAt; }

    void rename(String newName) { this.name = newName; }
    void retarget(long newTargetPaise) { this.targetPaise = newTargetPaise; }
    void moveDeadline(YearMonth month) { this.deadline = month.atEndOfMonth(); }
    void changeMode(GoalMode newMode) { this.mode = newMode; }

    /** Test data for E2E-06 only (local profile): pretend the goal was made earlier. */
    void backdate(LocalDate day) { this.createdOn = day; }

    void complete(Instant at) {
        if (status == GoalStatus.ACTIVE) {
            status = GoalStatus.DONE;
            completedAt = at;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public UUID getId() { return id; }

    @Override
    public boolean isNew() { return isNew; }

    @PostLoad
    @PostPersist
    void markNotNew() { this.isNew = false; }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalRepository.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Modifying;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

interface GoalRepository extends JpaRepository<GoalEntity, UUID> {

    Optional<GoalEntity> findByIdAndUserIdAndStatusNot(UUID id, UUID userId, GoalStatus status);

    List<GoalEntity> findByUserIdAndStatusNotOrderByCreatedAtAsc(UUID userId, GoalStatus status);

    Optional<GoalEntity> findByUserIdAndTypeAndStatusNot(UUID userId, GoalType type, GoalStatus status);

    /** Two first requests racing (setup and Home) make one monthly goal: the partial unique index wins. */
    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query(value = """
            insert into goals (id, user_id, name, type, mode, status, created_on)
            values (:id, :userId, 'Monthly savings', 'MONTHLY', :mode, 'ACTIVE', :createdOn)
            on conflict do nothing
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    int insertMonthlyIfAbsent(@Param("id") UUID id, @Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("mode") String mode,
                              @Param("createdOn") LocalDate createdOn);

    /** Moves a goal between two statuses only if it is still in the first: a racing request changes nothing. */
    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true, clearAutomatically = true)
    @Query(value = """
            update goals set status = :to, updated_at = now()
            where id = :id and user_id = :userId and type = 'DEADLINE' and status = :from
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    int moveStatus(@Param("id") UUID id, @Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("from") String from, @Param("to") String to);

    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true, clearAutomatically = true)
    @Query(value = """
            update goals set status = 'DELETED', updated_at = now()
            where id = :id and user_id = :userId and type = 'DEADLINE' and status <> 'DELETED'
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    int markDeleted(@Param("id") UUID id, @Param("userId") UUID userId);
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.EnumType;
import jakarta.persistence.Enumerated;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.PostLoad;
import jakarta.persistence.PostPersist;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import jakarta.persistence.Transient;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Persistable;

@Entity
@Table(name = "goal_contributions")
class GoalContributionEntity implements Persistable<UUID> {
    @Id
    private UUID id;

    @Column(name = "goal_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID goalId;

    @Column(name = "user_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID userId;

    @Column(name = "cycle_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID cycleId;

    @Column(name = "amount_paise", nullable = false)
    private long amountPaise;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private ContributionKind kind;

    @Column(name = "catch_up_id")
    private UUID catchUpId;

    @Column(name = "transaction_id")
    private UUID transactionId;

    @Column(name = "occurred_on")
    private LocalDate occurredOn;

    @Column(name = "created_at", insertable = false, updatable = false)
    private Instant createdAt;

    @Transient
    private boolean isNew;

    protected GoalContributionEntity() {}

    /** An EXTRA or SHORTFALL row; {@code catchUpId} ties it to the plan that made it, or null for a cycle close. */
    static GoalContributionEntity of(UUID goalId, UUID userId, UUID cycleId, long amountPaise, ContributionKind kind, UUID catchUpId) {
        GoalContributionEntity c = new GoalContributionEntity();
        c.id = UUID.randomUUID();
        c.isNew = true;
        c.goalId = goalId;
        c.userId = userId;
        c.cycleId = cycleId;
        c.amountPaise = amountPaise;
        c.kind = kind;
        c.catchUpId = catchUpId;
        return c;
    }

    UUID goalId() { return goalId; }
    UUID cycleId() { return cycleId; }
    long amountPaise() { return amountPaise; }
    ContributionKind kind() { return kind; }
    LocalDate occurredOn() { return occurredOn; }

    @Override
    public UUID getId() { return id; }

    @Override
    public boolean isNew() { return isNew; }

    @PostLoad
    @PostPersist
    void markNotNew() { this.isNew = false; }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalContributionRepository.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Modifying;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

interface GoalContributionRepository extends JpaRepository<GoalContributionEntity, UUID> {

    interface SavedRow {
        UUID getGoalId();
        Long getSaved();
    }

    @Query("""
            select c.goalId as goalId, sum(c.amountPaise) as saved from GoalContributionEntity c
            where c.userId = :userId and c.kind <> :excluded group by c.goalId
            """)
    List<SavedRow> savedByGoal(@Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("excluded") ContributionKind excluded);

    @Query("""
            select coalesce(sum(c.amountPaise), 0) from GoalContributionEntity c
            where c.userId = :userId and c.goalId = :goalId and c.kind <> :excluded
            """)
    long savedFor(@Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("goalId") UUID goalId, @Param("excluded") ContributionKind excluded);

    List<GoalContributionEntity> findByUserIdAndGoalIdOrderByCreatedAtAsc(UUID userId, UUID goalId);

    List<GoalContributionEntity> findByUserIdAndCycleId(UUID userId, UUID cycleId);

    List<GoalContributionEntity> findByUserIdAndCycleIdAndKind(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, ContributionKind kind);

    /** One planned contribution per goal per cycle, whoever opens the cycle first. */
    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query(value = """
            insert into goal_contributions (id, goal_id, user_id, cycle_id, amount_paise, kind)
            values (:id, :goalId, :userId, :cycleId, :amount, 'PLANNED')
            on conflict do nothing
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    int insertPlannedIfAbsent(@Param("id") UUID id, @Param("goalId") UUID goalId, @Param("userId") UUID userId,
                              @Param("cycleId") UUID cycleId, @Param("amount") long amount);

    /** One confirmed-transfer row per statement transaction. */
    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query(value = """
            insert into goal_contributions (id, goal_id, user_id, cycle_id, amount_paise, kind, transaction_id, occurred_on)
            values (:id, :goalId, :userId, :cycleId, :amount, 'CONFIRMED_TRANSFER', :transactionId, :occurredOn)
            on conflict do nothing
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    int insertTransferIfAbsent(@Param("id") UUID id, @Param("goalId") UUID goalId, @Param("userId") UUID userId,
                               @Param("cycleId") UUID cycleId, @Param("amount") long amount,
                               @Param("transactionId") UUID transactionId, @Param("occurredOn") LocalDate occurredOn);

    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query("delete from GoalContributionEntity c where c.userId = :userId and c.catchUpId = :catchUpId")
    int deleteForCatchUp(@Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("catchUpId") UUID catchUpId);

    /** The rows a cycle close wrote (no catch-up attached), so a later leftover choice can replace them. */
    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query("""
            delete from GoalContributionEntity c
            where c.userId = :userId and c.cycleId = :cycleId and c.catchUpId is null and c.kind in :kinds
            """)
    int deleteCloseEffects(@Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("cycleId") UUID cycleId,
                           @Param("kinds") Collection<ContributionKind> kinds);
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import java.time.LocalDate;

/**
 * GET /goals. {@code split} is what each goal got on this cycle's payday; {@code freeThisCyclePaise} is the rest of
 * this cycle's savings; {@code committedPaise} is what active goals need each month from now; {@code freeNextPaise}
 * is savings minus that, the figure the reality check uses.
 */
public record GoalsResponse(Monthly monthly, List<GoalView> goals) {
    public record Monthly(UUID id, GoalMode mode, long savingsPaise, LocalDate cycleStart, List<Split> split,
                          long freeThisCyclePaise, long committedPaise, long freeNextPaise) {}

    public record Split(UUID goalId, String name, long amountPaise) {}
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalBook.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.common.error.NotFoundException;
import in.gullak.common.time.Ist;
import in.gullak.profile.PaydayRule;
import in.gullak.profile.Profile;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/** A user's goals with what each has saved, read once per request. Package-private: entities stay in goals. */
@Component
class GoalBook {
    record Entry(GoalEntity goal, long savedPaise) {}

    private final GoalRepository goals;
    private final GoalContributionRepository contributions;
    private final Clock clock;

    GoalBook(GoalRepository goals, GoalContributionRepository contributions, Clock clock) {
        this.goals = goals;
        this.contributions = contributions;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    /** Every goal that is not deleted (monthly savings included), oldest first. Confirmed transfers never count as saved. */
    List<Entry> live(UUID userId) {
        Map<UUID, Long> saved = contributions.savedByGoal(userId, ContributionKind.CONFIRMED_TRANSFER).stream()
            .collect(Collectors.toMap(GoalContributionRepository.SavedRow::getGoalId, GoalContributionRepository.SavedRow::getSaved));
        return goals.findByUserIdAndStatusNotOrderByCreatedAtAsc(userId, GoalStatus.DELETED).stream()
            .map(g -> new Entry(g, saved.getOrDefault(g.getId(), 0L)))
            .toList();
    }

    Entry entry(UUID userId, UUID goalId) {
        GoalEntity g = goals.findByIdAndUserIdAndStatusNot(goalId, userId, GoalStatus.DELETED)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Goal"));
        return new Entry(g, contributions.savedFor(userId, goalId, ContributionKind.CONFIRMED_TRANSFER));
    }

    /** The user's monthly savings goal, created Flexible on first use. */
    GoalEntity monthly(UUID userId) {
        Optional<GoalEntity> found = goals.findByUserIdAndTypeAndStatusNot(userId, GoalType.MONTHLY, GoalStatus.DELETED);
        if (found.isPresent()) {
            return found.get();
        }
        goals.insertMonthlyIfAbsent(UUID.randomUUID(), userId, GoalMode.FLEXIBLE.name(), Ist.today(clock));
        return goals.findByUserIdAndTypeAndStatusNot(userId, GoalType.MONTHLY, GoalStatus.DELETED)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("monthly goal missing after insert"));
    }

    GoalProgress progress(Entry e, LocalDate today, PaydayRule rule, Integer day) {
        GoalEntity g = e.goal();
        return GoalProgress.of(g.targetPaise(), e.savedPaise(), g.createdOn(), g.deadlineMonth(),
            g.status() == GoalStatus.ACTIVE, today, rule, day);
    }

    GoalProgress progress(Entry e, LocalDate today, Profile profile) {
        return progress(e, today, profile.paydayRule(), profile.paydayDay());
    }

    /** What active deadline goals need each month from now on, leaving {@code excludeGoalId} out (null leaves none out). */
    long committedPaise(List<Entry> entries, UUID excludeGoalId, LocalDate today, PaydayRule rule, Integer day) {
        return entries.stream()
            .filter(e -> e.goal().type() == GoalType.DEADLINE && e.goal().status() == GoalStatus.ACTIVE)
            .filter(e -> !e.goal().getId().equals(excludeGoalId))
            .mapToLong(e -> progress(e, today, rule, day).contributionPaise())
            .sum();
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalViews.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

/** Builds {@link GoalView}s. One place, so every endpoint shows a goal the same way. */
final class GoalViews {
    private GoalViews() {}

    static GoalView deadline(GoalBook.Entry e, GoalProgress p) {
        GoalEntity g = e.goal();
        return new GoalView(g.getId(), g.name(), g.type(), g.targetPaise(), g.deadlineMonth(), g.mode(), g.status(),
            e.savedPaise(), p.expectedPaise(), p.behindPaise(), p.percent(), p.expectedPercent(), p.contributionPaise(),
            p.paydaysTotal(), p.paydaysElapsed(), p.paydaysLeft());
    }

    /** Monthly savings: the target is the profile's savings; progress fields are zero. */
    static GoalView monthly(GoalEntity g, long savingsPaise) {
        return new GoalView(g.getId(), g.name(), g.type(), savingsPaise, null, g.mode(), g.status(),
            savingsPaise, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalService.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.BudgetService;
import in.gullak.budget.CurrentCycle;
import in.gullak.budget.CycleRecord;
import in.gullak.budget.CycleService;
import in.gullak.common.error.ErrorCode;
import in.gullak.common.error.GullakException;
import in.gullak.common.error.NotFoundException;
import in.gullak.common.error.UnprocessableException;
import in.gullak.common.error.ValidationException;
import in.gullak.common.log.Events;
import in.gullak.common.money.RupeeFormat;
import in.gullak.common.time.Ist;
import in.gullak.profile.Profile;
import in.gullak.profile.ProfileService;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.Function;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
public class GoalService {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GoalService.class);
    static final int MAX_GOALS = 20;
    static final int MAX_MONTHS_AHEAD = 120;
    private static final DateTimeFormatter MONTH = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("MMMM yyyy", Locale.ENGLISH);

    private final GoalRepository goals;
    private final GoalContributionRepository contributions;
    private final GoalBook book;
    private final ProfileService profiles;
    private final BudgetService budget;
    private final CycleService cycles;
    private final Clock clock;

    GoalService(GoalRepository goals, GoalContributionRepository contributions, GoalBook book, ProfileService profiles,
                BudgetService budget, CycleService cycles, Clock clock) {
        this.goals = goals;
        this.contributions = contributions;
        this.book = book;
        this.profiles = profiles;
        this.budget = budget;
        this.cycles = cycles;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public record CreateOutcome(GoalCreated body, boolean created) {}

    /** Creates a deadline goal, or creates or updates monthly savings. Takes the profile lock so parallel creates queue. */
    @Transactional
    public CreateOutcome create(UUID userId, GoalRequest r) {
        Profile profile = lockedProfile(userId);
        LocalDate today = Ist.today(clock);
        if (r.type() == GoalType.MONTHLY) {
            boolean existed = goals.findByUserIdAndTypeAndStatusNot(userId, GoalType.MONTHLY, GoalStatus.DELETED).isPresent();
            GoalEntity monthly = book.monthly(userId);
            monthly.changeMode(r.mode());
            goals.save(monthly);
            log.info("{} user={} goal={} type=monthly", existed ? Events.GOAL_UPDATED : Events.GOAL_CREATED, userId, monthly.getId());
            return new CreateOutcome(new GoalCreated(GoalViews.monthly(monthly, profile.savingsPaise()), null), !existed);
        }
        String name = requireName(r.name());
        if (r.targetPaise() == null) {
            throw new ValidationException("targetPaise", "Set a target");
        }
        if (r.deadline() == null) {
            throw new ValidationException("deadline", "Pick a month");
        }
        requireWithinTenYears(r.deadline(), today);
        CurrentCycle current = budget.currentCycle(userId);
        List<GoalBook.Entry> entries = book.live(userId);
        long open = entries.stream()
            .filter(e -> e.goal().type() == GoalType.DEADLINE && e.goal().status() != GoalStatus.DONE)
            .count();
        if (open >= MAX_GOALS) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE, "You can have up to 20 goals at once. Finish or delete one first.");
        }
        CheckResponse check = checkFor(r.targetPaise(), 0, r.deadline(), null, entries, today, profile);
        requirePossible(check);
        GoalEntity goal = goals.save(GoalEntity.deadlineGoal(userId, name, r.targetPaise(), r.deadline(), r.mode(),
            current.id(), today));
        log.info("{} user={} goal={} type=deadline", Events.GOAL_CREATED, userId, goal.getId());
        GoalBook.Entry entry = new GoalBook.Entry(goal, 0);
        return new CreateOutcome(new GoalCreated(GoalViews.deadline(entry, book.progress(entry, today, profile)), check), true);
    }

    /** The reality check without saving anything (API-GL-09). */
    @Transactional
    public CheckResponse check(UUID userId, CheckRequest r) {
        Profile profile = profiles.get(userId);
        LocalDate today = Ist.today(clock);
        requireWithinTenYears(r.deadline(), today);
        budget.currentCycle(userId);
        List<GoalBook.Entry> entries = book.live(userId);
        long saved = r.goalId() == null ? 0 : deadlineEntry(entries, r.goalId()).savedPaise();
        return checkFor(r.targetPaise(), saved, r.deadline(), r.goalId(), entries, today, profile);
    }

    @Transactional
    public GoalsResponse list(UUID userId) {
        Profile profile = profiles.get(userId);
        CurrentCycle current = budget.currentCycle(userId);
        LocalDate today = Ist.today(clock);
        GoalEntity monthly = book.monthly(userId);
        List<GoalBook.Entry> deadline = book.live(userId).stream().filter(e -> e.goal().type() == GoalType.DEADLINE).toList();
        List<GoalView> views = deadline.stream()
            .sorted(Comparator.comparing((GoalBook.Entry e) -> e.goal().status()))
            .map(e -> GoalViews.deadline(e, book.progress(e, today, profile)))
            .toList();
        long committed = views.stream().filter(v -> v.status() == GoalStatus.ACTIVE).mapToLong(GoalView::contributionPaise).sum();
        List<GoalContributionEntity> planned = current.id() == null ? List.of()
            : contributions.findByUserIdAndCycleIdAndKind(userId, current.id(), ContributionKind.PLANNED);
        Map<UUID, String> names = deadline.stream().collect(Collectors.toMap(e -> e.goal().getId(), e -> e.goal().name()));
        List<GoalsResponse.Split> split = planned.stream()
            .filter(c -> names.containsKey(c.goalId()))
            .map(c -> new GoalsResponse.Split(c.goalId(), names.get(c.goalId()), c.amountPaise()))
            .toList();
        long plannedTotal = planned.stream().mapToLong(GoalContributionEntity::amountPaise).sum();
        long savings = profile.savingsPaise();
        GoalsResponse.Monthly summary = new GoalsResponse.Monthly(monthly.getId(), monthly.mode(), savings,
            current.cycle().start(), split, Math.max(0, savings - plannedTotal), committed, savings - committed);
        return new GoalsResponse(summary, views);
    }

    @Transactional
    public GoalDetail get(UUID userId, UUID goalId) {
        Profile profile = profiles.get(userId);
        budget.currentCycle(userId);
        LocalDate today = Ist.today(clock);
        GoalBook.Entry e = book.entry(userId, goalId);
        List<GoalContributionEntity> rows = contributions.findByUserIdAndGoalIdOrderByCreatedAtAsc(userId, goalId);
        return new GoalDetail(view(e, today, profile), byCycle(userId, rows), transfers(rows));
    }

    GoalView view(GoalBook.Entry e, LocalDate today, Profile profile) {
        return e.goal().type() == GoalType.MONTHLY
            ? GoalViews.monthly(e.goal(), profile.savingsPaise())
            : GoalViews.deadline(e, book.progress(e, today, profile));
    }

    private List<GoalDetail.CycleContribution> byCycle(UUID userId, List<GoalContributionEntity> rows) {
        Map<UUID, List<GoalContributionEntity>> grouped = rows.stream()
            .filter(c -> c.kind() != ContributionKind.CONFIRMED_TRANSFER)
            .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(GoalContributionEntity::cycleId, LinkedHashMap::new, Collectors.toList()));
        Map<UUID, CycleRecord> dates = cycles.findAll(userId, grouped.keySet()).stream()
            .collect(Collectors.toMap(CycleRecord::id, Function.identity()));
        return grouped.entrySet().stream()
            .filter(x -> dates.containsKey(x.getKey()))
            .map(x -> {
                CycleRecord c = dates.get(x.getKey());
                long planned = sum(x.getValue(), ContributionKind.PLANNED);
                long extra = sum(x.getValue(), ContributionKind.EXTRA) + sum(x.getValue(), ContributionKind.MANUAL);
                long shortfall = sum(x.getValue(), ContributionKind.SHORTFALL);
                return new GoalDetail.CycleContribution(c.id(), c.cycle().start(), c.cycle().end(), planned, extra,
                    shortfall, planned + extra + shortfall);
            })
            .sorted(Comparator.comparing(GoalDetail.CycleContribution::cycleStart, Comparator.reverseOrder()))
            .toList();
    }

    private static List<GoalDetail.Transfer> transfers(List<GoalContributionEntity> rows) {
        return rows.stream()
            .filter(c -> c.kind() == ContributionKind.CONFIRMED_TRANSFER)
            .map(c -> new GoalDetail.Transfer(c.occurredOn(), c.amountPaise()))
            .sorted(Comparator.comparing(GoalDetail.Transfer::date, Comparator.nullsLast(Comparator.reverseOrder())))
            .toList();
    }

    private static long sum(List<GoalContributionEntity> rows, ContributionKind kind) {
        return rows.stream().filter(c -> c.kind() == kind).mapToLong(GoalContributionEntity::amountPaise).sum();
    }

    Profile lockedProfile(UUID userId) {
        return profiles.lockForGuard(userId)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new GullakException(ErrorCode.SETUP_INCOMPLETE, HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Finish setup first"));
    }

    GoalBook.Entry deadlineEntry(List<GoalBook.Entry> entries, UUID goalId) {
        return entries.stream()
            .filter(e -> e.goal().getId().equals(goalId) && e.goal().type() == GoalType.DEADLINE)
            .findFirst()
            .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Goal"));
    }

    CheckResponse checkFor(long targetPaise, long savedPaise, YearMonth deadline, UUID excludeGoalId,
                           List<GoalBook.Entry> entries, LocalDate today, Profile profile) {
        List<LocalDate> paydays = PaydayCalendar.between(today, deadline, profile.paydayRule(), profile.paydayDay());
        long free = profile.savingsPaise() - book.committedPaise(entries, excludeGoalId, today, profile.paydayRule(), profile.paydayDay());
        RealityCheck.Result c = RealityCheck.check(targetPaise, savedPaise, paydays.size(), free);
        LocalDate next = PaydayCalendar.next(today, profile.paydayRule(), profile.paydayDay());
        return new CheckResponse(c.verdict(), c.reason(), c.contributionPaise(), c.freePaise(), c.freeAfterPaise(),
            c.maxTargetPaise(), paydays.size(), paydays.isEmpty() ? null : paydays.getFirst(),
            paydays.isEmpty() ? null : paydays.getLast(), next);
    }

    void requirePossible(CheckResponse c) {
        if (c.reason() == VerdictReason.DEADLINE_PASSED) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.DEADLINE_PASSED,
                "That month has passed. Pick " + YearMonth.from(c.nextPayday()).format(MONTH) + " or later.");
        }
        if (c.verdict() == Verdict.NOT_POSSIBLE) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE, RupeeFormat.format(c.contributionPaise())
                + " a month is more than your free " + RupeeFormat.format(Math.max(0, c.freePaise())) + ". Aim for "
                + RupeeFormat.format(c.maxTargetPaise()) + " or less, or pick a later month.");
        }
    }

    static void requireWithinTenYears(YearMonth deadline, LocalDate today) {
        if (deadline.isAfter(YearMonth.from(today).plusMonths(MAX_MONTHS_AHEAD))) {
            throw new ValidationException("deadline", "Pick a month within 10 years");
        }
    }

    private static String requireName(String raw) {
        String name = raw == null ? "" : raw.trim();
        if (name.isEmpty()) {
            throw new ValidationException("name", "Name your goal");
        }
        return name;
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalController.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.common.web.CurrentUser;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.core.annotation.AuthenticationPrincipal;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/goals")
class GoalController {
    private final GoalService goals;

    GoalController(GoalService goals) {
        this.goals = goals;
    }

    @GetMapping
    GoalsResponse list(@AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt) {
        return goals.list(CurrentUser.id(jwt));
    }

    @PostMapping
    ResponseEntity<GoalCreated> create(@AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt, @Valid @RequestBody GoalRequest body) {
        GoalService.CreateOutcome out = goals.create(CurrentUser.id(jwt), body);
        return ResponseEntity.status(out.created() ? HttpStatus.CREATED : HttpStatus.OK).body(out.body());
    }

    @PostMapping("/check")
    CheckResponse check(@AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt, @Valid @RequestBody CheckRequest body) {
        return goals.check(CurrentUser.id(jwt), body);
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    GoalDetail get(@AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt, @PathVariable UUID id) {
        return goals.get(CurrentUser.id(jwt), id);
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.delete;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.patch;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

class GoalEditApiTest extends ApiTest {

    @Test
    void editingTheTargetRecomputesTheContribution() throws Exception { // API-GL-12
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        patchGoal(u, goa, "{\"targetPaise\":3600000}")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.targetPaise").value(3_600_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.contributionPaise").value(720_000));
        patchGoal(u, goa, "{\"name\":\"  Goa with friends  \",\"deadline\":\"2027-08\"}")
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.name").value("Goa with friends"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.deadline").value("2027-08"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.contributionPaise").value(360_000));
    }

    @Test
    void anImpossibleEditIsRefused() throws Exception { // API-GL-13
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        patchGoal(u, goa, "{\"targetPaise\":12000000}") // ₹1,20,000 over 5 paydays is ₹24,000 a month
            .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_POSSIBLE"));
        patchGoal(u, goa, "{\"deadline\":\"2026-09\"}")
            .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("DEADLINE_PASSED"));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.targetPaise").value(3_000_000));
    }

    @Test
    void aTargetAtOrBelowWhatIsSavedIsRefused() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        seedContribution(u, UUID.fromString(goa), cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01"), 1_000_000);
        patchGoal(u, goa, "{\"targetPaise\":1000000}")
            .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("You've saved ₹10,000 already. Pick a target above that."));
    }

    @Test
    void theModeCanChange() throws Exception { // API-GL-14
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        patchGoal(u, goa, "{\"mode\":\"STRICT\"}").andExpect(status().isOk()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.mode").value("STRICT"));
        patchGoal(u, goa, "{\"mode\":\"medium\"}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        patchGoal(u, goa, "{\"name\":\"   \"}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
    }

    @Test
    void monthlySavingsOnlyChangesItsMode() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String monthly = monthlyId(u);
        patchGoal(u, monthly, "{\"mode\":\"STRICT\"}").andExpect(status().isOk()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.mode").value("STRICT"));
        patchGoal(u, monthly, "{\"targetPaise\":100000}").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + monthly + "/pause").with(auth(u)))

        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/goals/" + monthly).with(auth(u)))

    @Test

        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        freeNext(u, 1_400_000);
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/pause").with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("PAUSED"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.contributionPaise").value(0));
        freeNext(u, 2_000_000);

    @Test
    void pausingTwiceIsAConflict() throws Exception { // API-GL-16
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/pause").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/pause").with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(status().isConflict())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("ALREADY_PAUSED"));
    }

    @Test
    void resumingRecomputesOverTheRemainingPaydays() throws Exception { // API-GL-17, GOAL-17
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/pause").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-11-02T04:00:00Z")); // the 1 Nov payday passed while paused
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/resume").with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.status").value("ACTIVE"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.paydaysLeft").value(4))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.contributionPaise").value(750_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.behindPaise").value(600_000));
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/resume").with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(status().isConflict())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_PAUSED"));
    }

    @Test
    void resumingAfterTheDeadlineIsRefused() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 300_000, "2026-11", "FLEXIBLE");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/pause").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-11-02T04:00:00Z"));
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/resume").with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("DEADLINE_PASSED"));
    }

    @Test
    void deletingKeepsTheHistoryAndFreesTheMoney() throws Exception { // API-GL-25
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        seedContribution(u, UUID.fromString(goa), cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01"), 300_000);
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select status from goals where id = ?").param(UUID.fromString(goa)).query(String.class).single())
            .isEqualTo("DELETED");
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from goal_contributions where goal_id = ?").param(UUID.fromString(goa))
            .query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(1L);
        freeNext(u, 2_000_000);
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals").isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void deletingTwiceIsNotFound() throws Exception { // API-GL-26
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        patchGoal(u, goa, "{\"mode\":\"STRICT\"}").andExpect(status().isNotFound());
    }

    @Test
    void aDoneGoalCannotChange() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        jdbc.sql("update goals set status = 'DONE' where id = ?").param(UUID.fromString(goa)).update();
        patchGoal(u, goa, "{\"targetPaise\":3600000}").andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("GOAL_DONE"));
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/pause").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("GOAL_DONE"));
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isNoContent());
    }

    @Test
    void anotherUsersGoalCannotBeChanged() throws Exception {
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(a, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        patchGoal(b, goa, "{\"name\":\"pwned\"}").andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/pause").with(auth(b))).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/resume").with(auth(b))).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(b))).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(a))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.name").value("Goa trip"));
    }

    @Test
    void savingsBelowGoalCommitmentsIsRefused() throws Exception { // Review focus, API-GL-30
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        putProfile(u, EXAMPLE_PROFILE.replace("2000000", "500000"))
            .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_POSSIBLE"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Your goals need ₹6,000 a month. Pause or change a goal first."));
        putProfile(u, EXAMPLE_PROFILE.replace("2000000", "600000")).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/pause").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        putProfile(u, EXAMPLE_PROFILE.replace("2000000", "0")).andExpect(status().isOk());
    }

    private ResultActions patchGoal(TestUser u, String id, String json) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(patch("/api/v1/goals/" + id).with(auth(u)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content(json));
    }

    private void freeNext(TestUser u, long paise) throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.monthly.freeNextPaise").value(paise));
    }

    private String monthlyId(TestUser u) throws Exception {
        return JsonPath.read(mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u))).andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString(), "$.monthly.id");
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/profile/SavingsFloor.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.profile;

import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * How much monthly savings another feature already depends on (goals' contributions). Profile asks through this
 * interface so it never imports goals.
 */
public interface SavingsFloor {
    long minimumSavingsPaise(UUID userId, PaydayRule rule, Integer paydayDay);
}
```

In `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/profile/ProfileService.java` replace the fields, constructor and `put` (imports `org.springframework.beans.factory.ObjectProvider`):

```java
    private final ProfileRepository profiles;
    private final FixedCostRepository fixedCosts;
    /** Lazy: the goals implementation reads goal tables only, but a constructor list would still tie the beans in a cycle. */
    private final ObjectProvider<SavingsFloor> floors;

    ProfileService(ProfileRepository profiles, FixedCostRepository fixedCosts, ObjectProvider<SavingsFloor> floors) {
        this.profiles = profiles;
        this.fixedCosts = fixedCosts;
        this.floors = floors;
    }

    @Transactional
    public Profile put(UUID userId, ProfileRequest r) {
        Integer day = switch (r.paydayRule()) {
            case FIXED_DAY -> {
                if (r.paydayDay() == null) {
                    throw new ValidationException("paydayDay", "Pick the day your salary arrives");
                }
                yield r.paydayDay();
            }
            case LAST_WORKING_DAY -> null;
        };
        Profile p = new Profile(userId, r.incomePaise(), r.savingsPaise(), r.paydayRule(), day,
            r.personality(), r.morningTime(), r.nightTime(), r.timezone());
        // Create the row if it is missing, then lock it: the guard runs under the same lock fixed-cost and goal
        // writes take, so they never interleave. A rejected first-time profile rolls the insert back with the transaction.
        profiles.insertIfAbsent(userId, p.incomePaise(), p.savingsPaise(), p.paydayRule().name(), p.paydayDay(),
            p.personality().name(), p.morningTime(), p.nightTime(), p.timezone());
        ProfileEntity entity = profiles.findByIdForUpdate(userId)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("profile row missing after insert"));
        requireSomethingToSpend(p.incomePaise(), fixedCosts.totalActive(userId), p.savingsPaise(), SAVINGS_MESSAGE);
        long floor = floors.orderedStream()                                                   // Plan 5
            .mapToLong(f -> f.minimumSavingsPaise(userId, p.paydayRule(), p.paydayDay()))
            .max().orElse(0);
        if (p.savingsPaise() < floor) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE,
                "Your goals need " + RupeeFormat.format(floor) + " a month. Pause or change a goal first.");
        }
        entity.apply(p);
        profiles.save(entity);
        log.info("{} user={}", Events.PROFILE_UPDATED, userId);
        return p;
    }
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalSavingsFloor.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.common.time.Ist;
import in.gullak.profile.PaydayRule;
import in.gullak.profile.SavingsFloor;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/** GOAL-20 from the other side: savings may not drop below what active goals need each month. */
@Component
class GoalSavingsFloor implements SavingsFloor {
    private final GoalBook book;
    private final Clock clock;

    GoalSavingsFloor(GoalBook book, Clock clock) {
        this.book = book;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Override
    public long minimumSavingsPaise(UUID userId, PaydayRule rule, Integer paydayDay) {
        return book.committedPaise(book.live(userId), null, Ist.today(clock), rule, paydayDay);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalPatch.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.common.money.Money;
import in.gullak.common.web.SingleLineText;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Max;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Positive;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;
import java.time.YearMonth;

/** Every field optional; a null field keeps its current value. Monthly savings accepts {@code mode} only. */
public record GoalPatch(
    @Size(min = 1, max = 40) @SingleLineText @Pattern(regexp = ".*\\S.*", message = "must not be blank") String name,
    @Positive @Max(Money.MAX_AMOUNT_PAISE) Long targetPaise,
    YearMonth deadline,
    GoalMode mode) {}
```

Add to `GoalService` (imports `in.gullak.common.error.ConflictException`):

```java
    @Transactional
    public GoalView update(UUID userId, UUID goalId, GoalPatch p) {
        Profile profile = lockedProfile(userId);
        LocalDate today = Ist.today(clock);
        budget.currentCycle(userId);
        List<GoalBook.Entry> entries = book.live(userId);
        GoalBook.Entry e = entries.stream().filter(x -> x.goal().getId().equals(goalId)).findFirst()
            .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Goal"));
        GoalEntity g = e.goal();
        if (g.type() == GoalType.MONTHLY) {
            if (p.name() != null || p.targetPaise() != null || p.deadline() != null) {
                throw new ValidationException("mode", "Only the mode of monthly savings changes here. Savings are set in your profile.");
            }
            if (p.mode() != null) {
                g.changeMode(p.mode());
                goals.save(g);
            }
            log.info("{} user={} goal={}", Events.GOAL_UPDATED, userId, goalId);
            return GoalViews.monthly(g, profile.savingsPaise());
        }
        if (g.status() == GoalStatus.DONE) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.GOAL_DONE, "This goal is done, so it can't be changed");
        }
        long target = p.targetPaise() != null ? p.targetPaise() : g.targetPaise();
        YearMonth deadline = p.deadline() != null ? p.deadline() : g.deadlineMonth();
        if (p.targetPaise() != null || p.deadline() != null) {
            requireWithinTenYears(deadline, today);
            if (target <= e.savedPaise()) {
                throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE,
                    "You've saved " + RupeeFormat.format(e.savedPaise()) + " already. Pick a target above that.");
            }
            CheckResponse check = checkFor(target, e.savedPaise(), deadline, goalId, entries, today, profile);
            if (g.status() == GoalStatus.ACTIVE || check.reason() == VerdictReason.DEADLINE_PASSED) {
                requirePossible(check);
            }
        }
        if (p.name() != null) {
            g.rename(p.name().trim());
        }
        g.retarget(target);
        g.moveDeadline(deadline);
        if (p.mode() != null) {
            g.changeMode(p.mode());
        }
        goals.save(g);
        log.info("{} user={} goal={}", Events.GOAL_UPDATED, userId, goalId);
        GoalBook.Entry updated = new GoalBook.Entry(g, e.savedPaise());
        return GoalViews.deadline(updated, book.progress(updated, today, profile));
    }

    /** Spec 2.2: a paused goal's contribution drops to ₹0 and the money returns to free savings. */
    @Transactional
    public GoalView pause(UUID userId, UUID goalId) {
        Profile profile = lockedProfile(userId);
        GoalBook.Entry e = book.entry(userId, goalId);
        rejectMonthly(e.goal());
        switch (e.goal().status()) {
            case PAUSED -> throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.ALREADY_PAUSED, "This goal is already paused");
            case DONE -> throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.GOAL_DONE, "This goal is done, so it can't be changed");
            default -> { }
        }
        if (goals.moveStatus(goalId, userId, GoalStatus.ACTIVE.name(), GoalStatus.PAUSED.name()) == 0) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.ALREADY_PAUSED, "This goal is already paused");
        }
        log.info("{} user={} goal={}", Events.GOAL_PAUSED, userId, goalId);
        return view(userId, goalId, profile);
    }

    /** GOAL-17: the contribution is recomputed over the paydays left; the reality check runs again. */
    @Transactional
    public GoalView resume(UUID userId, UUID goalId) {
        Profile profile = lockedProfile(userId);
        LocalDate today = Ist.today(clock);
        budget.currentCycle(userId);
        List<GoalBook.Entry> entries = book.live(userId);
        GoalBook.Entry e = entries.stream().filter(x -> x.goal().getId().equals(goalId)).findFirst()
            .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Goal"));
        rejectMonthly(e.goal());
        switch (e.goal().status()) {
            case ACTIVE -> throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.NOT_PAUSED, "This goal is not paused");
            case DONE -> throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.GOAL_DONE, "This goal is done, so it can't be changed");
            default -> { }
        }
        requirePossible(checkFor(e.goal().targetPaise(), e.savedPaise(), e.goal().deadlineMonth(), goalId, entries, today, profile));
        if (goals.moveStatus(goalId, userId, GoalStatus.PAUSED.name(), GoalStatus.ACTIVE.name()) == 0) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.NOT_PAUSED, "This goal is not paused");
        }
        log.info("{} user={} goal={}", Events.GOAL_RESUMED, userId, goalId);
        return view(userId, goalId, profile);
    }

    /** Spec 2.2: deleting keeps the goal's history (its row and contributions stay, marked deleted). */
    @Transactional
    public void delete(UUID userId, UUID goalId) {
        lockedProfile(userId);
        GoalBook.Entry e = book.entry(userId, goalId);
        rejectMonthly(e.goal());
        if (goals.markDeleted(goalId, userId) == 0) {
            throw new NotFoundException("Goal");
        }
        log.info("{} user={} goal={}", Events.GOAL_DELETED, userId, goalId);
    }

    GoalView view(UUID userId, UUID goalId, Profile profile) {
        return view(book.entry(userId, goalId), Ist.today(clock), profile);
    }

    private static void rejectMonthly(GoalEntity g) {
        if (g.type() == GoalType.MONTHLY) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.MONTHLY_GOAL, "Monthly savings can't be paused or deleted. Set them to ₹0 instead.");
        }
    }
```

- Test: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalLedgerApiTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's `CycleOpenedListener`, `CycleService.covering`; Task 3's `GoalBook`, `GoalContributionRepository.insertPlannedIfAbsent`, `insertTransferIfAbsent`; Plan 3's `XpService.award(UUID userId, XpKind kind, String refType, UUID refId) : int` and `xp_events(user_id, kind, xp, ref_type, ref_id, day, reversed_at)` (unique per user, kind, ref); Plan 4's `SavingsTransferListener#onSavingsTransfer(SavingsTransfer(userId, transactionId, amountPaise, date))`, called through an `ObjectProvider` inside the import commit, `BadgeTrigger#fire(UUID, String, UUID)` and the test beans `RecordingBadgeTrigger` and `RecordingSavingsListener`.

Plan 4's `RecordingSavingsListener` keeps recording alongside it in tests.

`server/src/test/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalLedgerApiTest.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.gamification.BadgeTrigger;
import in.gullak.ingestion.imports.SavingsTransfer;
import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.RecordingBadgeTrigger;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;

class GoalLedgerApiTest extends ApiTest {
    static final Instant NOV_2 = Instant.parse("2026-11-02T04:00:00Z");
    static final Instant DEC_2 = Instant.parse("2026-12-02T04:00:00Z");

    @Autowired GoalSavingsTransfers transfers;
    @Autowired RecordingBadgeTrigger badges;

    @Test
    void plannedContributionsLandWhenTheCycleOpens() throws Exception { // BUD-33
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        clock.set(NOV_2);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals[0].savedPaise").value(600_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals[0].expectedPaise").value(600_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals[0].behindPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals[0].paydaysElapsed").value(1))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.monthly.cycleStart").value("2026-11-01"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.monthly.split[0].name").value("Goa trip"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.monthly.split[0].amountPaise").value(600_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.monthly.freeThisCyclePaise").value(1_400_000));
        assertThat(rows(u, UUID.fromString(goa), "PLANNED")).isEqualTo(1);
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cycles[0].cycleStart").value("2026-11-01"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cycles[0].plannedPaise").value(600_000));
    }

    @Test
    void pausedGoalsGetNothing() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/pause").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        clock.set(NOV_2);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThat(rows(u, UUID.fromString(goa), "PLANNED")).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void milestonesHappenOnceEach() throws Exception { // GOAL-18, API-GL-27
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        seedContribution(u, UUID.fromString(goa), cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01"), 700_000); // 23%
        clock.set(NOV_2); // + ₹4,600 planned → 38%
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThat(percents(u)).containsExactly(25);
        assertThat(xpEvents(u, "GOAL_MILESTONE")).isEqualTo(1);
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.milestones[0].percent").value(25))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.lastMilestone.percent").value(25));
        clock.set(DEC_2); // + ₹4,600 → 54%
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThat(percents(u)).containsExactly(25, 50);
        assertThat(xpEvents(u, "GOAL_MILESTONE")).isEqualTo(2);
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals[0].lastMilestone.percent").value(50));
    }

    @Test
    void reachingTheTargetCompletesTheGoal() throws Exception { // GOAL-19
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID laptop = seedGoal(u, "Laptop", 1_000_000, "2026-11", "FLEXIBLE", "2026-10-20");
        clock.set(NOV_2); // the one payday sets aside all ₹10,000
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select status from goals where id = ?").param(laptop).query(String.class).single()).isEqualTo("DONE");
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select completed_at is not null from goals where id = ?").param(laptop).query(Boolean.class).single()).isTrue();
        assertThat(percents(u)).containsExactly(25, 50, 75, 100);
        assertThat(xpEvents(u, "GOAL_COMPLETED")).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select coalesce(sum(xp), 0) from xp_events where user_id = ? and kind = 'GOAL_COMPLETED'")
            .param(u.id()).query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(1_000L);
        assertThat(xpEvents(u, "GOAL_MILESTONE")).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(badges.triggersFor(u.id())).containsExactly(BadgeTrigger.GOAL_CRUSHER);
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals[0].status").value("DONE"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals[0].contributionPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.monthly.committedPaise").value(0));
    }

    @Test
    void aConfirmedTransferIsShownNotCounted() throws Exception { // MAT-19 server half, API-GL-31
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        clock.set(NOV_2);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk()); // planned ₹6,000
        UUID sip = seedTransfer(u, 598_000, Instant.parse("2026-11-05T05:00:00Z")); // within ₹5 or 2% of ₹6,000
        transfers.onSavingsTransfer(new SavingsTransfer(u.id(), sip, 598_000, LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 5)));
        transfers.onSavingsTransfer(new SavingsTransfer(u.id(), sip, 598_000, LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 5)));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.transfers.length()").value(1))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.transfers[0].date").value("2026-11-05"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.transfers[0].amountPaise").value(598_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.savedPaise").value(600_000));
    }

    @Test
    void aTransferThatMatchesNoGoalGoesToMonthlySavings() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        clock.set(NOV_2);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        UUID rd = seedTransfer(u, 250_000, Instant.parse("2026-11-06T05:00:00Z"));
        transfers.onSavingsTransfer(new SavingsTransfer(u.id(), rd, 250_000, LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 6)));
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("""
                select g.type from goal_contributions c join goals g on g.id = c.goal_id
                where c.transaction_id = ?
                """).param(rd).query(String.class).single()).isEqualTo("MONTHLY");
    }

    @Test
    void aTransferOutsideAnyCycleIsIgnored() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID old = seedTransfer(u, 250_000, Instant.parse("2026-01-06T05:00:00Z"));
        transfers.onSavingsTransfer(new SavingsTransfer(u.id(), old, 250_000, LocalDate.of(2026, 1, 6)));
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from goal_contributions where transaction_id = ?").param(old)
            .query(Long.class).single()).isZero();
    }

    private long rows(TestUser u, UUID goalId, String kind) {
        return jdbc.sql("select count(*) from goal_contributions where user_id = ? and goal_id = ? and kind = ?")
            .params(u.id(), goalId, kind).query(Long.class).single();
    }

    private List<Integer> percents(TestUser u) {
        return jdbc.sql("select percent from goal_milestones where user_id = ? order by percent")
            .param(u.id()).query(Integer.class).list();
    }

    private long xpEvents(TestUser u, String kind) {
        return jdbc.sql("select count(*) from xp_events where user_id = ? and kind = ? and reversed_at is null")
            .params(u.id(), kind).query(Long.class).single();
    }
}
```
