```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

/** Read-only here: rows are written by {@link GoalMilestoneRepository#insertIfAbsent}, once per goal and percent. */
@Entity
@Table(name = "goal_milestones")
class GoalMilestoneEntity {
    @Id
    private UUID id;

    @Column(name = "goal_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID goalId;

    @Column(name = "user_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID userId;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private int percent;

    @Column(name = "reached_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant reachedAt;

    protected GoalMilestoneEntity() {}

    UUID goalId() { return goalId; }
    int percent() { return percent; }
    Instant reachedAt() { return reachedAt; }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalMilestoneRepository.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import java.time.OffsetDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Modifying;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

interface GoalMilestoneRepository extends JpaRepository<GoalMilestoneEntity, UUID> {

    /** 1 for the caller that reached it first, 0 for everyone after: XP and celebrations follow the 1. */
    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query(value = """
            insert into goal_milestones (id, goal_id, user_id, percent, reached_at)
            values (:id, :goalId, :userId, :percent, :at)
            on conflict do nothing
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    int insertIfAbsent(@Param("id") UUID id, @Param("goalId") UUID goalId, @Param("userId") UUID userId,
                       @Param("percent") int percent, @Param("at") OffsetDateTime at);

    List<GoalMilestoneEntity> findByUserIdOrderByReachedAtAscPercentAsc(UUID userId);

    List<GoalMilestoneEntity> findByUserIdAndGoalIdOrderByPercentAsc(UUID userId, UUID goalId);
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalProgressService.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.common.db.Db;
import in.gullak.common.log.Events;
import in.gullak.gamification.BadgeTrigger;
import in.gullak.gamification.XpKind;
import in.gullak.gamification.XpService;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.ObjectProvider;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/** Spec 2.2 milestones and completion. Runs inside the caller's transaction after money lands or a target drops. */
@Component
class GoalProgressService {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GoalProgressService.class);

    private final GoalBook book;
    private final GoalRepository goals;
    private final GoalMilestoneRepository milestones;
    private final XpService xp;
    /** Plan 4's seam; Plan 6 implements it. Until then nothing is registered in production and the call is a no-op. */
    private final ObjectProvider<BadgeTrigger> badges;
    private final Clock clock;

    GoalProgressService(GoalBook book, GoalRepository goals, GoalMilestoneRepository milestones, XpService xp,
                        ObjectProvider<BadgeTrigger> badges, Clock clock) {
        this.book = book;
        this.goals = goals;
        this.milestones = milestones;
        this.xp = xp;
        this.badges = badges;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    void afterChange(UUID userId, UUID goalId) {
        GoalBook.Entry e = book.entry(userId, goalId);
        GoalEntity g = e.goal();
        if (g.type() != GoalType.DEADLINE || g.status() != GoalStatus.ACTIVE) {
            return;
        }
        for (int percent : Milestones.reached(e.savedPaise(), g.targetPaise())) {
            UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
            if (milestones.insertIfAbsent(id, goalId, userId, percent, Db.ts(clock.instant())) == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            log.info("{} user={} goal={} percent={}", Events.GOAL_MILESTONE, userId, goalId, percent);
            if (percent < 100) {
                xp.award(userId, XpKind.GOAL_MILESTONE, "goal_milestone", id);
            } else {
                complete(userId, g);
            }
        }
    }

    private void complete(UUID userId, GoalEntity g) {
        g.complete(clock.instant());
        goals.save(g);
        xp.award(userId, XpKind.GOAL_COMPLETED, "goal", g.getId());
        badges.orderedStream().forEach(b -> b.fire(userId, BadgeTrigger.GOAL_CRUSHER, g.getId()));
        log.info("{} user={} goal={}", Events.GOAL_COMPLETED, userId, g.getId());
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalLedger.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.Cycle;
import in.gullak.common.log.Events;
import in.gullak.profile.Profile;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/** Spec 2.2 "savings come out first": each payday every active deadline goal gets its contribution. */
@Component
class GoalLedger {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GoalLedger.class);

    private final GoalBook book;
    private final GoalContributionRepository contributions;
    private final GoalProgressService progress;

    GoalLedger(GoalBook book, GoalContributionRepository contributions, GoalProgressService progress) {
        this.book = book;
        this.contributions = contributions;
        this.progress = progress;
    }

    /** Records the planned contribution of every goal with a payday inside {@code cycle}. Safe to call twice. */
    void recordPlanned(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, Cycle cycle, Profile profile) {
        int recorded = 0;
        for (GoalBook.Entry e : book.live(userId)) {
            GoalEntity g = e.goal();
            if (g.type() != GoalType.DEADLINE || g.status() != GoalStatus.ACTIVE) {
                continue;
            }
            List<LocalDate> paydays = PaydayCalendar.between(g.createdOn(), g.deadlineMonth(), profile.paydayRule(), profile.paydayDay());
            boolean due = paydays.stream().anyMatch(p -> !p.isBefore(cycle.start()) && !p.isAfter(cycle.end()));
            if (!due) {
                continue;
            }
            int leftIncludingThis = (int) paydays.stream().filter(p -> !p.isBefore(cycle.start())).count();
            long amountLeft = g.targetPaise() - e.savedPaise();
            long amount = Math.min(GoalMath.contribution(amountLeft, leftIncludingThis), amountLeft);
            if (amount <= 0) {
                continue;
            }
            if (contributions.insertPlannedIfAbsent(UUID.randomUUID(), g.getId(), userId, cycleId, amount) == 1) {
                recorded++;
                progress.afterChange(userId, g.getId());
            }
        }
        if (recorded > 0) {
            log.info("{} user={} cycle={} goals={}", Events.GOAL_CONTRIBUTION, userId, cycleId, recorded);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.Cycle;
import in.gullak.budget.CycleOpenedListener;
import in.gullak.profile.Profile;
import java.util.UUID;

/** Payday work for goals, run when budget opens a new cycle. */
@Component
class GoalCycleHooks implements CycleOpenedListener {
    private final GoalLedger ledger;

    GoalCycleHooks(GoalLedger ledger) {
        this.ledger = ledger;
    }

    @Override
    public void cycleOpened(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, Cycle cycle, Profile profile) {
        ledger.recordPlanned(userId, cycleId, cycle, profile);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalSavingsTransfers.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.CycleRecord;
import in.gullak.budget.CycleService;
import in.gullak.common.log.Events;
import in.gullak.ingestion.imports.SavingsTransfer;
import in.gullak.ingestion.imports.SavingsTransferListener;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.Function;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

/**
 * MAT-19: a statement transfer to savings is shown on the goal it most likely paid for, as evidence. It never adds to
 * saved, because that cycle's planned contribution already counted the money.
 */
@Component
class GoalSavingsTransfers implements SavingsTransferListener {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(GoalSavingsTransfers.class);
    private static final long TOLERANCE_FLOOR_PAISE = 500;

    private final CycleService cycles;
    private final GoalBook book;
    private final GoalContributionRepository contributions;

    GoalSavingsTransfers(CycleService cycles, GoalBook book, GoalContributionRepository contributions) {
        this.cycles = cycles;
        this.book = book;
        this.contributions = contributions;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void onSavingsTransfer(SavingsTransfer transfer) {
        UUID userId = transfer.userId();
        UUID transactionId = transfer.transactionId();
        long amountPaise = transfer.amountPaise();
        LocalDate date = transfer.date();
        Optional<CycleRecord> cycle = cycles.covering(userId, date);
        if (cycle.isEmpty()) {
            log.info("{} user={} txn={} matched=none", Events.GOAL_TRANSFER_CONFIRMED, userId, transactionId);
            return;
        }
        UUID cycleId = cycle.get().id();
        Map<UUID, GoalEntity> live = book.live(userId).stream()
            .map(GoalBook.Entry::goal)
            .filter(g -> g.type() == GoalType.DEADLINE)
            .collect(Collectors.toMap(GoalEntity::getId, Function.identity()));
        Set<UUID> alreadyConfirmed = contributions.findByUserIdAndCycleIdAndKind(userId, cycleId, ContributionKind.CONFIRMED_TRANSFER)
            .stream().map(GoalContributionEntity::goalId).collect(Collectors.toSet());
        Optional<UUID> match = contributions.findByUserIdAndCycleIdAndKind(userId, cycleId, ContributionKind.PLANNED).stream()
            .filter(c -> live.containsKey(c.goalId()) && !alreadyConfirmed.contains(c.goalId()))
            .filter(c -> Math.abs(c.amountPaise() - amountPaise) <= Math.max(TOLERANCE_FLOOR_PAISE, c.amountPaise() * 2 / 100))
            .min(Comparator.comparingLong(c -> Math.abs(c.amountPaise() - amountPaise)))
            .map(GoalContributionEntity::goalId);
        UUID goalId = match.orElseGet(() -> book.monthly(userId).getId());
        int inserted = contributions.insertTransferIfAbsent(UUID.randomUUID(), goalId, userId, cycleId, amountPaise, transactionId, date);
        if (inserted == 1) {
            log.info("{} user={} txn={} goal={} matched={}", Events.GOAL_TRANSFER_CONFIRMED, userId, transactionId, goalId,
                match.isPresent() ? "goal" : "monthly");
        }
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

/** Builds {@link GoalView}s. One place, so every endpoint shows a goal the same way. */
final class GoalViews {
    private GoalViews() {}

    static GoalView deadline(GoalBook.Entry e, GoalProgress p, GoalBook.Extras extras) {
        GoalEntity g = e.goal();
        return new GoalView(g.getId(), g.name(), g.type(), g.targetPaise(), g.deadlineMonth(), g.mode(), g.status(),
            e.savedPaise(), p.expectedPaise(), p.behindPaise(), p.percent(), p.expectedPercent(), p.contributionPaise(),
            p.paydaysTotal(), p.paydaysElapsed(), p.paydaysLeft(), extras.lastMilestone(g.getId()));
    }

    /** Monthly savings: the target is the profile's savings; progress fields are zero. */
    static GoalView monthly(GoalEntity g, long savingsPaise) {
        return new GoalView(g.getId(), g.name(), g.type(), savingsPaise, null, g.mode(), g.status(),
            savingsPaise, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, null);
    }
}
```

```java
        GoalView.Milestone lastMilestone(UUID goalId) {

            .map(m -> new GoalView.Milestone(m.percent(), m.reachedAt()))
            .toList();
```

### Task 6: Cycle close: leftover to savings, rollover, Strict debt and Flexible shortfall

**Owner:** backend-engineer. **Test IDs:** BUD-22, BUD-23, BUD-24, BUD-25 (integration half), BUD-34 (new: closing is idempotent). **Red team:** yes (close the same cycle from parallel requests, apply a choice to another user's cycle, apply a choice twice).

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/CycleCloseService.java`
- Modify: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalCycleHooks.java` (close the previous cycle first), `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalBook.java` (`slices`), `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/common/log/Events.java` (`CYCLE_LEFTOVER_CHOSEN`)
- Test: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/goals/CycleCloseApiTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `CycleClose`, `LeftoverChoice`; Task 2's `BudgetService.poolAtClose`, `CycleService.openEndingBefore`, `markClosed`, `recordChoice`, `find`; Task 3's `GoalContributionRepository.deleteCloseEffects`, `GoalContributionEntity.of`.
- Produces (public, for Plans 6 and 7):
  - `CycleCloseService.close(UUID userId, UUID cycleId) : CloseOutcome(boolean alreadyClosed, boolean savingsMet, CycleClose.Result result)` closes one ended cycle with the defaults (leftover → an `EXTRA` contribution on monthly savings; shortfall → `carried_paise` debt). Idempotent; 404 for another user's cycle; 422 `NOT_POSSIBLE` for a cycle that has not ended. This is what Plan 6's `CycleCloser` adapter calls (Plan 6 expected `CycleClose.compute(userId, cycleId)` with `alreadyClosed()` and `savingsMet()`; the adapter maps to this). `CycleService.markClosed` logs `cycle.closed`; Plan 6's job also logs it, so Plan 6 keeps one of the two (LOG-03).
  - `CycleCloseService.closeEndedBefore(UUID userId, LocalDate date) : int` closes every open cycle that ended before `date`; the next cycle's opening calls it.
  - `CycleCloseService.applyChoice(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, LeftoverChoice choice) : CycleClose.Result` replaces the default close effects with the user's pick (`ROLLOVER` or `SAVINGS` for a leftover, `CARRY` or `TAKE` for a shortfall) and records `cycles.leftover_choice`. It throws 404 for another user's cycle and 422 `NOT_POSSIBLE` for an open cycle, a second choice or a choice that does not fit; Plan 7's `POST /budget/cycles/{id}/leftover` may map those to its own `CYCLE_OPEN` and `ALREADY_CHOSEN` codes before calling it.
  - `GoalBook.slices(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, long savingsPaise) : List<CycleClose.Slice>` (Task 8's `TAKE` reuses it).

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.common.error.NotFoundException;
import in.gullak.common.error.UnprocessableException;
import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.Callable;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;

class CycleCloseApiTest extends ApiTest {
    static final Instant OCT_10 = Instant.parse("2026-10-10T06:00:00Z");
    static final Instant NOV_1 = Instant.parse("2026-11-01T04:00:00Z");

    @Autowired CycleCloseService closer;

    @Test
    void theLeftoverGoesToSavings() throws Exception { // BUD-22: ₹1,700 left
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 1_330_000, OCT_10);
        clock.set(NOV_1);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(50_000));
        UUID oct = cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01");
        assertThat(cycleColumn(oct, "closed_at is not null", Boolean.class)).isTrue();
        assertThat(cycleColumn(oct, "leftover_paise", Long.class)).isEqualTo(170_000L);
        assertThat(cycleColumn(oct, "carried_paise", Long.class)).isZero();
        assertThat(cycleColumn(oct, "leftover_choice", String.class)).isNull();
        assertThat(sum(u, oct, "EXTRA", "MONTHLY")).isEqualTo(170_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void aRolloverMovesItIntoTheNextPool() throws Exception { // BUD-23
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 1_330_000, OCT_10);
        clock.set(NOV_1);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        UUID oct = cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01");
        closer.applyChoice(u.id(), oct, LeftoverChoice.ROLLOVER);
        assertThat(sum(u, oct, "EXTRA", "MONTHLY")).isZero();
        assertThat(cycleColumn(oct, "leftover_choice", String.class)).isEqualTo("ROLLOVER");
        assertThat(cycleColumn(oct, "carried_paise", Long.class)).isEqualTo(170_000L);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(55_600)); // (15,000 + 1,700) ÷ 30
    }

    @Test
    void aStrictShortfallIsCarriedAsDebt() throws Exception { // BUD-24: pool −₹400 → next spendable ₹14,600
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        monthlyMode(u, "STRICT");
        seedSpend(u, 1_540_000, OCT_10);
        clock.set(NOV_1);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(48_600));
        UUID oct = cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01");
        assertThat(cycleColumn(oct, "leftover_paise", Long.class)).isEqualTo(-40_000L);
        assertThat(cycleColumn(oct, "carried_paise", Long.class)).isEqualTo(-40_000L);
        assertThat(sum(u, oct, "SHORTFALL", "MONTHLY")).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void aFlexibleShortfallCanComeOutOfThisCyclesSavings() throws Exception { // BUD-25
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 1_540_000, OCT_10);
        clock.set(NOV_1);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(48_600)); // the default carries the debt
        UUID oct = cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01");
        CycleClose.Result r = closer.applyChoice(u.id(), oct, LeftoverChoice.TAKE);
        assertThat(r.takenPaise()).isEqualTo(40_000L);
        assertThat(sum(u, oct, "SHORTFALL", "MONTHLY")).isEqualTo(-40_000L);
        assertThat(cycleColumn(oct, "carried_paise", Long.class)).isZero();
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(50_000)); // pool back to normal
    }

    @Test
    void withStrictSavingsTheShortfallComesFromAFlexibleGoal() throws Exception { // GOAL-23 integration
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        monthlyMode(u, "STRICT");
        UUID oct = cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01");
        UUID goa = seedGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE", "2026-09-20");
        jdbc.sql("insert into goal_contributions (id, goal_id, user_id, cycle_id, amount_paise, kind) values (?, ?, ?, ?, 360000, 'PLANNED')")
            .params(UUID.randomUUID(), goa, u.id(), oct).update();
        seedSpend(u, 1_540_000, OCT_10);
        clock.set(NOV_1);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        closer.applyChoice(u.id(), oct, LeftoverChoice.TAKE);
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select coalesce(sum(amount_paise), 0) from goal_contributions where goal_id = ? and kind = 'SHORTFALL'")
            .param(goa).query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(-40_000L);
    }

    @Test
    void closingTwiceChangesNothing() throws Exception { // BUD-34
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 1_330_000, OCT_10);
        clock.set(NOV_1);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThat(closer.closeEndedBefore(u.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 1))).isZero();
        assertThat(closer.closeEndedBefore(u.id(), LocalDate.of(2026, 11, 1))).isZero();
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from goal_contributions where user_id = ? and kind = 'EXTRA'")
            .param(u.id()).query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    @Test
    void racingFirstRequestsCloseOnceAndPlanOnce() throws Exception { // Review focus
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        seedSpend(u, 1_330_000, OCT_10);
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-11-02T04:00:00Z"));
        ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(4);
        try {
            CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);
            List<Future<Integer>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
                Callable<Integer> call = () -> {
                    start.await();
                    return budgetToday(u).andReturn().getResponse().getStatus();
                };
                futures.add(pool.submit(call));
            }
            start.countDown();
            List<Integer> statuses = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Future<Integer> f : futures) {
                statuses.add(f.get());
            }
            assertThat(statuses).containsOnly(200);
        } finally {
            pool.shutdownNow();
        }
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from cycles where user_id = ?").param(u.id()).query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(2L);
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from goal_contributions where user_id = ? and kind = 'EXTRA'").param(u.id()).query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(1L);
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from goal_contributions where user_id = ? and kind = 'PLANNED'").param(u.id()).query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    @Test
    void aChoiceMustFitAndHappensOnce() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID oct = cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> closer.applyChoice(u.id(), oct, LeftoverChoice.ROLLOVER)).isInstanceOf(UnprocessableException.class); // still open
        seedSpend(u, 1_330_000, OCT_10);
        clock.set(NOV_1);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk());
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> closer.applyChoice(u.id(), oct, LeftoverChoice.TAKE)).isInstanceOf(UnprocessableException.class);
        closer.applyChoice(u.id(), oct, LeftoverChoice.SAVINGS);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> closer.applyChoice(u.id(), oct, LeftoverChoice.ROLLOVER)).isInstanceOf(UnprocessableException.class);
        assertThat(sum(u, oct, "EXTRA", "MONTHLY")).isEqualTo(170_000L);
        TestUser other = exampleUser();
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> closer.applyChoice(other.id(), oct, LeftoverChoice.ROLLOVER)).isInstanceOf(NotFoundException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void closeReportsWhetherSavingsWereMetAndRunsOnce() throws Exception { // the seam Plan 6's job uses
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID oct = cycleIdStarting(u, "2026-10-01");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> closer.close(u.id(), oct)).isInstanceOf(UnprocessableException.class); // still running on 23 Oct
        seedSpend(u, 1_540_000, OCT_10);
        clock.set(NOV_1);
        CycleCloseService.CloseOutcome first = closer.close(u.id(), oct);
        assertThat(first.alreadyClosed()).isFalse();
        assertThat(first.savingsMet()).isFalse();
        assertThat(first.result().carriedPaise()).isEqualTo(-40_000L);
        CycleCloseService.CloseOutcome second = closer.close(u.id(), oct);
        assertThat(second.alreadyClosed()).isTrue();
        assertThat(second.result()).isNull();
    }

    private void monthlyMode(TestUser u, String mode) throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .content("{\"type\":\"MONTHLY\",\"mode\":\"" + mode + "\"}")).andExpect(status().is2xxSuccessful());
    }

    private <T> T cycleColumn(UUID cycleId, String column, Class<T> type) {
        return jdbc.sql("select " + column + " from cycles where id = ?").param(cycleId).query(type).single();
    }

    private long sum(TestUser u, UUID cycleId, String kind, String goalType) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select coalesce(sum(c.amount_paise), 0) from goal_contributions c join goals g on g.id = c.goal_id
                where c.user_id = ? and c.cycle_id = ? and c.kind = ? and g.type = ?
                """).params(u.id(), cycleId, kind, goalType).query(Long.class).single();
    }
}
```

```java
    public static final String CYCLE_LEFTOVER_CHOSEN = "cycle.leftover_chosen";
```

Add to `GoalBook` (imports `java.util.ArrayList`):

```java
    /**
     * What a shortfall in {@code cycleId} could come out of: unassigned savings (savings minus every planned
     * contribution, minus what was already handed back) and each deadline goal's planned share still left.
     */
    List<CycleClose.Slice> slices(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, long savingsPaise) {
        GoalEntity monthly = monthly(userId);
        List<GoalContributionEntity> rows = contributions.findByUserIdAndCycleId(userId, cycleId);
        long plannedAll = rows.stream().filter(c -> c.kind() == ContributionKind.PLANNED)
            .mapToLong(GoalContributionEntity::amountPaise).sum();
        Map<UUID, Long> net = rows.stream()
            .filter(c -> c.kind() == ContributionKind.PLANNED || c.kind() == ContributionKind.SHORTFALL)
            .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(GoalContributionEntity::goalId, Collectors.summingLong(GoalContributionEntity::amountPaise)));
        List<CycleClose.Slice> out = new ArrayList<>();
        out.add(new CycleClose.Slice(monthly.getId(), true, monthly.mode(),
            Math.max(0, savingsPaise - plannedAll + net.getOrDefault(monthly.getId(), 0L))));
        for (Entry e : live(userId)) {
            GoalEntity g = e.goal();
            long left = net.getOrDefault(g.getId(), 0L);
            if (g.type() == GoalType.DEADLINE && left > 0) {
                out.add(new CycleClose.Slice(g.getId(), false, g.mode(), left));
            }
        }
        return List.copyOf(out);
    }
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/CycleCloseService.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.BudgetService;
import in.gullak.budget.CycleRecord;
import in.gullak.budget.CycleService;
import in.gullak.common.error.ErrorCode;
import in.gullak.common.error.NotFoundException;
import in.gullak.common.error.UnprocessableException;
import in.gullak.common.log.Events;
import in.gullak.common.time.Ist;
import in.gullak.profile.ProfileService;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

/** Spec 2.1 and 2.2 at cycle close. Called when the next cycle opens and by Plan 6's daily job. */
@Service
public class CycleCloseService {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(CycleCloseService.class);
    private static final List<ContributionKind> CLOSE_KINDS = List.of(ContributionKind.EXTRA, ContributionKind.SHORTFALL);

    private final CycleService cycles;
    private final BudgetService budget;
    private final GoalBook book;
    private final GoalContributionRepository contributions;
    private final ProfileService profiles;
    private final Clock clock;

    CycleCloseService(CycleService cycles, BudgetService budget, GoalBook book, GoalContributionRepository contributions,
                      ProfileService profiles, Clock clock) {
        this.cycles = cycles;
        this.budget = budget;
        this.book = book;
        this.contributions = contributions;
        this.profiles = profiles;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    /**
     * What one close did. {@code savingsMet} is true when the cycle did not end short (spec 2.6's "monthly savings met
     * at cycle close", which Plan 6 rewards); {@code result} is null when the cycle was already closed.
     */
    public record CloseOutcome(boolean alreadyClosed, boolean savingsMet, CycleClose.Result result) {}

    /** Closes every still-open cycle of the user that ended before {@code date}, oldest first; returns how many it closed. */
    @Transactional
    public int closeEndedBefore(UUID userId, LocalDate date) {
        int closed = 0;
        for (CycleRecord c : cycles.openEndingBefore(userId, date)) {
            if (!close(userId, c.id()).alreadyClosed()) {
                closed++;
            }
        }
        return closed;
    }

    /** Closes one ended cycle with the defaults. Idempotent: a second call (job and request racing) changes nothing. */
    @Transactional
    public CloseOutcome close(UUID userId, UUID cycleId) {
        CycleRecord c = cycles.find(userId, cycleId).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Cycle"));
        if (c.closedAt() != null) {
            return new CloseOutcome(true, c.leftoverPaise() == null || c.leftoverPaise() >= 0, null);
        }
        if (!c.cycle().end().isBefore(Ist.today(clock))) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE, "This cycle has not ended yet");
        }
        long pool = budget.poolAtClose(userId, cycleId);
        CycleClose.Result r = CycleClose.compute(pool, CycleClose.defaultChoice(pool), List.of());
        if (!cycles.markClosed(userId, cycleId, clock.instant(), r.leftoverPaise(), r.carriedPaise())) {
            return new CloseOutcome(true, pool >= 0, null);
        }
        write(userId, cycleId, r);
        return new CloseOutcome(false, pool >= 0, r);
    }

    /** The user's pick for a closed cycle (Plan 7): the default close effects are replaced, once. */
    @Transactional
    public CycleClose.Result applyChoice(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, LeftoverChoice choice) {
        CycleRecord c = cycles.find(userId, cycleId).orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("Cycle"));
        if (c.closedAt() == null) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE, "This cycle is still open");
        }
        if (c.leftoverChoice() != null) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE, "You already chose for this cycle");
        }
        long pool = c.leftoverPaise() == null ? 0 : c.leftoverPaise();
        boolean fits = pool >= 0
            ? choice == LeftoverChoice.SAVINGS || choice == LeftoverChoice.ROLLOVER
            : choice == LeftoverChoice.CARRY || choice == LeftoverChoice.TAKE;
        if (!fits) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE, pool >= 0
                ? "There's money left, so it can go to savings or roll over"
                : "The cycle ended short, so it can be carried or taken from savings");
        }
        List<CycleClose.Slice> slices = choice == LeftoverChoice.TAKE
            ? book.slices(userId, cycleId, profiles.get(userId).savingsPaise())
            : List.of();
        CycleClose.Result r = CycleClose.compute(pool, choice, slices);
        contributions.deleteCloseEffects(userId, cycleId, CLOSE_KINDS);
        if (!cycles.recordChoice(userId, cycleId, choice.name(), r.carriedPaise())) {
            throw new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE, "You already chose for this cycle");
        }
        write(userId, cycleId, r);
        log.info("{} user={} cycle={} choice={}", Events.CYCLE_LEFTOVER_CHOSEN, userId, cycleId, choice);
        return r;
    }

    private void write(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, CycleClose.Result r) {
        if (r.extraSavedPaise() > 0) {
            contributions.save(GoalContributionEntity.of(book.monthly(userId).getId(), userId, cycleId,
                r.extraSavedPaise(), ContributionKind.EXTRA, null));
        }
        for (CycleClose.Reduction cut : r.reductions()) {
            contributions.save(GoalContributionEntity.of(cut.goalId(), userId, cycleId, -cut.amountPaise(),
                ContributionKind.SHORTFALL, null));
        }
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.Cycle;
import in.gullak.budget.CycleOpenedListener;
import in.gullak.profile.Profile;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/** Payday work for goals, run when budget opens a new cycle: close what ended, then set this payday's savings aside. */
@Component
class GoalCycleHooks implements CycleOpenedListener {
    private final CycleCloseService closer;
    private final GoalLedger ledger;

    GoalCycleHooks(CycleCloseService closer, GoalLedger ledger) {
        this.closer = closer;
        this.ledger = ledger;
    }

    @Override
    public void cycleOpened(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, Cycle cycle, Profile profile) {
        closer.closeEndedBefore(userId, cycle.start());
        ledger.recordPlanned(userId, cycleId, cycle, profile);
    }
}
```

**Owner:** backend-engineer. **Test IDs:** API-TDY-04, API-TDY-05, API-TDY-06 (catch-up half), GOAL-22 (new: tighten is not offered on the last day or when the gap is bigger than tomorrow's pool). **Red team:** no (read-only; Task 8's red team attacks the actions).

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/CatchUpActionEntity.java`, `CatchUpActionRepository.java`, `CatchUpPlanner.java`, `GoalBudgetAdjustment.java`
- Modify: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalView.java` (`catchingUp`), `GoalViews.java`, `GoalBook.java` (`Extras.catchingUp`; constructor gains `CycleService` and `CatchUpActionRepository`), `GoalDetail.java` (`catchUp`), `GoalService.java` (`get` uses the snapshot and the planner; constructor gains `CatchUpPlanner`), `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/support/ApiTest.java` (`goaBehind`), `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/budget/TodayApiTest.java` (`poolNegative`)
- Test: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/goals/CatchUpTodayApiTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's `BudgetAdjustment`, `TodaySnapshot`, `CatchUp`, `BudgetService.snapshot`; Task 1's `CatchUpMath`, `CycleClose`; Task 6's `GoalBook.slices`.
- Produces:
  - `GET /api/v1/budget/today` returns `catchUp` (spec 4.6 "catch-up if any"): `POOL_NEGATIVE` when the pool after today's spending is below zero (card for monthly savings; option `TAKE` while some Flexible savings remain and no `TAKE` is active), else `APPLIED` for the latest plan picked this cycle, else `BEHIND` for the active deadline goal furthest behind without a plan this cycle (options `TIGHTEN` when at least two days are left and the gap fits tomorrow's pool; `MOVE` for Flexible goals with a monthly contribution; `LOWER` for Flexible goals); `null` otherwise.
  - The pool moves by every live catch-up in the cycle: `TIGHTEN` takes its amount from the day after it was picked, `TAKE` gives its amount back from the day it was picked.
  - `GET /api/v1/goals/{id}` gains `catchUp` (the same card for that goal, or absent); every `GoalView` gains `catchingUp`.
  - Package-private `CatchUpPlanner.home(userId, snapshot)`, `CatchUpPlanner.forGoal(userId, goalId, snapshot)`, `CatchUpActionRepository.findFirstByUserIdAndGoalIdAndCycleIdAndUndoneAtIsNull`, `insertIfNoneActive`, `markUndone` (Task 8).
  - Test helper `ApiTest.goaBehind(TestUser, String mode) : UUID`.

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;

class CatchUpTodayApiTest extends ApiTest {

    @Test
    void aGoalBehindOffersThreeWaysWithTheirNumbers() throws Exception { // API-TDY-04
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        budgetToday(u)
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(59_300))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.tomorrowPaise").value(57_700))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.situation").value("BEHIND"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.goalId").value(goa.toString()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.goalName").value("Goa trip"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.strict").value(false))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.gapPaise").value(180_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options.length()").value(3))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[0].kind").value("TIGHTEN"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[0].perDayPaise").value(22_500))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[0].days").value(8))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[0].tomorrowPaise").value(35_200))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[1].kind").value("MOVE"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[1].newDeadline").value("2027-04"))

            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[2].kind").value("LOWER"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[2].newTargetPaise").value(2_820_000))

    @Test
    void aStrictGoalBehindOnlyOffersSpendingLess() throws Exception { // API-TDY-05

            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.strict").value(true))

            .content("{\"type\":\"MONTHLY\",\"mode\":\"STRICT\"}")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        seedSpend(u, 1_540_000, Instant.parse("2026-10-10T06:00:00Z"));

    @Test

        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        seedSpend(u, 600_000, Instant.parse("2026-12-11T06:00:00Z")); // pool now −₹660 before today's ₹720
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.situation").value("POOL_NEGATIVE"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.gapPaise").value(138_000));
    }

    @Test
    void theGoalFurthestBehindIsShown() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        UUID bike = seedGoal(u, "Bike", 6_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE", "2026-10-20"); // nothing saved: ₹24,000 behind
        budgetToday(u)
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.goalId").value(bike.toString()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.gapPaise").value(2_400_000));
    }

    @Test
    void tightenIsNotOfferedOnTheLastDay() throws Exception { // GOAL-22
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-12-31T04:00:00Z"));
        budgetToday(u)
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.tomorrowPaise").doesNotExist())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options.length()").value(2))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[0].kind").value("MOVE"));
    }

    @Test
    void tightenIsNotOfferedWhenTheGapIsBiggerThanWhatIsLeft() throws Exception { // GOAL-22
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        seedSpend(u, 450_000, Instant.parse("2026-12-11T06:00:00Z")); // tomorrow's pool ₹120
        budgetToday(u)
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.situation").value("BEHIND"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[0].kind").value("MOVE"));
    }

    @Test
    void aGoalOnTrackShowsNoCard() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp").doesNotExist());
    }

    @Test
    void theGoalDetailCarriesItsOwnCard() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals/" + goa).with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.situation").value("BEHIND"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.options[0].perDayPaise").value(22_500))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.catchingUp").value(false))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.behindPaise").value(180_000));
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u))).andExpect(jsonPath("$.goals[0].catchingUp").value(false));
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.CatchUpKind;
import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.EnumType;
import jakarta.persistence.Enumerated;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.UUID;

/** Read-only here: rows are written by {@link CatchUpActionRepository#insertIfNoneActive} and undone by {@code markUndone}. */
@Entity
@Table(name = "catch_up_actions")
class CatchUpActionEntity {
    @Id
    private UUID id;

    @Column(name = "goal_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID goalId;

    @Column(name = "user_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID userId;

    @Column(name = "cycle_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID cycleId;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 10)
    private CatchUpKind kind;

    @Column(name = "gap_paise", nullable = false)
    private long gapPaise;

    @Column(name = "amount_paise")
    private Long amountPaise;

    @Column(name = "per_day_paise")
    private Long perDayPaise;

    @Column(name = "days")
    private Integer days;

    @Column(name = "previous_deadline")
    private LocalDate previousDeadline;

    @Column(name = "new_deadline")
    private LocalDate newDeadline;

    @Column(name = "previous_target_paise")
    private Long previousTargetPaise;

    @Column(name = "new_target_paise")
    private Long newTargetPaise;

    @Column(name = "effective_from", nullable = false)
    private LocalDate effectiveFrom;

    @Column(name = "undone_at")
    private Instant undoneAt;

    @Column(name = "created_at", insertable = false, updatable = false)
    private Instant createdAt;

    protected CatchUpActionEntity() {}

    UUID getId() { return id; }
    UUID goalId() { return goalId; }
    CatchUpKind kind() { return kind; }
    long gapPaise() { return gapPaise; }
    Long amountPaise() { return amountPaise; }
    Long perDayPaise() { return perDayPaise; }
    Integer days() { return days; }
    LocalDate previousDeadline() { return previousDeadline; }
    LocalDate newDeadline() { return newDeadline; }
    Long previousTargetPaise() { return previousTargetPaise; }
    Long newTargetPaise() { return newTargetPaise; }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/CatchUpActionRepository.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.OffsetDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Modifying;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

interface CatchUpActionRepository extends JpaRepository<CatchUpActionEntity, UUID> {

    List<CatchUpActionEntity> findByUserIdAndCycleIdAndUndoneAtIsNullOrderByCreatedAtDesc(UUID userId, UUID cycleId);

    Optional<CatchUpActionEntity> findFirstByUserIdAndGoalIdAndCycleIdAndUndoneAtIsNull(UUID userId, UUID goalId, UUID cycleId);

    /** What live plans do to the pool for the budget of {@code day}: TIGHTEN takes its amount, TAKE gives it back. */
    @Query(value = """
            select cast(coalesce(sum(case kind when 'TIGHTEN' then -amount_paise when 'TAKE' then amount_paise else 0 end), 0) as bigint)
            from catch_up_actions
            where user_id = :userId and cycle_id = :cycleId and undone_at is null and effective_from <= :day
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    long poolDelta(@Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("cycleId") UUID cycleId, @Param("day") LocalDate day);

    /** 0 when the goal already has a live plan this cycle (the partial unique index): a double tap applies one. */
    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query(value = """
            insert into catch_up_actions (id, goal_id, user_id, cycle_id, kind, gap_paise, amount_paise, per_day_paise, days,
                previous_deadline, new_deadline, previous_target_paise, new_target_paise, effective_from)
            values (:id, :goalId, :userId, :cycleId, :kind, :gap, :amount, :perDay, :days,
                :previousDeadline, :newDeadline, :previousTarget, :newTarget, :effectiveFrom)
            on conflict do nothing
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    int insertIfNoneActive(@Param("id") UUID id, @Param("goalId") UUID goalId, @Param("userId") UUID userId,
                           @Param("cycleId") UUID cycleId, @Param("kind") String kind, @Param("gap") long gap,
                           @Param("amount") Long amount, @Param("perDay") Long perDay, @Param("days") Integer days,
                           @Param("previousDeadline") LocalDate previousDeadline, @Param("newDeadline") LocalDate newDeadline,
                           @Param("previousTarget") Long previousTarget, @Param("newTarget") Long newTarget,
                           @Param("effectiveFrom") LocalDate effectiveFrom);

    /** 1 for the caller that undid it; 0 if it was already undone (a second Undo). */
    @Modifying(flushAutomatically = true, clearAutomatically = true)
    @Query(value = """
            update catch_up_actions set undone_at = :at, updated_at = now()
            where id = :id and user_id = :userId and undone_at is null
            """, nativeQuery = true)
    int markUndone(@Param("id") UUID id, @Param("userId") UUID userId, @Param("at") OffsetDateTime at);
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/CatchUpPlanner.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.CatchUp;
import in.gullak.budget.CatchUpKind;
import in.gullak.budget.CatchUpSituation;
import in.gullak.budget.TodaySnapshot;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.Function;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/** Spec 2.2 catch-up plan: which card to show and the exact numbers each option will apply. */
@Component
class CatchUpPlanner {
    private final GoalBook book;
    private final CatchUpActionRepository actions;

    CatchUpPlanner(GoalBook book, CatchUpActionRepository actions) {
        this.book = book;
        this.actions = actions;
    }

    /** Home: an empty pool first, then a plan already picked, then the goal furthest behind. */
    Optional<CatchUp> home(UUID userId, TodaySnapshot s) {
        if (s.cycleId() == null) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        List<CatchUpActionEntity> live = actions.findByUserIdAndCycleIdAndUndoneAtIsNullOrderByCreatedAtDesc(userId, s.cycleId());
        GoalEntity monthly = book.monthly(userId);
        boolean taken = live.stream().anyMatch(a -> a.goalId().equals(monthly.getId()));
        Optional<CatchUp> pool = poolCard(userId, monthly, taken, s);
        if (pool.isPresent()) {
            return pool;
        }
        List<GoalBook.Entry> entries = book.live(userId);
        Map<UUID, GoalBook.Entry> byId = entries.stream().collect(Collectors.toMap(e -> e.goal().getId(), Function.identity()));
        for (CatchUpActionEntity a : live) {
            GoalBook.Entry e = byId.get(a.goalId());
            if (e != null) {
                return Optional.of(applied(e, a));
            }
        }
        Set<UUID> withPlan = live.stream().map(CatchUpActionEntity::goalId).collect(Collectors.toSet());
        record Candidate(GoalBook.Entry entry, GoalProgress progress) {}
        return entries.stream()
            .filter(e -> e.goal().type() == GoalType.DEADLINE && e.goal().status() == GoalStatus.ACTIVE)
            .filter(e -> !withPlan.contains(e.goal().getId()))
            .map(e -> new Candidate(e, book.progress(e, s.date(), s.paydayRule(), s.paydayDay())))
            .filter(c -> c.progress().behindPaise() > 0)
            .max(Comparator.comparingLong((Candidate c) -> c.progress().behindPaise()))
            .map(c -> behind(c.entry(), c.progress(), s));
    }

    /** One goal's card: its live plan, the empty-pool card for monthly savings, or its behind card. */
    Optional<CatchUp> forGoal(UUID userId, UUID goalId, TodaySnapshot s) {
        if (s.cycleId() == null) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        GoalBook.Entry e = book.entry(userId, goalId);
        Optional<CatchUpActionEntity> live = actions.findFirstByUserIdAndGoalIdAndCycleIdAndUndoneAtIsNull(userId, goalId, s.cycleId());
        if (live.isPresent()) {
            return Optional.of(applied(e, live.get()));
        }
        if (e.goal().type() == GoalType.MONTHLY) {
            return poolCard(userId, e.goal(), false, s);
        }
        if (e.goal().status() != GoalStatus.ACTIVE) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        GoalProgress p = book.progress(e, s.date(), s.paydayRule(), s.paydayDay());
        return p.behindPaise() > 0 ? Optional.of(behind(e, p, s)) : Optional.empty();
    }

    private Optional<CatchUp> poolCard(UUID userId, GoalEntity monthly, boolean taken, TodaySnapshot s) {
        long deficit = -s.poolAfterTodayPaise();
        if (deficit <= 0) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        long reducible = taken ? 0 : book.slices(userId, s.cycleId(), s.savingsPaise()).stream()
            .filter(x -> x.mode() == GoalMode.FLEXIBLE)
            .mapToLong(CycleClose.Slice::amountPaise)
            .sum();
        List<CatchUp.Option> options = reducible > 0 ? List.of(CatchUp.Option.take(Math.min(deficit, reducible))) : List.of();
        return Optional.of(new CatchUp(CatchUpSituation.POOL_NEGATIVE, monthly.getId(), monthly.name(),
            monthly.mode() == GoalMode.STRICT, deficit, options, null));
    }

    private CatchUp behind(GoalBook.Entry e, GoalProgress p, TodaySnapshot s) {
        GoalEntity g = e.goal();
        long gap = p.behindPaise();
        Set<CatchUpKind> allowed = CatchUpMath.allowed(g.mode(), g.type());
        List<CatchUp.Option> options = new ArrayList<>();
        if (allowed.contains(CatchUpKind.TIGHTEN)) {
            tighten(gap, s).ifPresent(options::add);
        }
        if (allowed.contains(CatchUpKind.MOVE) && p.contributionPaise() > 0) {
            YearMonth to = CatchUpMath.movedDeadline(g.deadlineMonth(), gap, p.contributionPaise());
            options.add(CatchUp.Option.move(to, (int) ChronoUnit.MONTHS.between(g.deadlineMonth(), to)));
        }
        if (allowed.contains(CatchUpKind.LOWER)) {
            options.add(CatchUp.Option.lower(CatchUpMath.loweredTarget(g.targetPaise(), gap)));
        }
        return new CatchUp(CatchUpSituation.BEHIND, g.getId(), g.name(), g.mode() == GoalMode.STRICT, gap, List.copyOf(options), null);
    }

    /** From tomorrow: offered with two or more days left and only when tomorrow's pool can give up the gap. */
    private static Optional<CatchUp.Option> tighten(long gap, TodaySnapshot s) {
        int days = s.daysLeft() - 1;
        if (days < 1 || s.tomorrowPoolPaise() == null || gap > s.tomorrowPoolPaise()) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        long pool = s.tomorrowPoolPaise() - gap;
        long tomorrow = pool > 0 ? pool / days / GoalMath.RUPEE * GoalMath.RUPEE : 0;
        return Optional.of(CatchUp.Option.tighten(CatchUpMath.tightenPerDay(gap, days), days, tomorrow));
    }

    private static CatchUp applied(GoalBook.Entry e, CatchUpActionEntity a) {
        GoalEntity g = e.goal();
        YearMonth newDeadline = a.newDeadline() == null ? null : YearMonth.from(a.newDeadline());
        Integer extra = a.kind() == CatchUpKind.MOVE && a.previousDeadline() != null && newDeadline != null
            ? (int) ChronoUnit.MONTHS.between(YearMonth.from(a.previousDeadline()), newDeadline)
            : null;
        CatchUp.Applied applied = new CatchUp.Applied(a.getId(), a.kind(), a.perDayPaise(), a.days(), newDeadline, extra,
            a.newTargetPaise(), a.kind() == CatchUpKind.TAKE ? a.amountPaise() : null, g.status() != GoalStatus.DONE);
        return new CatchUp(CatchUpSituation.APPLIED, g.getId(), g.name(), g.mode() == GoalMode.STRICT, a.gapPaise(),
            List.of(), applied);
    }
}
```

`server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalBudgetAdjustment.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.BudgetAdjustment;
import in.gullak.budget.CatchUp;
import in.gullak.budget.TodaySnapshot;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/** Goals' side of the budget hook: catch-up plans move the pool, and Home gets the catch-up card. */
@Component
class GoalBudgetAdjustment implements BudgetAdjustment {
    private final CatchUpActionRepository actions;
    private final CatchUpPlanner planner;

    GoalBudgetAdjustment(CatchUpActionRepository actions, CatchUpPlanner planner) {
        this.actions = actions;
        this.planner = planner;
    }

    @Override
    public long poolDeltaPaise(UUID userId, UUID cycleId, LocalDate day) {
        return actions.poolDelta(userId, cycleId, day);
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CatchUp> catchUp(UUID userId, TodaySnapshot today) {
        return planner.home(userId, today);
    }
}
```

```java
    record Extras(Map<UUID, GoalView.Milestone> lastMilestones, Set<UUID> catchingUp) {
        static final Extras NONE = new Extras(Map.of(), Set.of());

        GoalView.Milestone lastMilestone(UUID goalId) {
            return lastMilestones.get(goalId);
        }
    }

    Extras extras(UUID userId) {
        Map<UUID, GoalView.Milestone> last = milestones.findByUserIdOrderByReachedAtAscPercentAsc(userId).stream()
            .collect(Collectors.toMap(GoalMilestoneEntity::goalId, m -> new GoalView.Milestone(m.percent(), m.reachedAt()),
                (earlier, later) -> later));
        Set<UUID> catchingUp = cycles.covering(userId, Ist.today(clock))
            .map(c -> actions.findByUserIdAndCycleIdAndUndoneAtIsNullOrderByCreatedAtDesc(userId, c.id()).stream()
                .map(CatchUpActionEntity::goalId).collect(Collectors.toSet()))
            .orElse(Set.of());
        return new Extras(last, catchingUp);
    }
```

### Task 8: Picking and undoing a catch-up plan; the local fall-behind seam

**Owner:** backend-engineer. **Test IDs:** API-GL-18 to API-GL-24, GOAL-15, API-GL-32 (new: take from this cycle's savings and undo it), API-X-01 (goals rows). **Red team:** yes (catch-up on another user's goal, undo twice, double-tapped picks, undo after payday, the dev route outside `local`, logs carrying goal names or amounts).

**Files:**
- Create: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/CatchUpRequest.java`, `CatchUpResult.java`, `DevGoalController.java`
- Modify: `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalService.java` (add `catchUp`, `undo`, `devFallBehind`; constructor gains `CatchUpActionRepository actions` and `GoalProgressService progress`), `server/src/main/java/in/gullak/goals/GoalController.java` (two endpoints)
- Test: `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/goals/CatchUpApiTest.java`, `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/goals/DevGoalApiTest.java`, `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/security/GoalIsolationApiTest.java`, `server/src/test/java/in/gullak/security/GoalLogRedactionApiTest.java`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7's `CatchUpPlanner.forGoal`, `CatchUpActionRepository`; Task 6's `GoalBook.slices`; Task 5's `GoalProgressService.afterChange`; Task 1's `CycleClose.take`.
- Produces:
  - `POST /api/v1/goals/{id}/catch-up {kind: TIGHTEN | MOVE | LOWER | TAKE}` → 200 `{goal: GoalView, today: TodayResponse}`. Errors: 404 another user's goal; 400 `VALIDATION` for `TAKE` on a deadline goal or `TIGHTEN`, `MOVE`, `LOWER` on monthly savings; 409 `STRICT_GOAL` (`MOVE` or `LOWER` on a Strict goal, `TAKE` when every share is Strict); 409 `NOT_BEHIND` (on track, paused, or the pool has not run out); 409 `CATCH_UP_ACTIVE` (a plan is live this cycle); 409 `GOAL_DONE`; 422 `NOT_POSSIBLE` (the option is not offered today, for example tighten on the last day).
  - `POST /api/v1/goals/{id}/catch-up/undo` → 200 `{goal, today}`; 409 `NOTHING_TO_UNDO` when no plan is live in the current cycle (also after payday); 409 `GOAL_DONE`.
  - `POST /api/v1/dev/goals/{id}/fall-behind` (`local` profile only): moves the goal's creation date back two months so two paydays pass without contributions. Used by E2E-06.

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.Callable;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.ResultActions;

class CatchUpApiTest extends ApiTest {

    @Test
    void tightenLowersTomorrowsBudget() throws Exception { // API-GL-18
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        pick(u, goa, "TIGHTEN")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.budgetPaise").value(59_300))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.tomorrowPaise").value(35_200))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.situation").value("APPLIED"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.goalName").value("Goa trip"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.applied.kind").value("TIGHTEN"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.applied.perDayPaise").value(22_500))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.applied.days").value(8))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.applied.canUndo").value(true))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.savedPaise").value(1_200_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.behindPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.catchingUp").value(true));
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.tomorrowPaise").value(35_200)).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(59_300));
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-12-24T04:00:00Z")); // tomorrow: the lower budget is today's now
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(35_200));
    }

    @Test
    void moveTheDeadline() throws Exception { // API-GL-19
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        pick(u, goa, "MOVE")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.deadline").value("2027-04"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.behindPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.applied.newDeadline").value("2027-04"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.applied.extraPaydays").value(1))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.tomorrowPaise").value(57_700));
    }

    @Test
    void lowerTheTarget() throws Exception { // API-GL-20
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        pick(u, goa, "LOWER")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.targetPaise").value(2_820_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.applied.newTargetPaise").value(2_820_000));
    }

    @Test
    void aStrictGoalOnlyTightens() throws Exception { // API-GL-21, GOAL-14
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "STRICT");
        pick(u, goa, "MOVE").andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("STRICT_GOAL"));
        pick(u, goa, "LOWER").andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("STRICT_GOAL"));
        pick(u, goa, "TIGHTEN").andExpect(status().isOk());
    }

    @Test
    void aGoalOnTrackHasNothingToCatchUp() throws Exception { // API-GL-22
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        pick(u, UUID.fromString(goa), "TIGHTEN").andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_BEHIND"));
    }

    @Test
    void undoPutsEverythingBack() throws Exception { // API-GL-23, GOAL-15
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        pick(u, goa, "TIGHTEN").andExpect(status().isOk());
        undo(u, goa)
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.tomorrowPaise").value(57_700))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.situation").value("BEHIND"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.savedPaise").value(1_020_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.catchingUp").value(false));
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from goal_contributions where goal_id = ? and catch_up_id is not null")
            .param(goa).query(Long.class).single()).isZero();
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from catch_up_actions where goal_id = ? and undone_at is not null")
            .param(goa).query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    @Test
    void undoRestoresAMovedDeadlineAndALoweredTarget() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        pick(u, goa, "MOVE").andExpect(status().isOk());
        undo(u, goa).andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.deadline").value("2027-03"));
        pick(u, goa, "LOWER").andExpect(status().isOk());
        undo(u, goa).andExpect(jsonPath("$.goal.targetPaise").value(3_000_000));
    }

    @Test
    void undoWithNothingToUndoIsAConflict() throws Exception { // API-GL-24
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        undo(u, goa).andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOTHING_TO_UNDO"));
        pick(u, goa, "TIGHTEN").andExpect(status().isOk());
        undo(u, goa).andExpect(status().isOk());
        undo(u, goa).andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOTHING_TO_UNDO"));
    }

    @Test
    void aSecondPlanNeedsAnUndoFirst() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        pick(u, goa, "LOWER").andExpect(status().isOk());
        pick(u, goa, "MOVE").andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("CATCH_UP_ACTIVE"));
    }

    @Test
    void takeFromThisCyclesSavingsAndUndoIt() throws Exception { // API-GL-32, BUD-25 during the cycle
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        seedSpend(u, 1_540_000, Instant.parse("2026-10-10T06:00:00Z"));
        String monthly = monthlyId(u);
        pick(u, UUID.fromString(monthly), "TAKE")
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.budgetPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.poolOverPaise").value(0))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.mood").value("NERVOUS"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.situation").value("APPLIED"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.applied.kind").value("TAKE"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.applied.takePaise").value(40_000));
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select coalesce(sum(amount_paise), 0) from goal_contributions where goal_id = ? and kind = 'SHORTFALL'")
            .param(UUID.fromString(monthly)).query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(-40_000L);
        undo(u, UUID.fromString(monthly))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.poolOverPaise").value(40_000))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.mood").value("FAINTED"))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.today.catchUp.situation").value("POOL_NEGATIVE"));
    }

    @Test
    void strictSavingsCannotBeTaken() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .content("{\"type\":\"MONTHLY\",\"mode\":\"STRICT\"}")).andExpect(status().isCreated());
        seedSpend(u, 1_540_000, Instant.parse("2026-10-10T06:00:00Z"));
        pick(u, UUID.fromString(monthlyId(u)), "TAKE").andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("STRICT_GOAL"));
    }

    @Test
    void takeNeedsAnEmptyPool() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        pick(u, UUID.fromString(monthlyId(u)), "TAKE").andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_BEHIND"));
    }

    @Test
    void kindsMustSuitTheGoal() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        pick(u, goa, "TAKE").andExpect(status().isBadRequest()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.fields[0].field").value("kind"));
        pick(u, UUID.fromString(monthlyId(u)), "TIGHTEN").andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/catch-up").with(auth(u)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .content("{\"kind\":\"BURN\"}")).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/catch-up").with(auth(u)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .content("{}")).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
    }

    @Test
    void tightenOnTheLastDayIsNotPossible() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2026-12-31T04:00:00Z"));
        pick(u, goa, "TIGHTEN").andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_POSSIBLE"));
    }

    @Test
    void aPausedGoalIsNotCaughtUp() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/pause").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        pick(u, goa, "TIGHTEN").andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_BEHIND"));
    }

    @Test
    void doubleTappedTightenRecordsOneAction() throws Exception { // Review focus
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
        try {
            CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);
            List<Future<Integer>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
                Callable<Integer> call = () -> {
                    start.await();
                    return pick(u, goa, "TIGHTEN").andReturn().getResponse().getStatus();
                };
                futures.add(pool.submit(call));
            }
            start.countDown();
            List<Integer> statuses = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Future<Integer> f : futures) {
                statuses.add(f.get());
            }
            assertThat(statuses).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(200, 409);
        } finally {
            pool.shutdownNow();
        }
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from catch_up_actions where goal_id = ? and undone_at is null").param(goa)
            .query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(1L);
        assertThat(jdbc.sql("select count(*) from goal_contributions where goal_id = ? and catch_up_id is not null").param(goa)
            .query(Long.class).single()).isEqualTo(1L);
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(jsonPath("$.tomorrowPaise").value(35_200));
    }

    @Test
    void undoAfterTheCycleClosedIsRefused() throws Exception { // Review focus
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        pick(u, goa, "TIGHTEN").andExpect(status().isOk());
        clock.set(Instant.parse("2027-01-02T04:00:00Z"));
        budgetToday(u).andExpect(status().isOk()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.budgetPaise").value(48_300)); // ₹15,000 ÷ 31, untouched
        undo(u, goa).andExpect(status().isConflict()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOTHING_TO_UNDO"));
    }

    private ResultActions pick(TestUser u, UUID goalId, String kind) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goalId + "/catch-up").with(auth(u))
            .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"kind\":\"" + kind + "\"}"));
    }

    private ResultActions undo(TestUser u, UUID goalId) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goalId + "/catch-up/undo").with(auth(u)));
    }

    private String monthlyId(TestUser u) throws Exception {
        return JsonPath.read(mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u))).andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString(), "$.monthly.id");
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Profile;

class DevGoalApiTest extends ApiTest {

    @Test
    void theFallBehindRouteDoesNotExistOutsideLocal() throws Exception {
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String goa = createGoal(u, "Goa trip", 3_000_000, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/dev/goals/" + goa + "/fall-behind").with(auth(u)))
            .andExpect(status().isNotFound())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_FOUND"));
    }

    @Test
    void theControllerIsBoundToTheLocalProfileOnly() {
        Profile profile = DevGoalController.class.getAnnotation(Profile.class);
        assertThat(profile).isNotNull();
        assertThat(profile.value()).containsExactly("local");
    }
}
```

```java
package in.gullak.security;

import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;

class GoalIsolationApiTest extends ApiTest {

    @Test
    void anotherUsersGoalCannotBeCaughtUpOrUndone() throws Exception { // API-X-01
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        TestUser b = exampleUser();
        UUID goa = goaBehind(a, "FLEXIBLE");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/catch-up").with(auth(b)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .content("{\"kind\":\"TIGHTEN\"}"))
            .andExpect(status().isNotFound()).andExpect(jsonPath("$.code").value("NOT_FOUND"));
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/catch-up").with(auth(a)).contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .content("{\"kind\":\"TIGHTEN\"}")).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + goa + "/catch-up/undo").with(auth(b))).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        budgetToday(a).andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp.situation").value("APPLIED"));
        budgetToday(b).andExpect(jsonPath("$.catchUp").doesNotExist());
    }

    @Test
    void aRandomGoalIdIsNotFound() throws Exception {
        TestUser a = exampleUser();
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + UUID.randomUUID() + "/catch-up/undo").with(auth(a))).andExpect(status().isNotFound());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/not-a-uuid/pause").with(auth(a))).andExpect(status().isBadRequest());
    }
}
```

`server/src/test/java/in/gullak/security/GoalLogRedactionApiTest.java`:

```java
package in.gullak.security;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.delete;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.patch;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import in.gullak.support.ApiTest;
import in.gullak.support.TestUser;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.springframework.boot.test.system.CapturedOutput;
import org.springframework.boot.test.system.OutputCaptureExtension;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;

@ExtendWith(OutputCaptureExtension.class)
class GoalLogRedactionApiTest extends ApiTest {
    private static final String NAME = "Quokka Getaway";
    private static final long TARGET = 4_321_000;

    @Test
    void goalTrafficLeavesNoNamesOrAmountsInTheLogs(CapturedOutput out) throws Exception { // LOG-05 over goals
        TestUser u = exampleUser();
        String id = createGoal(u, NAME, TARGET, "2027-03", "FLEXIBLE");
        String json = MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE;
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/check").with(auth(u)).contentType(json)
            .content("{\"targetPaise\":" + TARGET + ",\"deadline\":\"2027-03\"}")).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(get("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(patch("/api/v1/goals/" + id).with(auth(u)).contentType(json)
            .content("{\"name\":\"" + NAME + " 2\",\"targetPaise\":" + (TARGET + 1) + "}")).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + id + "/pause").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + id + "/resume").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals").with(auth(u)).contentType(json)
            .content("{\"type\":\"DEADLINE\",\"name\":\"" + NAME + "\",\"targetPaise\":99999900,\"deadline\":\"2026-11\",\"mode\":\"FLEXIBLE\"}"))
            .andExpect(status().isUnprocessableEntity());
        UUID behind = goaBehind(u, "FLEXIBLE");
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + behind + "/catch-up").with(auth(u)).contentType(json)
            .content("{\"kind\":\"LOWER\"}")).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/goals/" + behind + "/catch-up/undo").with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isOk());
        mvc.perform(delete("/api/v1/goals/" + id).with(auth(u))).andExpect(status().isNoContent());
        assertThat(out.getAll())
            .doesNotContain(NAME)
            .doesNotContain("Quokka")
            .doesNotContain("4321000")
            .doesNotContain("43,210")
            .doesNotContain("Goa trip")
            .doesNotContain("2820000");
    }
}
```
