```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.budget.TodayResponse;

/** The goal and today's budget after a pick or an undo, so Home updates at once (spec 2.2). */
public record CatchUpResult(GoalView goal, TodayResponse today) {}
```

In `GoalService`, the constructor takes the catch-up repository and the progress service (imports `in.gullak.budget.CatchUp`, `in.gullak.budget.CatchUpKind`, `in.gullak.budget.TodaySnapshot`, `in.gullak.common.db.Db`):

```java
    private final CatchUpActionRepository actions;
    private final GoalProgressService progress;

    GoalService(GoalRepository goals, GoalContributionRepository contributions, GoalBook book, ProfileService profiles,
                BudgetService budget, CycleService cycles, CatchUpPlanner planner, CatchUpActionRepository actions,
                GoalProgressService progress, Clock clock) {
        this.goals = goals;
        this.contributions = contributions;
        this.book = book;
        this.profiles = profiles;
        this.budget = budget;
        this.cycles = cycles;
        this.planner = planner;
        this.actions = actions;
        this.progress = progress;
        this.clock = clock;
    }
```

and gains these methods:


```java
    /** Spec 2.2: picking a catch-up option records it for this cycle; tighten changes tomorrow's budget at once. */
    @Transactional
    public CatchUpResult catchUp(UUID userId, UUID goalId, CatchUpKind kind) {
        Profile profile = lockedProfile(userId);
        TodaySnapshot s = budget.snapshot(userId); // opens the cycle before any goal row is loaded
        GoalBook.Entry e = book.entry(userId, goalId);
        GoalEntity g = e.goal();
        if (g.status() == GoalStatus.DONE) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.GOAL_DONE, "This goal is done, so it can't be changed");
        }
        if (g.status() == GoalStatus.PAUSED) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.NOT_BEHIND, "This goal is paused. Resume it first.");
        }
        boolean monthly = g.type() == GoalType.MONTHLY;
        if (monthly != (kind == CatchUpKind.TAKE)) {
            throw new ValidationException("kind", monthly
                ? "Monthly savings can only give back this cycle's savings"
                : "Taking from this cycle's savings is for monthly savings");
        }
        if (!CatchUpMath.allowed(g.mode(), g.type()).contains(kind)) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.STRICT_GOAL, "This goal is Strict, so only spending less is offered");
        }
        if (s.cycleId() != null && actions.findFirstByUserIdAndGoalIdAndCycleIdAndUndoneAtIsNull(userId, goalId, s.cycleId()).isPresent()) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.CATCH_UP_ACTIVE, "You already picked a catch-up plan this cycle. Undo it first.");
        }
        CatchUp card = planner.forGoal(userId, goalId, s).orElseThrow(() -> new ConflictException(ErrorCode.NOT_BEHIND, monthly
            ? "Your budget hasn't run out, so there's nothing to take back"
            : "This goal is on track, so there's nothing to catch up"));
        CatchUp.Option option = card.options().stream().filter(o -> o.kind() == kind).findFirst()
            .orElseThrow(() -> notOffered(kind, g));
        UUID actionId = UUID.randomUUID();
        switch (kind) {
            case TIGHTEN -> {
                saveAction(actionId, g, s, kind, card.gapPaise(), card.gapPaise(), option.perDayPaise(), option.days(),
                    null, null, null, null, s.date().plusDays(1));
                contributions.save(GoalContributionEntity.of(goalId, userId, s.cycleId(), card.gapPaise(), ContributionKind.EXTRA, actionId));
                progress.afterChange(userId, goalId);
            }
            case MOVE -> {
                saveAction(actionId, g, s, kind, card.gapPaise(), null, null, null,
                    g.deadlineMonth().atEndOfMonth(), option.newDeadline().atEndOfMonth(), null, null, s.date());
                g.moveDeadline(option.newDeadline());
                goals.save(g);
            }
            case LOWER -> {
                saveAction(actionId, g, s, kind, card.gapPaise(), null, null, null,
                    null, null, g.targetPaise(), option.newTargetPaise(), s.date());
                g.retarget(option.newTargetPaise());
                goals.save(g);
                progress.afterChange(userId, goalId);
            }
            case TAKE -> {
                List<CycleClose.Reduction> cuts = CycleClose.take(option.takePaise(), book.slices(userId, s.cycleId(), s.savingsPaise()));
                long taken = cuts.stream().mapToLong(CycleClose.Reduction::amountPaise).sum();
                saveAction(actionId, g, s, kind, card.gapPaise(), taken, null, null, null, null, null, null, s.date());
                for (CycleClose.Reduction cut : cuts) {
                    contributions.save(GoalContributionEntity.of(cut.goalId(), userId, s.cycleId(), -cut.amountPaise(),
                        ContributionKind.SHORTFALL, actionId));
                }
            }
        }
        log.info("{} user={} goal={} kind={}", Events.GOAL_CATCHUP, userId, goalId, kind);
        return new CatchUpResult(view(userId, goalId, profile), budget.today(userId));
    }

    /** Undo is possible until the cycle closes (spec 2.2); after payday nothing is live in the current cycle. */
    @Transactional
    public CatchUpResult undo(UUID userId, UUID goalId) {
        Profile profile = lockedProfile(userId);
        TodaySnapshot s = budget.snapshot(userId);
        GoalBook.Entry e = book.entry(userId, goalId);
        if (e.goal().status() == GoalStatus.DONE) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.GOAL_DONE, "This goal is done, so it can't be changed");
        }
        CatchUpActionEntity a = s.cycleId() == null ? null
            : actions.findFirstByUserIdAndGoalIdAndCycleIdAndUndoneAtIsNull(userId, goalId, s.cycleId()).orElse(null);
        if (a == null) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.NOTHING_TO_UNDO, "There's nothing to undo");
        }
        UUID actionId = a.getId();
        CatchUpKind kind = a.kind();
        LocalDate previousDeadline = a.previousDeadline();
        Long previousTarget = a.previousTargetPaise();
        if (actions.markUndone(actionId, userId, Db.ts(clock.instant())) == 0) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.NOTHING_TO_UNDO, "There's nothing to undo");
        }
        switch (kind) {
            case TIGHTEN, TAKE -> contributions.deleteForCatchUp(userId, actionId);
            case MOVE -> {
                GoalEntity g = book.entry(userId, goalId).goal();
                g.moveDeadline(YearMonth.from(previousDeadline));
                goals.save(g);
            }
            case LOWER -> {
                GoalEntity g = book.entry(userId, goalId).goal();
                g.retarget(previousTarget);
                goals.save(g);
            }
        }
        log.info("{} user={} goal={} kind={}", Events.GOAL_CATCHUP_UNDONE, userId, goalId, kind);
        return new CatchUpResult(view(userId, goalId, profile), budget.today(userId));
    }

    /** Test data for E2E-06, reachable only through the local-profile {@link DevGoalController}. */
    @Transactional
    public GoalView devFallBehind(UUID userId, UUID goalId) {
        Profile profile = lockedProfile(userId);
        budget.currentCycle(userId);
        GoalEntity g = book.entry(userId, goalId).goal();
        if (g.type() != GoalType.DEADLINE) {
            throw new ValidationException("id", "Only deadline goals can fall behind");
        }
        g.backdate(g.createdOn().minusMonths(2));
        goals.save(g);
        return view(userId, goalId, profile);
    }

    private void saveAction(UUID actionId, GoalEntity g, TodaySnapshot s, CatchUpKind kind, long gap, Long amount, Long perDay,
                        Integer days, LocalDate previousDeadline, LocalDate newDeadline, Long previousTarget, Long newTarget,
                        LocalDate effectiveFrom) {
        int inserted = actions.insertIfNoneActive(actionId, g.getId(), g.userId(), s.cycleId(), kind.name(), gap, amount,
            perDay, days, previousDeadline, newDeadline, previousTarget, newTarget, effectiveFrom);
        if (inserted == 0) {
            throw new ConflictException(ErrorCode.CATCH_UP_ACTIVE, "You already picked a catch-up plan this cycle. Undo it first.");
        }
    }

    private static GullakException notOffered(CatchUpKind kind, GoalEntity g) {
        return switch (kind) {
            case TIGHTEN -> new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE,
                "Spending less can't close the gap this cycle. Move the deadline or lower the target instead.");
            case MOVE -> new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE,
                "Nothing is set aside each month, so a later deadline won't help");
            case LOWER -> new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE, "The target can't be lowered right now");
            case TAKE -> g.mode() == GoalMode.STRICT
                ? new ConflictException(ErrorCode.STRICT_GOAL, "Your savings are Strict, so nothing can be taken back")
                : new UnprocessableException(ErrorCode.NOT_POSSIBLE, "Nothing in this cycle's savings can be taken back");
        };
    }
```

```java
package in.gullak.goals;

import in.gullak.common.web.CurrentUser;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Profile;
import org.springframework.security.core.annotation.AuthenticationPrincipal;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

/**
 * Local development only: makes one of the caller's goals fall two paydays behind so E2E-06 can exercise the
 * catch-up plan. Never registered under test or prod (DevGoalApiTest pins the profile).
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/dev/goals")
@Profile("local")
class DevGoalController {
    private final GoalService goals;

    DevGoalController(GoalService goals) {
        this.goals = goals;
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/fall-behind")
    GoalView fallBehind(@AuthenticationPrincipal Jwt jwt, @PathVariable UUID id) {
        return goals.devFallBehind(CurrentUser.id(jwt), id);
    }
}
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { act, renderHook } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import React from "react";
import { ApiError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { useFinishSetup } from "../hooks";

jest.mock("@/features/profile/api", () => ({
  profileApi: { get: jest.fn(), put: jest.fn() },
  fixedCostApi: { list: jest.fn(), add: jest.fn(), patch: jest.fn(), remove: jest.fn() },
}));
jest.mock("@/features/budget/api", () => ({ budgetApi: { getToday: jest.fn(), setFirstCycle: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/features/goals/api", () => ({ goalsApi: { create: jest.fn() } }));
import { budgetApi } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { goalsApi } from "@/features/goals/api";
import { profileApi } from "@/features/profile/api";

const put = profileApi.put as jest.Mock;
const create = goalsApi.create as jest.Mock;
const setFirstCycle = budgetApi.setFirstCycle as jest.Mock;

const request = {
  incomePaise: 5000000,
  savingsPaise: 2000000,
  paydayRule: "FIXED_DAY" as const,
  paydayDay: 1,
  personality: "CHILL" as const,
  morningTime: "08:00",
  nightTime: "22:00",
  timezone: "Asia/Kolkata" as const,
};

function wrapper({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <QueryClientProvider client={new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } })}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>;
}

beforeEach(() => jest.clearAllMocks());

describe("finishing setup (APP-34)", () => {
  it("saves the profile, creates monthly savings with the chosen mode, then pins the first cycle", async () => {
    const calls: string[] = [];
    put.mockImplementation(() => {
      calls.push("profile");
      return Promise.resolve(request);
    });
    create.mockImplementation(() => {
      calls.push("goal");
      return Promise.resolve({ goal: { id: "m", type: "MONTHLY", mode: "STRICT" } });
    });
    setFirstCycle.mockImplementation(() => {
      calls.push("cycle");
      return Promise.resolve({ date: "2026-10-23" });
    });
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useFinishSetup(), { wrapper });
    await act(() => result.current({ ...request, moneyLeftPaise: 600000, mode: "STRICT" }));
    expect(put).toHaveBeenCalledWith(request);
    expect(create).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ type: "MONTHLY", mode: "STRICT" });
    expect(setFirstCycle).toHaveBeenCalledWith(600000);
    expect(calls).toEqual(["profile", "goal", "cycle"]);
  });

  it("a failed goal save rejects so the Start button can be pressed again", async () => {
    put.mockResolvedValue(request);
    create.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError("INTERNAL", 500, "x"));
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useFinishSetup(), { wrapper });
    await expect(result.current({ ...request, moneyLeftPaise: null, mode: "FLEXIBLE" })).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(setFirstCycle).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

Append to `app/src/lib/dates.ts`:

```ts
const shortMonths = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
const longMonths = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"];

/** "2027-04" → "Apr 2027" */
export function formatMonth(month: string): string {
  return `${shortMonths[Number(month.slice(5, 7)) - 1] ?? ""} ${month.slice(0, 4)}`;
}

/** "2026-11" → "November 2026" */
export function formatMonthLong(month: string): string {
  return `${longMonths[Number(month.slice(5, 7)) - 1] ?? ""} ${month.slice(0, 4)}`;
}

/** "2026-10-23" → "2026-10" */
export function monthOf(date: string): string {
  return date.slice(0, 7);
}

/** "2026-11" + 9 → "2027-08" */
export function addMonths(month: string, n: number): string {
  const index = Number(month.slice(0, 4)) * 12 + (Number(month.slice(5, 7)) - 1) + n;
  return `${Math.floor(index / 12)}-${String((index % 12) + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}
```

Append to `app/src/lib/cycle.ts`:

```ts
/** The first payday strictly after a date (the server's PaydayCalendar.next). */
export function nextPayday(after: string, rule: PaydayRule): string {
  const { y, m } = ym(after);
  const thisMonth = payday(`${y}-${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}`, rule);
  if (thisMonth > after) return thisMonth;
  const n = shiftMonth(y, m, 1);
  return payday(`${n.y}-${String(n.m).padStart(2, "0")}`, rule);
}
```

`app/src/features/profile/payday.ts`:

```ts
import type { PaydayRule } from "@/lib/cycle";
import type { Profile } from "./api";

/** The profile's payday in the form the cycle helpers take. */
export function paydayRuleOf(profile: Pick<Profile, "paydayRule" | "paydayDay">): PaydayRule {
  return profile.paydayRule === "FIXED_DAY" ? { rule: "FIXED_DAY", day: profile.paydayDay ?? 1 } : { rule: "LAST_WORKING_DAY" };
}
```

`app/src/features/budget/api.ts` (keep anything Plans 3 and 4 added to `Today` and `budgetApi`):

```ts
import { api } from "@/lib/client";
import type { ServerMood } from "@/features/gullak/moods";

export type CatchUpKind = "TIGHTEN" | "MOVE" | "LOWER" | "TAKE";
export type CatchUpSituation = "BEHIND" | "POOL_NEGATIVE" | "APPLIED";

/** One choice with the numbers its label shows; only the fields for its kind are present. */
export type CatchUpOption = {
  kind: CatchUpKind;
  perDayPaise?: number;
  days?: number;
  tomorrowPaise?: number;
  newDeadline?: string;
  extraPaydays?: number;
  newTargetPaise?: number;
  takePaise?: number;
};

export type CatchUpApplied = Omit<CatchUpOption, "tomorrowPaise"> & { actionId: string; canUndo: boolean };

export type CatchUp = {
  situation: CatchUpSituation;
  goalId: string;
  goalName: string;
  strict: boolean;
  gapPaise: number;
  options: CatchUpOption[];
  applied?: CatchUpApplied;
};

export type Today = {
  date: string;
  budgetPaise: number;
  spentPaise: number;
  leftPaise: number;
  overPaise: number;
  poolOverPaise: number;
  mood: ServerMood;
  line: string;
  daysLeft: number;
  cycleStart: string;
  cycleEnd: string;
  /** Absent on the cycle's last day. */
  tomorrowPaise?: number;
  /** Absent when there is nothing to catch up. */
  catchUp?: CatchUp;
};

export const budgetApi = {
  getToday: () => api.get<Today>("/budget/today"),
  setFirstCycle: (moneyLeftPaise: number) => api.put<Today>("/budget/first-cycle", { moneyLeftPaise }),
};
```

```ts
import type { CatchUp, CatchUpKind, Today } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { api } from "@/lib/client";

export type GoalMode = "FLEXIBLE" | "STRICT";
export type GoalType = "MONTHLY" | "DEADLINE";
export type GoalStatus = "ACTIVE" | "PAUSED" | "DONE";
export type Verdict = "DOABLE" | "TIGHT" | "NOT_POSSIBLE";
export type VerdictReason = "DEADLINE_PASSED" | "OVER_FREE_SAVINGS";

export type Milestone = { percent: number; reachedAt: string };

export type Goal = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: GoalType;
  targetPaise?: number;
  /** "2027-03" */
  deadline?: string;
  mode: GoalMode;
  status: GoalStatus;
  savedPaise: number;
  expectedPaise: number;
  behindPaise: number;
  percent: number;
  expectedPercent: number;
  contributionPaise: number;
  paydaysTotal: number;
  paydaysElapsed: number;
  paydaysLeft: number;
  lastMilestone?: Milestone;
  catchingUp: boolean;
};

export type SplitRow = { goalId: string; name: string; amountPaise: number };

export type MonthlySummary = {
  id: string;
  mode: GoalMode;
  savingsPaise: number;
  cycleStart: string;
  split: SplitRow[];
  freeThisCyclePaise: number;
  committedPaise: number;
  freeNextPaise: number;
};

export type GoalsList = { monthly: MonthlySummary; goals: Goal[] };

export type CycleContribution = {
  cycleId: string;
  cycleStart: string;
  cycleEnd: string;
  plannedPaise: number;
  extraPaise: number;
  shortfallPaise: number;
  totalPaise: number;
};

export type Transfer = { date: string; amountPaise: number };

export type GoalDetail = { goal: Goal; cycles: CycleContribution[]; transfers: Transfer[]; milestones: Milestone[]; catchUp?: CatchUp };

export type CheckInput = { targetPaise: number; deadline: string; goalId?: string };

export type Check = {
  verdict: Verdict;
  reason?: VerdictReason;
  contributionPaise: number;
  freePaise: number;
  freeAfterPaise: number;
  maxTargetPaise: number;
  paydays: number;
  firstPayday?: string;
  lastPayday?: string;
  nextPayday: string;
};

export type NewGoal =
  | { type: "DEADLINE"; name: string; targetPaise: number; deadline: string; mode: GoalMode }
  | { type: "MONTHLY"; mode: GoalMode };

export type GoalPatch = { name?: string; targetPaise?: number; deadline?: string; mode?: GoalMode };

export type CatchUpResult = { goal: Goal; today: Today };

export const goalsApi = {
  list: () => api.get<GoalsList>("/goals"),
  get: (id: string) => api.get<GoalDetail>(`/goals/${id}`),
  check: (input: CheckInput) => api.post<Check>("/goals/check", input),
  create: (body: NewGoal) => api.post<{ goal: Goal; check?: Check }>("/goals", body),
  patch: (id: string, body: GoalPatch) => api.patch<Goal>(`/goals/${id}`, body),
  pause: (id: string) => api.post<Goal>(`/goals/${id}/pause`),
  resume: (id: string) => api.post<Goal>(`/goals/${id}/resume`),
  remove: (id: string) => api.del<void>(`/goals/${id}`),
  catchUp: (id: string, kind: CatchUpKind) => api.post<CatchUpResult>(`/goals/${id}/catch-up`, { kind }),
  undoCatchUp: (id: string) => api.post<CatchUpResult>(`/goals/${id}/catch-up/undo`),
};
```

`app/src/features/goals/keys.ts` (no runtime imports, so profile hooks can use it without a cycle):

```ts
import type { CheckInput } from "./api";

export const goalKeys = {
  all: ["goals"] as const,
  list: ["goals", "list"] as const,
  detail: (id: string) => ["goals", "detail", id] as const,
  check: (input: CheckInput) => ["goals", "check", input.targetPaise, input.deadline, input.goalId ?? null] as const,
};
```

`app/src/features/goals/hooks.ts`:

```ts
import { keepPreviousData, useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { CatchUpKind } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { todayKey } from "@/features/budget/hooks";
import { profileApi, type Profile } from "@/features/profile/api";
import { profileKeys } from "@/features/profile/hooks";
import { goalsApi, type CatchUpResult, type CheckInput, type GoalMode, type GoalPatch, type NewGoal } from "./api";
import { goalKeys } from "./keys";

export function useGoals() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: goalKeys.list, queryFn: goalsApi.list });
}

export function useGoal(id: string) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: goalKeys.detail(id), queryFn: () => goalsApi.get(id) });
}

/** The reality check for a form; the last answer stays on screen while the next one loads. */
export function useGoalCheck(input: CheckInput, enabled: boolean) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: goalKeys.check(input),
    queryFn: () => goalsApi.check(input),
    enabled,
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });
}

function useRefresh() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return async () => {
    await Promise.all([qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: goalKeys.all }), qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: todayKey })]);
  };
}

export function useCreateGoal() {
  const refresh = useRefresh();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: (body: NewGoal) => goalsApi.create(body), onSuccess: refresh });
}

export function usePatchGoal() {
  const refresh = useRefresh();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: ({ id, body }: { id: string; body: GoalPatch }) => goalsApi.patch(id, body), onSuccess: refresh });
}

export function usePauseGoal() {
  const refresh = useRefresh();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: (id: string) => goalsApi.pause(id), onSuccess: refresh });
}

export function useResumeGoal() {
  const refresh = useRefresh();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: (id: string) => goalsApi.resume(id), onSuccess: refresh });
}

export function useDeleteGoal() {
  const refresh = useRefresh();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: (id: string) => goalsApi.remove(id), onSuccess: refresh });
}

/** Home shows the server's new day straight away (spec 2.2: the budget updates at once). */
function useApplyCatchUpResult() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return (result: CatchUpResult) => {
    qc.setQueryData(todayKey, result.today);
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: goalKeys.all });
  };
}

export function useCatchUp() {
  const apply = useApplyCatchUpResult();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ goalId, kind }: { goalId: string; kind: CatchUpKind }) => goalsApi.catchUp(goalId, kind),
    onSuccess: apply,
  });
}

export function useUndoCatchUp() {
  const apply = useApplyCatchUpResult();
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: (goalId: string) => goalsApi.undoCatchUp(goalId), onSuccess: apply });
}

/** The Goals tab's savings sheet: the amount lives on the profile, the mode on monthly savings. */
export function useSaveSavings() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ profile, savingsPaise, monthlyId, mode }: { profile: Profile; savingsPaise: number; monthlyId: string; mode: GoalMode | null }) => {
      const saved = await profileApi.put({ ...profile, savingsPaise });
      if (mode !== null) await goalsApi.patch(monthlyId, { mode });
      return saved;
    },
    onSuccess: (saved) => {
      qc.setQueryData(profileKeys.profile, saved);
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: goalKeys.all });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: todayKey });
    },
  });
}
```

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";

import type { GoalMode } from "../api";

/** "If I overspend" from the Setup and New goal mockups: two radio cards side by side. */
export function ModePicker({ value, onChange, variant }: { value: GoalMode; onChange: (mode: GoalMode) => void; variant: "savings" | "goal" }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
      <Text variant="label">If I overspend</Text>
      <View accessibilityRole="radiogroup" accessibilityLabel="If I overspend" style={{ flexDirection: "row", gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <RadioCard title="Flexible" description="Warn me and offer a catch-up plan" checked={value === "FLEXIBLE"} onPress={() => onChange("FLEXIBLE")} testID="mode-flexible" />
        </View>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <RadioCard
            title="Strict"
            description={variant === "savings" ? "Shrink my daily budget so savings never slip" : "Shrink my daily budget so it never slips"}
            checked={value === "STRICT"}
            onPress={() => onChange("STRICT")}
            testID="mode-strict"
          />
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
export type SetupDraft = {
  incomePaise: number;
  /** null until a payday option is picked; "Another date" without a day is `{ rule: "FIXED_DAY", day: null }`. */
```

In `app/src/features/profile/screens/savings-screen.tsx` import `ModePicker` from `@/features/goals/components/mode-picker` and replace the `SavingsScreen` function:

```tsx
export function SavingsScreen({ today, fixedCosts, onContinue, onBack }: { today: string; fixedCosts: FixedCost[]; onContinue: () => void; onBack: () => void }) {
  const { draft, set } = useSetup();
  const [deferred, setDeferred] = useState(false);
  const fixedPaise = fixedCosts.reduce((s, c) => s + c.amountPaise, 0);
  const payday = paydayIsComplete(draft.payday) ? draft.payday : { rule: "FIXED_DAY" as const, day: 1 };
  const next = perDayPreview({ freePaise: draft.incomePaise - fixedPaise - draft.savingsPaise, today, payday });
  const check = realityCheck({ incomePaise: draft.incomePaise, fixedPaise, savingsPaise: draft.savingsPaise, cycleDays: next.cycleDays });
  const maxSavings = Math.max(0, draft.incomePaise - fixedPaise);
  const suggested = wholeRupees(Math.max(0, Math.floor((draft.incomePaise - fixedPaise - MIN_PER_DAY_PAISE * next.cycleDays) / 100000) * 100000));
  const preview = firstCyclePreview({ monthlySpendablePaise: check.freePaise, today, payday, ...(draft.moneyLeftPaise !== null ? { moneyLeftPaise: draft.moneyLeftPaise } : {}) });
  const blocked = check.verdict === "notPossible";

  return (
    <Screen
      testID="setup-savings"
      footer={
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
          <Button title={blocked ? "Lower your savings to continue" : "Continue"} onPress={onContinue} disabled={blocked} />
          {draft.savingsPaise > 0 ? (
            <Button
              title="Set savings later"
              variant="ghost"
              onPress={() => {
                set({ savingsPaise: 0 });
                setDeferred(true);
              }}
            />
          ) : null}
        </View>
      }
    >
      <StepHeader step={3} total={4} name="Savings" onBack={onBack} />
      <Text variant="heading">Monthly savings</Text>
      <Text variant="bodyMuted">This comes out first. Your daily budget is what's left.</Text>
      <SavingsStepper valuePaise={draft.savingsPaise} max={maxSavings} onChange={(savingsPaise) => set({ savingsPaise })} />
      <RealityCheck verdict={check.verdict} perDayPaise={check.perDayPaise} essentialsLeftPaise={draft.incomePaise - fixedPaise} suggestedSavingsPaise={suggested} />
      {deferred && draft.savingsPaise === 0 ? <Text variant="label" color={colors.warnInk}>No savings this month. Set a target later from Me.</Text> : null}
      <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <Row label="Salary" value={formatRupees(draft.incomePaise)} />
        <Row label={essentialsLabel(fixedCosts)} value={`−${formatRupees(fixedPaise)}`} />
        <Row label="Savings" value={`−${formatRupees(draft.savingsPaise)}`} />
        <Row label="Free to spend" value={formatRupees(Math.max(0, check.freePaise))} strong />
        <Row label={`Per day in ${next.cycleName} (${next.cycleDays} days)`} value={formatRupees(check.perDayPaise)} strong />
      </View>
      <ModePicker variant="savings" value={draft.mode} onChange={(mode) => set({ mode })} />
      {!blocked && preview.proRated ? <MoneyLeftCard preview={preview} edited={draft.moneyLeftPaise !== null} onChange={(moneyLeftPaise) => set({ moneyLeftPaise })} /> : null}
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`useFinishSetup` in `app/src/features/profile/hooks.ts` (imports `goalsApi` from `@/features/goals/api`, `goalKeys` from `@/features/goals/keys`, `todayKey` from `@/features/budget/hooks`):

```ts
/** PUT the profile, create monthly savings with the chosen mode, then pin the first cycle's pool if the user changed it. */
export function useFinishSetup() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useCallback(
    async ({ moneyLeftPaise, mode, ...profile }: FinishSetupInput) => {
      const saved = await profileApi.put(profile);
      qc.setQueryData(profileKeys.profile, saved);
      await goalsApi.create({ type: "MONTHLY", mode });
      if (moneyLeftPaise !== null) {
        const today = await budgetApi.setFirstCycle(moneyLeftPaise);
        qc.setQueryData(todayKey, today);
      } else {
        await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: todayKey });
      }
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: goalKeys.all });
    },
    [qc],
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import React from "react";
import { ApiError } from "@/lib/api-errors";

import { GoalForm, type GoalFormValues } from "./goal-form";

jest.mock("@/features/goals/api", () => ({ goalsApi: { check: jest.fn() } }));
import { goalsApi } from "@/features/goals/api";

const check = goalsApi.check as jest.Mock;

const doable: Check = {
  verdict: "DOABLE",
  contributionPaise: 600000,
  freePaise: 970000,
  freeAfterPaise: 370000,
  maxTargetPaise: 9700000,
  paydays: 10,
  firstPayday: "2026-11-01",
  lastPayday: "2027-08-01",
  nextPayday: "2026-11-01",
};

const notPossible: Check = {
  verdict: "NOT_POSSIBLE",
  reason: "OVER_FREE_SAVINGS",
  contributionPaise: 2000000,
  freePaise: 970000,
  freeAfterPaise: -1030000,
  maxTargetPaise: 2910000,
  paydays: 3,
  firstPayday: "2026-11-01",
  lastPayday: "2027-01-01",

    </QueryClientProvider>,

}

beforeEach(() => {

  check.mockImplementation((input: { deadline: string }) => Promise.resolve(input.deadline === "2027-01" ? notPossible : doable));
```

`app/src/components/ui/stepper.tsx` (Plan 2's file with two optional props; defaults keep Plan 2's labels):

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { Pressable, View } from "react-native";
import { colors, radius, spacing, touchTarget } from "@/theme";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import { Icon } from "./icon";
import { Text } from "./text";

export type StepperProps = {
  label: string;
  valuePaise: number;
  stepPaise: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  onChange: (paise: number) => void;
  /** Tapping the number; Task 9 of Plan 2 uses it to open a typed input. */
  onPressValue?: () => void;
  /** Labels of the − and + buttons; default "Save ₹1,000 less" and "Save ₹1,000 more". */
  lessLabel?: string;
  moreLabel?: string;
  testID?: string;
};

export function Stepper({ label, valuePaise, stepPaise, min, max, onChange, onPressValue, lessLabel, moreLabel, testID }: StepperProps) {
  const step = formatRupees(stepPaise);
  const canLess = valuePaise - stepPaise >= min;
  const canMore = valuePaise + stepPaise <= max;
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }} testID={testID}>
      <Text variant="label">{label}</Text>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: spacing.md }}>
        <RoundButton label={lessLabel ?? `Save ${step} less`} icon="minus" disabled={!canLess} onPress={() => onChange(Math.max(min, valuePaise - stepPaise))} />
        <Pressable
          accessibilityRole={onPressValue ? "button" : "text"}
          accessibilityLabel={onPressValue ? `${label}, ${formatRupees(valuePaise)}, tap to type an amount` : undefined}
          onPress={onPressValue}
          disabled={!onPressValue}
          style={{ minHeight: touchTarget, justifyContent: "center" }}
        >
          <Text variant="hero" align="center" style={{ fontVariant: ["tabular-nums"] }}>
            {formatRupees(valuePaise)}
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
        <RoundButton label={moreLabel ?? `Save ${step} more`} icon="plus" disabled={!canMore} onPress={() => onChange(Math.min(max, valuePaise + stepPaise))} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

function RoundButton({ label, icon, disabled, onPress }: { label: string; icon: "plus" | "minus"; disabled: boolean; onPress: () => void }) {
  return (
    <Pressable
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={label}
      accessibilityState={{ disabled }}
      disabled={disabled}
      onPress={onPress}
      hitSlop={6}
      style={({ pressed }) => ({
        width: 56,
        height: 56,
        borderRadius: radius.full,
        borderWidth: 1,
        borderColor: colors.control,
        backgroundColor: colors.surface,
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "center",
        opacity: disabled ? 0.4 : pressed ? 0.8 : 1,
      })}
    >
      <Icon name={icon} size={24} />
    </Pressable>
  );
}
```

`app/src/components/ui/chip.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { Pressable } from "react-native";
import { colors, radius, spacing, touchTarget } from "@/theme";
import { Text } from "./text";

export type ChipProps = {
  label: string;
  selected?: boolean;
  onPress: () => void;
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
  /** When the visible label needs context ("Nov" → "Nov 2026"). */
  accessibilityLabel?: string;
  testID?: string;
};

/** A selectable pill. Selected: 2px ink border (design.md). */
export function Chip({ label, selected = false, onPress, icon, accessibilityLabel, testID }: ChipProps) {
  return (
    <Pressable
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={accessibilityLabel ?? label}
      accessibilityState={{ selected }}
      onPress={onPress}
      testID={testID}
      style={({ pressed }) => ({
        minHeight: touchTarget,
        paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,
        borderRadius: radius.full,
        borderWidth: selected ? 2 : 1,
        borderColor: selected ? colors.ink : colors.control,
        backgroundColor: selected ? colors.surface : "transparent",
        flexDirection: "row",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: spacing.sm,
        opacity: pressed ? 0.85 : 1,
      })}
    >
      {icon ?? null}
      <Text variant={selected ? "bodyStrong" : "body"}>{label}</Text>
    </Pressable>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/components/month-picker.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { ScrollView, View } from "react-native";
import { Chip } from "@/components/ui/chip";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { addMonths, formatMonth } from "@/lib/dates";
import { spacing } from "@/theme";

/** The furthest deadline the server accepts: 120 months after this one. */
export const MAX_MONTHS_AHEAD = 120;

/**
 * Year chips over a grid of month chips. The current month stays visible even when its payday has passed, so picking it
 * explains why it is blocked (the New goal mockup's "That month has passed").
 */
export function MonthPicker({ value, currentMonth, onChange }: { value: string; currentMonth: string; onChange: (month: string) => void }) {
  const lastMonth = addMonths(currentMonth, MAX_MONTHS_AHEAD);
  const firstYear = Number(currentMonth.slice(0, 4));
  const lastYear = Number(lastMonth.slice(0, 4));
  const [year, setYear] = useState(Number(value.slice(0, 4)));
  const years = Array.from({ length: lastYear - firstYear + 1 }, (_, i) => firstYear + i);
  const months = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => `${year}-${String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`).filter((m) => m >= currentMonth && m <= lastMonth);
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
      <Text variant="label">Reach it by</Text>
      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
        {years.map((y) => (
          <Chip key={y} label={String(y)} accessibilityLabel={`Year ${y}`} selected={y === year} onPress={() => setYear(y)} />
        ))}
      </ScrollView>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: spacing.sm }}>
        {months.map((m) => {
          const label = formatMonth(m);
          return <Chip key={m} label={label.slice(0, 3)} accessibilityLabel={label} selected={m === value} onPress={() => onChange(m)} />;
        })}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/components/target-stepper.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Field } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Stepper } from "@/components/ui/stepper";
import { MAX_AMOUNT_PAISE, parseRupeesToPaise } from "@/lib/money";
import { spacing } from "@/theme";

const STEP_PAISE = 500000; // ₹5,000, as in the New goal mockup

/** Spec 3.2 #3: the stepper also takes a typed amount; tapping the number opens the keyboard. */
export function TargetStepper({ valuePaise, onChange }: { valuePaise: number; onChange: (paise: number) => void }) {
  const [typing, setTyping] = useState<string | null>(null);
  if (typing !== null) {
    return (
      <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <Field label="Target" prefix="₹" large value={typing} onChangeText={(t) => setTyping(t.replace(/[^\d,]/g, ""))} keyboardType="number-pad" inputMode="numeric" autoFocus />
        <Button
          title="Done"
          onPress={() => {
            const typed = parseRupeesToPaise(typing);
            onChange(typed > 0 ? Math.min(MAX_AMOUNT_PAISE, typed) : valuePaise);
            setTyping(null);
          }}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }
  return (
    <Stepper
      label="Target"
      valuePaise={valuePaise}
      stepPaise={STEP_PAISE}
      min={STEP_PAISE}
      max={MAX_AMOUNT_PAISE}
      onChange={onChange}
      onPressValue={() => setTyping(String(valuePaise / 100))}
      lessLabel="₹5,000 less"
      moreLabel="₹5,000 more"
    />
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/components/verdict-card.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { Icon, type IconName } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { formatDayLong, formatMonthLong } from "@/lib/dates";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import { colors, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";
import type { Check } from "../api";

export type Look = { icon: IconName; title: string; line: string; ink: string; bg: string };

/** The New goal mockup's reality-check copy, with design.md's full-stop rule applied. */
export function verdictCopy(check: Check): Look {
  const monthly = formatRupees(check.contributionPaise);
  const free = formatRupees(Math.max(0, check.freePaise));
  switch (check.verdict) {
    case "DOABLE":
      return { icon: "check", title: "Doable", line: `${monthly} a month from your free ${free}. ${formatRupees(check.freeAfterPaise)} stays free.`, ink: colors.goodDeep, bg: colors.goodTint };
    case "TIGHT":
      return { icon: "alert", title: "Possible, but tight", line: `${monthly} a month leaves ${formatRupees(check.freeAfterPaise)} of free savings`, ink: colors.warnInk, bg: colors.warnTint };
    case "NOT_POSSIBLE":
      return { icon: "x", title: "Not possible yet", line: `${monthly} a month is more than your free ${free}. Lower the target or pick a later date.`, ink: colors.bad, bg: colors.badTint };
  }
}

export function passedCopy(nextPayday: string): Look {
  return {
    icon: "alert",
    title: "That month has passed",
    line: `Your next payday is ${formatDayLong(nextPayday)}. Pick ${formatMonthLong(nextPayday.slice(0, 7))} or later.`,
    ink: colors.bad,
    bg: colors.badTint,
  };
}

/** "10 paydays from Sun, 1 Nov 2026 to Sun, 1 Aug 2027" or "1 payday: Sun, 1 Nov 2026". */
export function paydaysLine(check: Check): string {
  if (check.paydays === 1 && check.firstPayday) return `1 payday: ${formatDayLong(check.firstPayday)}`;
  if (check.firstPayday && check.lastPayday) return `${check.paydays} paydays from ${formatDayLong(check.firstPayday)} to ${formatDayLong(check.lastPayday)}`;
  return `Pick ${formatMonthLong(check.nextPayday.slice(0, 7))} or later`;
}

export function VerdictCard({ look }: { look: Look }) {
  return (
    <View accessibilityLiveRegion="polite" accessibilityLabel={`Reality check: ${look.title}`} style={{ gap: spacing.sm, padding: spacing.md, borderRadius: radius.lg, borderCurve: "continuous", backgroundColor: look.bg }}>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <Icon name={look.icon} size={20} color={look.ink} />
        <Text variant="bodyStrong" color={look.ink}>
          {look.title}
        </Text>
      </View>
      <Text variant="label">{look.line}</Text>
    </View>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/components/goal-form.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Pressable, View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/error-state";
import { Field } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Screen } from "@/components/ui/screen";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { describeError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { nextPayday, type PaydayRule } from "@/lib/cycle";
import { formatMonthLong, monthOf } from "@/lib/dates";
import { messageFor } from "@/lib/errors";
import { colors, radius, spacing, touchTarget } from "@/theme";
import type { GoalMode } from "../api";
import { useGoalCheck } from "../hooks";
import { ModePicker } from "./mode-picker";
import { MonthPicker } from "./month-picker";
import { TargetStepper } from "./target-stepper";
import { VerdictCard, passedCopy, paydaysLine, verdictCopy } from "./verdict-card";

export type GoalFormValues = { name: string; targetPaise: number; deadline: string; mode: GoalMode };

type Props = {
  mode: "new" | "edit";
  title: string;
  initial: GoalFormValues;
  submitLabel: string;
  today: string;
  payday: PaydayRule;
  /** Editing: the server leaves this goal's own contribution out of the check. */
  goalId?: string;
  onSubmit: (values: GoalFormValues) => Promise<void>;
  onClose: () => void;
};

/** The New goal mockup, also used to edit a goal. The sheet closes only after the save succeeds. */
export function GoalForm({ mode, title, initial, submitLabel, today, payday, goalId, onSubmit, onClose }: Props) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState(initial);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const next = nextPayday(today, payday);
  const passed = values.deadline < monthOf(next);
  const named = values.name.trim() !== "";
  const check = useGoalCheck({ targetPaise: values.targetPaise, deadline: values.deadline, ...(goalId ? { goalId } : {}) }, !passed);
  const verdict = passed ? undefined : check.data;
  const blockedText = !named
    ? mode === "new" ? "Name your goal to start" : "Name your goal to save"
    : passed
      ? "Pick a later month"
      : verdict?.verdict === "NOT_POSSIBLE"
        ? "Lower the target or pick a later date"
        : null;
  const ready = blockedText === null && verdict !== undefined && !check.isError;

  async function submit() {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await onSubmit({ ...values, name: values.name.trim() });
    } catch (e) {
      setError(describeError(e, messageFor));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Screen
      testID="goal-form"
      footer={
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.xs }}>
          {error ? (
            <Text variant="label" color={colors.bad} accessibilityRole="alert">
              {error}
            </Text>
          ) : null}
          <Button title={blockedText ?? submitLabel} onPress={() => void submit()} disabled={!ready} loading={busy} />
        </View>
      }
    >
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: spacing.cm, minHeight: 48 }}>
        <Pressable
          accessibilityRole="button"
          accessibilityLabel="Back to goals"
          onPress={onClose}
          style={{ width: 48, height: 48, minWidth: touchTarget, borderRadius: radius.full, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.control, backgroundColor: colors.surface, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}
        >
          <Icon name="left" size={20} />
        </Pressable>
        <Text variant="title" accessibilityRole="header">
          {title}
        </Text>
      </View>
      <Field label="Goal name" placeholder="e.g. Bike down payment" maxLength={40} value={values.name} onChangeText={(name) => setValues((v) => ({ ...v, name }))} />
      <TargetStepper valuePaise={values.targetPaise} onChange={(targetPaise) => setValues((v) => ({ ...v, targetPaise }))} />
      <View style={{ gap: spacing.xs }}>
        <MonthPicker value={values.deadline} currentMonth={monthOf(today)} onChange={(deadline) => setValues((v) => ({ ...v, deadline }))} />
        <Text variant="caption">{passed ? `Pick ${formatMonthLong(monthOf(next))} or later` : verdict ? paydaysLine(verdict) : " "}</Text>
      </View>
      {passed ? (
        <VerdictCard look={passedCopy(next)} />
      ) : verdict ? (
        <VerdictCard look={verdictCopy(verdict)} />
      ) : check.isError ? (
        <ErrorState title="We couldn't check this goal" body={describeError(check.error, messageFor)} onRetry={() => void check.refetch()} />
      ) : (
        <Skeleton height={88} />
      )}
      <ModePicker variant="goal" value={values.mode} onChange={(goalMode) => setValues((v) => ({ ...v, mode: goalMode }))} />
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/screens/new-goal-screen.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/error-state";
import { Screen } from "@/components/ui/screen";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { useProfile } from "@/features/profile/hooks";
import { paydayRuleOf } from "@/features/profile/payday";
import { describeError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { nextPayday } from "@/lib/cycle";
import { addMonths, monthOf, todayIst } from "@/lib/dates";
import { messageFor } from "@/lib/errors";
import { GoalForm } from "../components/goal-form";
import { useCreateGoal } from "../hooks";

export function NewGoalScreen({ onClose, today = todayIst() }: { onClose: () => void; today?: string }) {
  const profile = useProfile();
  const create = useCreateGoal();
  if (profile.isLoading) {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <Skeleton height={52} />
        <Skeleton height={120} />
      </Screen>
    );
  }
  if (!profile.data) {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <ErrorState body={profile.error ? describeError(profile.error, messageFor) : "Finish setup first"} onRetry={() => void profile.refetch()} />
      </Screen>
    );
  }
  const payday = paydayRuleOf(profile.data);
  const firstMonth = monthOf(nextPayday(today, payday));
  return (
    <GoalForm
      mode="new"
      title="New goal"
      submitLabel="Start this quest"
      initial={{ name: "", targetPaise: 6000000, deadline: addMonths(firstMonth, 9), mode: "FLEXIBLE" }}
      today={today}
      payday={payday}
      onSubmit={async (v) => {
        await create.mutateAsync({ type: "DEADLINE", ...v });
        onClose();
      }}
      onClose={onClose}
    />
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/screens/edit-goal-screen.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/empty-state";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/error-state";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Screen } from "@/components/ui/screen";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { useProfile } from "@/features/profile/hooks";
import { paydayRuleOf } from "@/features/profile/payday";
import { describeError, isApiError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { nextPayday } from "@/lib/cycle";
import { monthOf, todayIst } from "@/lib/dates";
import { messageFor } from "@/lib/errors";
import type { GoalPatch } from "../api";
import { GoalForm } from "../components/goal-form";
import { useGoal, usePatchGoal } from "../hooks";

export function EditGoalScreen({ id, onClose, today = todayIst() }: { id: string; onClose: () => void; today?: string }) {
  const goal = useGoal(id);
  const profile = useProfile();
  const patch = usePatchGoal();
  if (goal.isLoading || profile.isLoading) {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <Skeleton height={52} />
        <Skeleton height={120} />
      </Screen>
    );
  }
  if (isApiError(goal.error, "NOT_FOUND")) {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <EmptyState title="That goal no longer exists" action={<Button title="Back to goals" variant="secondary" onPress={onClose} />} />
      </Screen>
    );
  }
  if (!goal.data || !profile.data) {
    const failed = goal.error ?? profile.error;
    return (
      <Screen>
        <ErrorState body={failed ? describeError(failed, messageFor) : undefined} onRetry={() => void Promise.all([goal.refetch(), profile.refetch()])} />
      </Screen>
    );
  }
  const g = goal.data.goal;
  const payday = paydayRuleOf(profile.data);
  const initial = { name: g.name, targetPaise: g.targetPaise ?? 0, deadline: g.deadline ?? monthOf(nextPayday(today, payday)), mode: g.mode };
  return (
    <GoalForm
      mode="edit"
      title="Edit goal"
      submitLabel="Save changes"
      initial={initial}
      today={today}
      payday={payday}
      goalId={id}
      onSubmit={async (v) => {
        const body: GoalPatch = {
          ...(v.name !== initial.name ? { name: v.name } : {}),
          ...(v.targetPaise !== initial.targetPaise ? { targetPaise: v.targetPaise } : {}),
          ...(v.deadline !== initial.deadline ? { deadline: v.deadline } : {}),
          ...(v.mode !== initial.mode ? { mode: v.mode } : {}),
        };
        await patch.mutateAsync({ id, body });
        onClose();
      }}
      onClose={onClose}
    />
  );
}
```

- Produces: `optionLabel(option)`, `cardTitle(catchUp)`, `cardBody(catchUp)`, `resultLine(catchUp, tomorrowPaise, cycleEnd)`; `statusOf(goal) : {text, icon, ink, behind}`; `<ModePill mode />`; `<CatchUpCard catchUp tomorrowPaise cycleEnd busy? error? onPick(goalId, kind) onUndo(goalId) />` (Task 13 puts it on Home); `<GoalBar percent expectedPercent label showTick />` (Task 12 reuses it); `<GoalDetailScreen id onBack onEdit onDeleted />`; route `/goals/[id]`.

```ts
import type { CatchUp } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { cardBody, cardTitle, optionLabel, resultLine } from "./catch-up-copy";

const behind: CatchUp = {
  situation: "BEHIND",
  goalId: "g",
  goalName: "Goa trip",
  strict: false,
  gapPaise: 180000,
  options: [
    { kind: "TIGHTEN", perDayPaise: 22500, days: 8, tomorrowPaise: 35200 },
    { kind: "MOVE", newDeadline: "2027-04", extraPaydays: 1 },
    { kind: "LOWER", newTargetPaise: 2820000 },
  ],
};

function applied(kind: "TIGHTEN" | "MOVE" | "LOWER" | "TAKE", extra: Partial<NonNullable<CatchUp["applied"]>> = {}): CatchUp {
  return { ...behind, situation: "APPLIED", options: [], applied: { actionId: "a", kind, canUndo: true, ...extra } };
}

describe("catch-up copy (HomeOver mockup)", () => {
  it("names each option with its numbers", () => {
    expect(behind.options.map(optionLabel)).toEqual(["Spend ₹225 less a day for 8 days", "Move the deadline to Apr 2027", "Lower the target to ₹28,200"]);
    expect(optionLabel({ kind: "TIGHTEN", perDayPaise: 90000, days: 1 })).toBe("Spend ₹900 less a day for 1 day");
    expect(optionLabel({ kind: "TAKE", takePaise: 40000 })).toBe("Take ₹400 from this cycle's savings");
  });

  it("titles and bodies follow the situation", () => {
    expect(cardTitle(behind)).toBe("Goa trip: ₹1,800 behind");
    expect(cardBody(behind)).toBe("Earlier months came up short. Pick a way to catch up.");
    expect(cardBody({ ...behind, strict: true, options: [behind.options[0]!] })).toBe("This goal is Strict, so the way back is spending less");
    expect(cardBody({ ...behind, options: [] })).toBe("The next contributions grow to close the gap");
    expect(cardTitle(applied("TIGHTEN"))).toBe("Goa trip: catching up");
    expect(cardBody(applied("TIGHTEN"))).toBe("Plan saved. You can change it any time in Goals.");
    const pool: CatchUp = { situation: "POOL_NEGATIVE", goalId: "m", goalName: "Monthly savings", strict: false, gapPaise: 40000, options: [{ kind: "TAKE", takePaise: 40000 }] };
    expect(cardTitle(pool)).toBe("₹400 over this cycle's budget");
    expect(cardBody(pool)).toBe("Take it from this cycle's savings, or it comes off next cycle's budget");
    expect(cardBody({ ...pool, strict: true, options: [] })).toBe("Your savings are Strict, so this comes off next cycle's budget");
  });

  it("says what changed after a pick", () => {
    expect(resultLine(applied("TIGHTEN", { perDayPaise: 22500, days: 8 }), 35200, "2026-10-31")).toBe("Done. Tomorrow's budget is ₹352, and Goa trip is back on track by Sat, 31 Oct.");
    expect(resultLine(applied("TIGHTEN", { perDayPaise: 22500, days: 8 }), undefined, "2026-10-31")).toBe("Done. You'll spend ₹225 less a day for 8 days.");
    expect(resultLine(applied("MOVE", { newDeadline: "2027-04", extraPaydays: 1 }), 57700, "2026-10-31")).toBe("Done. Goa trip now ends in Apr 2027, which adds one payday to catch up.");
    expect(resultLine(applied("MOVE", { newDeadline: "2027-06", extraPaydays: 3 }), 57700, "2026-10-31")).toBe("Done. Goa trip now ends in Jun 2027, which adds 3 paydays to catch up.");
    expect(resultLine(applied("LOWER", { newTargetPaise: 2820000 }), 57700, "2026-10-31")).toBe("Done. Goa trip's target is now ₹28,200.");
    expect(resultLine(applied("TAKE", { takePaise: 40000 }), 0, "2026-10-31")).toBe("Done. ₹400 of this cycle's savings went back into your budget.");
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import React from "react";
import type { CatchUp } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { CatchUpCard } from "./catch-up-card";

const behind: CatchUp = {
  situation: "BEHIND",
  goalId: "g",
  goalName: "Goa trip",
  strict: false,
  gapPaise: 180000,
  options: [
    { kind: "TIGHTEN", perDayPaise: 22500, days: 8, tomorrowPaise: 35200 },
    { kind: "MOVE", newDeadline: "2027-04", extraPaydays: 1 },
    { kind: "LOWER", newTargetPaise: 2820000 },
  ],
};

describe("CatchUpCard (APP-23 card half)", () => {
  it("lists the options and reports a pick", () => {
    const onPick = jest.fn();
    render(<CatchUpCard catchUp={behind} tomorrowPaise={57700} cycleEnd="2026-10-31" onPick={onPick} onUndo={jest.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Goa trip: ₹1,800 behind")).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Move the deadline to Apr 2027" }));
    expect(onPick).toHaveBeenCalledWith("g", "MOVE");
  });

  it("after a pick shows the result and Undo", () => {
    const onUndo = jest.fn();
    const applied: CatchUp = { ...behind, situation: "APPLIED", options: [], applied: { actionId: "a", kind: "TIGHTEN", perDayPaise: 22500, days: 8, canUndo: true } };
    render(<CatchUpCard catchUp={applied} tomorrowPaise={35200} cycleEnd="2026-10-31" onPick={jest.fn()} onUndo={onUndo} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Goa trip: catching up")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Done. Tomorrow's budget is ₹352, and Goa trip is back on track by Sat, 31 Oct.")).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Undo" }));
    expect(onUndo).toHaveBeenCalledWith("g");
  });

  it("hides Undo when it is no longer possible and disables options while busy", () => {
    const done: CatchUp = { ...behind, situation: "APPLIED", options: [], applied: { actionId: "a", kind: "LOWER", newTargetPaise: 2820000, canUndo: false } };
    const { rerender } = render(<CatchUpCard catchUp={done} tomorrowPaise={57700} cycleEnd="2026-10-31" onPick={jest.fn()} onUndo={jest.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Undo" })).toBeNull();
    rerender(<CatchUpCard catchUp={behind} tomorrowPaise={57700} cycleEnd="2026-10-31" busy onPick={jest.fn()} onUndo={jest.fn()} error="Something went wrong. Try again in a moment." />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Spend ₹225 less a day for 8 days" })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText("Something went wrong. Try again in a moment.")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("every option is a labelled control of at least 48px", () => {
    render(<CatchUpCard catchUp={behind} tomorrowPaise={57700} cycleEnd="2026-10-31" onPick={jest.fn()} onUndo={jest.fn()} />);
    for (const b of screen.getAllByRole("button")) {
      expect(b).toHaveStyle({ minHeight: 48 });
    }
  });
});
```

```tsx
function renderDetail(handlers = { onBack: jest.fn(), onEdit: jest.fn(), onDeleted: jest.fn() }) {

    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Keep it" }));
    expect(screen.queryByText("Delete Goa trip? What you saved stays in its history.")).toBeNull();
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete goal" }));
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(onDeleted).toHaveBeenCalled());
  });

  it("edit opens the sheet", async () => {
    api.get.mockResolvedValue(goa);
    const { onEdit } = renderDetail();
    fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Edit goal" }));
    expect(onEdit).toHaveBeenCalledWith("g");
  });

  it("a done goal can only be deleted", async () => {
    api.get.mockResolvedValue({ ...goa, goal: { ...goa.goal, status: "DONE", savedPaise: 3000000, percent: 100 }, catchUp: undefined });
    renderDetail();
    expect(await screen.findByText("Done")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Edit goal" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Pause goal" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete goal" })).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("a deleted or unknown goal says so (ERR-13)", async () => {
    api.get.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError("NOT_FOUND", 404, "x"));
    const { onBack } = renderDetail();
    expect(await screen.findByText("That goal no longer exists")).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Back to goals" }));
    expect(onBack).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("a failure shows Retry, and Retry asks again (ERR-13)", async () => {
    api.get.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError("INTERNAL", 500, "x")).mockResolvedValueOnce(goa);
    renderDetail();
    fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Retry" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Goa trip")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`app/src/features/goals/catch-up-copy.ts`:

```ts
import type { CatchUp, CatchUpApplied, CatchUpOption } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { formatDay, formatMonth } from "@/lib/dates";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";

function days(n: number): string {
  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? "day" : "days"}`;
}

/** The HomeOver mockup's option rows. */
export function optionLabel(o: CatchUpOption): string {
  switch (o.kind) {
    case "TIGHTEN":
      return `Spend ${formatRupees(o.perDayPaise ?? 0)} less a day for ${days(o.days ?? 0)}`;
    case "MOVE":
      return `Move the deadline to ${o.newDeadline ? formatMonth(o.newDeadline) : ""}`;
    case "LOWER":
      return `Lower the target to ${formatRupees(o.newTargetPaise ?? 0)}`;
    case "TAKE":
      return `Take ${formatRupees(o.takePaise ?? 0)} from this cycle's savings`;
  }
}

export function cardTitle(c: CatchUp): string {
  switch (c.situation) {
    case "BEHIND":
      return `${c.goalName}: ${formatRupees(c.gapPaise)} behind`;
    case "APPLIED":
      return `${c.goalName}: catching up`;
    case "POOL_NEGATIVE":
      return `${formatRupees(c.gapPaise)} over this cycle's budget`;
  }
}

export function cardBody(c: CatchUp): string {
  switch (c.situation) {
    case "BEHIND":
      if (c.options.length === 0) return "The next contributions grow to close the gap";
      return c.strict ? "This goal is Strict, so the way back is spending less" : "Earlier months came up short. Pick a way to catch up.";
    case "APPLIED":
      return "Plan saved. You can change it any time in Goals.";
    case "POOL_NEGATIVE":
      return c.options.length > 0
        ? "Take it from this cycle's savings, or it comes off next cycle's budget"
        : "Your savings are Strict, so this comes off next cycle's budget";
  }
}

/** What changed, in the mockup's "Done. …" voice. {@code tomorrowPaise} is today's live figure from the server. */
export function resultLine(c: CatchUp, tomorrowPaise: number | undefined, cycleEnd: string): string {
  const a: CatchUpApplied | undefined = c.applied;
  if (!a) return "";
  switch (a.kind) {
    case "TIGHTEN":
      return tomorrowPaise !== undefined
        ? `Done. Tomorrow's budget is ${formatRupees(tomorrowPaise)}, and ${c.goalName} is back on track by ${formatDay(cycleEnd)}.`
        : `Done. You'll spend ${formatRupees(a.perDayPaise ?? 0)} less a day for ${days(a.days ?? 0)}.`;
    case "MOVE": {
      const n = a.extraPaydays ?? 1;
      return `Done. ${c.goalName} now ends in ${a.newDeadline ? formatMonth(a.newDeadline) : ""}, which adds ${n === 1 ? "one payday" : `${n} paydays`} to catch up.`;
    }
    case "LOWER":
      return `Done. ${c.goalName}'s target is now ${formatRupees(a.newTargetPaise ?? 0)}.`;
    case "TAKE":
      return `Done. ${formatRupees(a.takePaise ?? 0)} of this cycle's savings went back into your budget.`;
  }
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/components/goal-bar.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { colors, radius } from "@/theme";

function clamp(n: number): number {
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n));
}

/** The Goals mockup's bar: saved in green, with an ink tick at today's target. */
export function GoalBar({ percent, expectedPercent, label, showTick }: { percent: number; expectedPercent: number; label: string; showTick: boolean }) {
  return (
    <View accessibilityRole="progressbar" accessibilityLabel={label} accessibilityValue={{ now: clamp(percent), min: 0, max: 100 }} style={{ height: 16, justifyContent: "center" }}>
      <View style={{ height: 10, borderRadius: radius.full, backgroundColor: colors.sand, overflow: "hidden" }}>
        <View style={{ width: `${clamp(percent)}%`, height: "100%", borderRadius: radius.full, backgroundColor: colors.good }} />
      </View>
      {showTick ? (
        <View
          testID="expected-tick"
          accessibilityElementsHidden
          importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants"
          style={{ position: "absolute", top: 0, left: `${clamp(expectedPercent)}%`, width: 2, height: 16, backgroundColor: colors.ink }}
        />
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/components/catch-up-card.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { Pressable, View } from "react-native";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import type { CatchUp, CatchUpKind } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { colors, fonts, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";
import { cardBody, cardTitle, optionLabel, resultLine } from "../catch-up-copy";

type Props = {
  catchUp: CatchUp;
  tomorrowPaise: number | undefined;
  cycleEnd: string;
  busy?: boolean;
  error?: string | null;
  onPick: (goalId: string, kind: CatchUpKind) => void;
  onUndo: (goalId: string) => void;
};

/** The HomeOver mockup's catch-up card, also shown on a goal (spec 2.2). Errors render inline: they carry a decision. */
export function CatchUpCard({ catchUp, tomorrowPaise, cycleEnd, busy = false, error, onPick, onUndo }: Props) {
  const applied = catchUp.situation === "APPLIED" ? catchUp.applied : undefined;
  return (
    <View testID="catch-up-card" style={{ gap: spacing.cm, padding: spacing.md, borderRadius: radius.lg, borderCurve: "continuous", backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.line }}>
      <View style={{ gap: spacing.xs }}>
        <Text variant="bodyStrong" accessibilityRole="header">
          {cardTitle(catchUp)}
        </Text>
        <Text variant="labelMuted">{cardBody(catchUp)}</Text>
      </View>
      {applied ? (
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.xs }}>
          <View accessibilityLiveRegion="polite" style={{ flexDirection: "row", gap: spacing.sm, alignItems: "flex-start" }}>
            <Icon name="check" size={18} color={colors.goodDeep} />
            <Text variant="label" color={colors.goodDeep} style={{ flex: 1, fontFamily: fonts.ui700 }}>
              {resultLine(catchUp, tomorrowPaise, cycleEnd)}
            </Text>
          </View>
          {applied.canUndo ? (
            <Pressable
              accessibilityRole="button"
              accessibilityLabel="Undo"
              accessibilityState={{ disabled: busy }}
              disabled={busy}
              onPress={() => onUndo(catchUp.goalId)}
              style={{ minHeight: 48, alignSelf: "flex-start", justifyContent: "center" }}
            >
              <Text variant="label" style={{ textDecorationLine: "underline" }}>
                Undo
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          ) : null}
        </View>
      ) : (
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
          {catchUp.options.map((o) => (
            <Pressable
              key={o.kind}
              accessibilityRole="button"
              accessibilityLabel={optionLabel(o)}
              accessibilityState={{ disabled: busy }}
              disabled={busy}
              onPress={() => onPick(catchUp.goalId, o.kind)}
              style={({ pressed }) => ({
                minHeight: 48,
                paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,
                borderRadius: radius.md,
                borderCurve: "continuous",
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: colors.control,
                backgroundColor: colors.surface,
                flexDirection: "row",
                alignItems: "center",
                justifyContent: "space-between",
                gap: spacing.sm,
                opacity: busy ? 0.5 : pressed ? 0.85 : 1,
              })}
            >
              <Text variant="body" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                {optionLabel(o)}
              </Text>
              <Icon name="right" size={20} color={colors.muted} />
            </Pressable>
          ))}
        </View>
      )}
      {error ? (
        <Text variant="label" color={colors.bad} accessibilityRole="alert">
          {error}
        </Text>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Pressable, View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/empty-state";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/error-state";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Screen } from "@/components/ui/screen";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { useToday } from "@/features/budget/hooks";
import { describeError, isApiError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { formatDay, formatDayMonth, formatMonth, todayIst } from "@/lib/dates";
import { messageFor } from "@/lib/errors";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import { colors, radius, spacing, touchTarget } from "@/theme";
import type { CycleContribution } from "../api";
import { CatchUpCard } from "../components/catch-up-card";
import { GoalBar } from "../components/goal-bar";
import { ModePill } from "../components/mode-pill";
import { statusOf } from "../goal-status";
import { useCatchUp, useDeleteGoal, useGoal, usePauseGoal, useResumeGoal, useUndoCatchUp } from "../hooks";

type Props = { id: string; onBack: () => void; onEdit: (id: string) => void; onDeleted: () => void };

/** Spec 3.2 #5: progress, expected-by-today tick, contributions by cycle, confirmed transfers, edit, pause, delete, catch-up. */
export function GoalDetailScreen({ id, onBack, onEdit, onDeleted }: Props) {
  const goal = useGoal(id);
  const today = useToday();
  const pause = usePauseGoal();
  const resume = useResumeGoal();
  const remove = useDeleteGoal();
  const catchUp = useCatchUp();
  const undo = useUndoCatchUp();
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const run = async (action: () => Promise<unknown>) => {
    setActionError(null);
    try {
      await action();
    } catch (e) {
      setActionError(describeError(e, messageFor));
    }
  };

  if (isApiError(goal.error, "NOT_FOUND")) {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <EmptyState title="That goal no longer exists" action={<Button title="Back to goals" variant="secondary" onPress={onBack} />} />
      </Screen>
    );
  }
  if (!goal.data) {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <BackRow onBack={onBack} />
        {goal.isLoading ? (
          <View testID="goal-detail-skeleton" style={{ gap: spacing.md }}>
            <Skeleton height={32} width="60%" />
            <Skeleton height={16} />
            <Skeleton height={120} />
          </View>
        ) : (
          <ErrorState body={describeError(goal.error, messageFor)} onRetry={() => void goal.refetch()} />
        )}
      </Screen>
    );
  }

  const { goal: g, cycles, transfers, milestones } = goal.data;
  const status = statusOf(g);
  const busy = pause.isPending || resume.isPending || remove.isPending || catchUp.isPending || undo.isPending;

  return (
    <Screen testID="goal-detail">
      <BackRow onBack={onBack} />
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <Text variant="heading" style={{ flex: 1 }} accessibilityRole="header">
          {g.name}
        </Text>
        <ModePill mode={g.mode} />
      </View>
      <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "baseline", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
          <Text variant="amount" style={{ fontVariant: ["tabular-nums"] }}>
            {formatRupees(g.savedPaise)}
          </Text>
          <Text variant="labelMuted">{`of ${formatRupees(g.targetPaise ?? 0)} · ${g.percent}%`}</Text>
        </View>
        <GoalBar percent={g.percent} expectedPercent={g.expectedPercent} label={`${g.name} progress`} showTick={g.status === "ACTIVE"} />
        <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: spacing.sm }}>
          <Icon name={status.icon} size={16} color={status.ink} />
          <Text variant="label" color={status.ink}>
            {status.text}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
        <Text variant="body">Expected by today</Text>
        <Text variant="bodyStrong">{formatRupees(g.expectedPaise)}</Text>
      </View>
      <Text variant="caption">
        {g.status === "PAUSED"
          ? "Paused. Nothing is set aside until you resume."
          : `${formatRupees(g.contributionPaise)} a month · ${g.paydaysLeft} ${g.paydaysLeft === 1 ? "payday" : "paydays"} left · ${g.deadline ? formatMonth(g.deadline) : ""}`}
      </Text>
      {goal.data.catchUp ? (
        <CatchUpCard
          catchUp={goal.data.catchUp}
          tomorrowPaise={today.data?.tomorrowPaise}
          cycleEnd={today.data?.cycleEnd ?? ""}
          busy={busy}
          error={actionError}
          onPick={(goalId, kind) => void run(() => catchUp.mutateAsync({ goalId, kind }))}
          onUndo={(goalId) => void run(() => undo.mutateAsync(goalId))}
        />
      ) : null}
      {milestones.length > 0 ? (
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.xs }}>
          {milestones.map((m) => (
            <Text key={m.percent} variant="label" color={colors.goodDeep}>
              {`${m.percent}% reached on ${formatDay(todayIst(new Date(m.reachedAt)))}`}
            </Text>
          ))}
        </View>
      ) : null}
      <Text variant="title">Contributions</Text>
      {cycles.length === 0 ? (
        <Text variant="bodyMuted">Nothing set aside yet. The first contribution comes on your next payday.</Text>
      ) : (
        cycles.map((c) => <CycleRow key={c.cycleId} cycle={c} />)
      )}
      <Text variant="title">Confirmed by bank</Text>
      {transfers.length === 0 ? (
        <Text variant="bodyMuted">No savings transfers matched yet. Upload a statement and they show up here.</Text>
      ) : (
        transfers.map((t) => (
          <View key={`${t.date}-${t.amountPaise}`} style={{ flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", minHeight: touchTarget, alignItems: "center" }}>
            <Text variant="body">{formatDay(t.date)}</Text>
            <Text variant="bodyStrong">{formatRupees(t.amountPaise)}</Text>
          </View>
        ))
      )}
      {actionError && !goal.data.catchUp ? (
        <Text variant="label" color={colors.bad} accessibilityRole="alert">
          {actionError}
        </Text>
      ) : null}
      <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm, paddingTop: spacing.md }}>
        {g.status !== "DONE" ? <Button title="Edit goal" variant="secondary" onPress={() => onEdit(g.id)} /> : null}
        {g.status === "ACTIVE" ? <Button title="Pause goal" variant="secondary" disabled={busy} onPress={() => void run(() => pause.mutateAsync(g.id))} /> : null}
        {g.status === "PAUSED" ? <Button title="Resume goal" variant="secondary" disabled={busy} onPress={() => void run(() => resume.mutateAsync(g.id))} /> : null}
        {confirming ? (
          <View accessibilityRole="alert" style={{ gap: spacing.sm, padding: spacing.md, borderRadius: radius.lg, backgroundColor: colors.badTint }}>
            <Text variant="body">{`Delete ${g.name}? What you saved stays in its history.`}</Text>
            <Button title="Delete" variant="danger" loading={remove.isPending} onPress={() => void run(async () => {
              await remove.mutateAsync(g.id);
              onDeleted();
            })} />
            <Button title="Keep it" variant="ghost" onPress={() => setConfirming(false)} />
          </View>
        ) : (
          <Button title="Delete goal" variant="danger" disabled={busy} onPress={() => setConfirming(true)} />
        )}
      </View>
    </Screen>
  );
}

function BackRow({ onBack }: { onBack: () => void }) {
  return (
    <Pressable
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel="Back to goals"
      onPress={onBack}
      style={{ width: 48, height: 48, borderRadius: radius.full, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.control, backgroundColor: colors.surface, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}
    >
      <Icon name="left" size={20} />
    </Pressable>
  );
}

function CycleRow({ cycle }: { cycle: CycleContribution }) {
  const parts = [
    cycle.plannedPaise > 0 ? `Set aside ${formatRupees(cycle.plannedPaise)}` : null,
    cycle.extraPaise > 0 ? `Catch-up ${formatRupees(cycle.extraPaise)}` : null,
    cycle.shortfallPaise < 0 ? `Taken back ${formatRupees(-cycle.shortfallPaise)}` : null,
  ].filter((p): p is string => p !== null);
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: spacing.xs, paddingVertical: spacing.sm, borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: colors.line }}>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
        <Text variant="body">{`${formatDayMonth(cycle.cycleStart)} – ${formatDayMonth(cycle.cycleEnd)}`}</Text>
        <Text variant="bodyStrong">{formatRupees(cycle.totalPaise)}</Text>
      </View>
      {parts.length > 0 ? <Text variant="caption">{parts.join(" · ")}</Text> : null}
    </View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { colors, fonts, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";
import type { GoalMode } from "../api";

/** The Goals mockup's mode tag: a bell for Flexible, a lock for Strict. The text names it, so the icon is decorative. */
export function ModePill({ mode }: { mode: GoalMode }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: spacing.xs, height: 28, paddingHorizontal: spacing.cm, borderRadius: radius.full, backgroundColor: colors.sand }}>
      <Icon name={mode === "STRICT" ? "lock" : "bell"} size={14} />
      <Text variant="caption" color={colors.ink} style={{ fontFamily: fonts.ui700 }}>
        {mode === "STRICT" ? "Strict" : "Flexible"}
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/goal-status.ts` (shared with Task 12's goal card):

```ts
import type { IconName } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import { colors } from "@/theme";
import type { Goal } from "./api";

/** The status line under a goal's bar (Goals mockup). */
export function statusOf(g: Goal): { text: string; icon: IconName; ink: string; behind: boolean } {
  if (g.status === "DONE") return { text: "Done", icon: "trophy", ink: colors.goodDeep, behind: false };
  if (g.status === "PAUSED") return { text: "Paused", icon: "moon", ink: colors.muted, behind: false };
  if (g.catchingUp) return { text: "Catching up", icon: "check", ink: colors.good, behind: false };
  if (g.behindPaise > 0) return { text: `Behind by ${formatRupees(g.behindPaise)}`, icon: "alert", ink: colors.bad, behind: true };
  return { text: "On track", icon: "check", ink: colors.good, behind: false };
}
```

```tsx
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from "expo-router";
import React from "react";
import { GoalDetailScreen } from "@/features/goals/screens/goal-detail-screen";

export { RouteErrorFallback as ErrorBoundary } from "@/components/error-boundary";

export default function GoalDetailRoute() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  return (
    <GoalDetailScreen
      id={id}
      onBack={() => router.back()}
      onEdit={(goalId) => router.push({ pathname: "/goals/[id]/edit", params: { id: goalId } })}
      onDeleted={() => router.back()}
    />
  );
}
```

```tsx
};
const fund: Goal = {

    expect(screen.getByTestId("goals-skeleton")).toBeTruthy();

    expect(await screen.findByText("Goa trip")).toBeTruthy();
```
