```tsx
  it("celebrates a fresh milestone once", async () => {
    list.mockResolvedValue({ ...mockup, goals: [{ ...goa, lastMilestone: { percent: 50, reachedAt: "2026-10-22T10:00:00Z" } }] });
    renderGoals();
    expect(await screen.findByText("Goa trip is 50% there")).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Nice" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByText("Goa trip is 50% there")).toBeNull());
    expect(kv.set).toHaveBeenCalledWith("goal-milestones-seen", JSON.stringify(["g:50"]));
  });

  it("picks the newest unseen milestone from the last three days", () => {
    const now = NOW.getTime();
    const recent = { ...goa, lastMilestone: { percent: 25, reachedAt: "2026-10-22T10:00:00Z" } };
    const newer = { ...fund, lastMilestone: { percent: 50, reachedAt: "2026-10-23T07:00:00Z" } };
    const old = { ...goa, id: "o", lastMilestone: { percent: 75, reachedAt: "2026-10-10T07:00:00Z" } };
    expect(latestUnseen([recent, newer, old], [], now)?.key).toBe("f:50");
    expect(latestUnseen([recent, newer, old], ["f:50"], now)?.key).toBe("g:25");
    expect(latestUnseen([old], [], now)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import React from "react";
import { ApiError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { SavingsSheet } from "./savings-sheet";

jest.mock("@/features/goals/api", () => ({ goalsApi: { list: jest.fn(), patch: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/features/profile/api", () => ({
  profileApi: { get: jest.fn(), put: jest.fn() },
  fixedCostApi: { list: jest.fn(), add: jest.fn(), patch: jest.fn(), remove: jest.fn() },
}));
jest.mock("@/features/budget/api", () => ({ budgetApi: { getToday: jest.fn(), setFirstCycle: jest.fn() } }));
import { goalsApi } from "@/features/goals/api";
import { fixedCostApi, profileApi } from "@/features/profile/api";

const profile = { incomePaise: 5000000, savingsPaise: 2000000, paydayRule: "FIXED_DAY" as const, paydayDay: 1, personality: "CHILL" as const, morningTime: "08:00", nightTime: "22:00", timezone: "Asia/Kolkata" as const };

function renderSheet(onDone = jest.fn()) {
  render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } })}>
      <SavingsSheet onDone={onDone} today="2026-10-23" />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
  return onDone;
}

function typeSavings(rupees: string) {
  fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText(/^Save every month, ₹[\d,]+, tap to type an amount$/));
  fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText("Save every month"), rupees);
  fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Done" }));
}

beforeEach(() => {
  jest.clearAllMocks();
  (profileApi.get as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue(profile);
  (fixedCostApi.list as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue([{ id: "a", name: "Rent", amountPaise: 1500000 }]);
  (goalsApi.list as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ monthly: { id: "m", mode: "FLEXIBLE", savingsPaise: 2000000, cycleStart: "2026-10-01", split: [], freeThisCyclePaise: 2000000, committedPaise: 600000, freeNextPaise: 1400000 }, goals: [] });
});

describe("Savings sheet (APP-34)", () => {
  it("shows the reality check and what goals need", async () => {
    renderSheet();
    expect(await screen.findByText("Doable")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Your goals need ₹6,000 a month")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Flexible" })).toBeChecked();
  });

  it("refuses savings below what goals need", async () => {
    renderSheet();
    await screen.findByText("Doable");
    typeSavings("5000");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Your goals need at least ₹6,000" })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("saves the amount and a new mode, then closes", async () => {
    (profileApi.put as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ ...profile, savingsPaise: 2500000 });
    (goalsApi.patch as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ id: "m", mode: "STRICT" });
    const onDone = renderSheet();
    await screen.findByText("Doable");
    typeSavings("25000");
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "Strict" }));
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(profileApi.put).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ ...profile, savingsPaise: 2500000 });
    expect(goalsApi.patch).toHaveBeenCalledWith("m", { mode: "STRICT" });
  });

  it("a server refusal stays on the sheet with the reason", async () => {
    (profileApi.put as jest.Mock).mockRejectedValue(new ApiError("NOT_POSSIBLE", 422, "x"));
    const onDone = renderSheet();
    await screen.findByText("Doable");
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Those numbers do not add up")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(onDone).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

`app/src/features/goals/components/goal-card.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { Pressable, View } from "react-native";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { formatMonth } from "@/lib/dates";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import { colors, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";
import type { Goal } from "../api";
import { statusOf } from "../goal-status";
import { GoalBar } from "./goal-bar";
import { ModePill } from "./mode-pill";

/** One quest card from the Goals mockup. The whole card opens the goal, including "See catch-up plan". */
export function GoalCard({ goal, onOpen }: { goal: Goal; onOpen: (id: string) => void }) {
  const status = statusOf(goal);
  const month = goal.deadline ? formatMonth(goal.deadline) : "";
  const sub = goal.status === "ACTIVE" ? `${month} · ${formatRupees(goal.contributionPaise)}/mo` : `${month} · ${status.text}`;
  return (
    <Pressable
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={`${goal.name}, ${formatRupees(goal.savedPaise)} of ${formatRupees(goal.targetPaise ?? 0)}, ${status.text}`}
      onPress={() => onOpen(goal.id)}
      style={({ pressed }) => ({
        gap: spacing.cm,
        padding: spacing.md,
        borderRadius: radius.lg,
        borderCurve: "continuous",
        backgroundColor: colors.surface,
        borderWidth: 1,
        borderColor: colors.line,
        opacity: pressed ? 0.9 : 1,
      })}
    >
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: spacing.xs }}>
          <Text variant="bodyStrong">{goal.name}</Text>
          <Text variant="labelMuted">{sub}</Text>
        </View>
        <ModePill mode={goal.mode} />
      </View>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "baseline", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
        <Text variant="amount" style={{ fontVariant: ["tabular-nums"] }}>
          {formatRupees(goal.savedPaise)}
        </Text>
        <Text variant="labelMuted">{`of ${formatRupees(goal.targetPaise ?? 0)} · ${goal.percent}%`}</Text>
      </View>
      <GoalBar percent={goal.percent} expectedPercent={goal.expectedPercent} label={`${goal.name} progress`} showTick={goal.status === "ACTIVE"} />
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: spacing.sm, minHeight: 44 }}>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: spacing.sm }}>
          <Icon name={status.icon} size={16} color={status.ink} />
          <Text variant="label" color={status.ink}>
            {status.text}
          </Text>
        </View>
        {status.behind ? (
          <Text variant="label" style={{ textDecorationLine: "underline" }}>
            See catch-up plan
          </Text>
        ) : null}
      </View>
    </Pressable>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Gullak } from "@/features/gullak/gullak";
import { formatDay } from "@/lib/dates";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import { colors, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";
import type { MonthlySummary } from "../api";

/** The Goals mockup's dark header: this cycle's savings, how they split, and the mode. */
export function MonthlyCard({ monthly, onEdit }: { monthly: MonthlySummary; onEdit: () => void }) {
  const split = [...monthly.split.map((s) => `${s.name} ${formatRupees(s.amountPaise)}`), `Free ${formatRupees(monthly.freeThisCyclePaise)}`].join(" · ");
  return (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", gap: spacing.md, padding: spacing.md, borderRadius: radius.lg, borderCurve: "continuous", backgroundColor: colors.dark }}>
      <Gullak mood="happy" stage="clay" size={88} />
      <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <Text variant="label" color={colors.darkMuted} accessibilityRole="header">
          This cycle's savings
        </Text>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "baseline", gap: spacing.sm, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <Text variant="amount" color={colors.darkText} style={{ fontVariant: ["tabular-nums"] }}>
            {formatRupees(monthly.savingsPaise)}
          </Text>
          <Text variant="label" color={colors.darkMuted}>{`on ${formatDay(monthly.cycleStart)}`}</Text>
        </View>
        <Text variant="caption" color={colors.darkMuted}>
          {split}
        </Text>
        <Text variant="caption" color={colors.darkMuted}>{`If you overspend: ${monthly.mode === "STRICT" ? "Strict" : "Flexible"}`}</Text>
        <Button title="Edit savings" variant="secondary" onPress={onEdit} style={{ alignSelf: "flex-start" }} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/components/milestone-banner.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Gullak } from "@/features/gullak/gullak";
import { kv } from "@/lib/kv";
import { colors, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";
import type { Goal } from "../api";

export const SEEN_KEY = "goal-milestones-seen";
const RECENT_MS = 3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

/** The newest milestone from the last three days that the user has not dismissed. */
export function latestUnseen(goals: Goal[], seen: string[], now: number): { goal: Goal; percent: number; key: string } | null {
  let best: { goal: Goal; percent: number; key: string; at: number } | null = null;
  for (const g of goals) {
    const m = g.lastMilestone;
    if (!m) continue;
    const key = `${g.id}:${m.percent}`;
    const at = Date.parse(m.reachedAt);
    if (seen.includes(key) || now - at > RECENT_MS) continue;
    if (best === null || at > best.at) best = { goal: g, percent: m.percent, key, at };
  }
  return best === null ? null : { goal: best.goal, percent: best.percent, key: best.key };
}

function parseSeen(raw: string | null): string[] {
  if (!raw) return [];
  try {
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((x): x is string => typeof x === "string") : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

/** Spec 2.2: a milestone gets a Gullak celebration. Shown once per milestone on this phone. */
export function MilestoneBanner({ goals, now }: { goals: Goal[]; now: number }) {
  const [seen, setSeen] = useState<string[] | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void kv.get(SEEN_KEY).then((raw) => {
      if (alive) setSeen(parseSeen(raw));
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);
  if (seen === null) return null;
  const pick = latestUnseen(goals, seen, now);
  if (pick === null) return null;
  const text = pick.percent === 100 ? `${pick.goal.name} is done` : `${pick.goal.name} is ${pick.percent}% there`;
  return (
    <View accessibilityLiveRegion="polite" style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: spacing.md, padding: spacing.md, borderRadius: radius.lg, borderCurve: "continuous", backgroundColor: colors.goodTint }}>
      <Gullak mood="celebrating" stage="clay" size={64} />
      <Text variant="bodyStrong" color={colors.goodDeep} style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        {text}
      </Text>
      <Button
        title="Nice"
        variant="ghost"
        onPress={() => {
          const next = [...seen, pick.key];
          setSeen(next);
          void kv.set(SEEN_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
        }}
      />
    </View>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/screens/goals-screen.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/empty-state";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/error-state";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Screen } from "@/components/ui/screen";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { describeError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { messageFor } from "@/lib/errors";
import { spacing } from "@/theme";
import { GoalCard } from "../components/goal-card";
import { MilestoneBanner } from "../components/milestone-banner";
import { MonthlyCard } from "../components/monthly-card";
import { useGoals } from "../hooks";

type Props = { onNew: () => void; onOpen: (id: string) => void; onEditSavings: () => void; now?: Date };

/** The Goals mockup. At most 20 deadline goals exist (server cap), so a plain list inside the scroll view is fine. */
export function GoalsScreen({ onNew, onOpen, onEditSavings, now = new Date() }: Props) {
  const goals = useGoals();
  return (
    <Screen testID="goals">
      <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", minHeight: 48 }}>
        <Text variant="heading" accessibilityRole="header">
          Goals
        </Text>
        <Button title="New goal" variant="secondary" icon={<Icon name="plus" size={18} />} onPress={onNew} />
      </View>
      {!goals.data && goals.isLoading ? (
        <View testID="goals-skeleton" style={{ gap: spacing.md }}>
          <Skeleton height={140} />
          <Skeleton height={20} width="30%" />
          <Skeleton height={160} />
        </View>
      ) : !goals.data ? (
        <ErrorState body={describeError(goals.error, messageFor)} onRetry={() => void goals.refetch()} />
      ) : (
        <>
          <MonthlyCard monthly={goals.data.monthly} onEdit={onEditSavings} />
          <MilestoneBanner goals={goals.data.goals} now={now.getTime()} />
          <Text variant="title">Quests</Text>
          {goals.data.goals.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              title="No goals yet"
              body="Set a goal like Goa by March and Gullak tells you whether you are on track"
              action={<Button title="New goal" onPress={onNew} />}
            />
          ) : (
            goals.data.goals.map((g) => <GoalCard key={g.id} goal={g} onOpen={onOpen} />)
          )}
          {goals.data.goals.length > 0 ? <Text variant="caption">The tick on each bar marks today's target</Text> : null}
        </>
      )}
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`app/src/features/goals/screens/savings-sheet.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/error-state";
import { Screen } from "@/components/ui/screen";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { RealityCheck } from "@/features/profile/components/reality-check";
import { SavingsStepper } from "@/features/profile/components/savings-stepper";
import { useFixedCosts, useProfile } from "@/features/profile/hooks";
import { paydayRuleOf } from "@/features/profile/payday";
import { describeError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { perDayPreview, realityCheck, wholeRupees } from "@/lib/budget";
import { todayIst } from "@/lib/dates";
import { messageFor } from "@/lib/errors";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import { colors, spacing } from "@/theme";
import type { GoalMode } from "../api";
import { ModePicker } from "../components/mode-picker";
import { useGoals, useSaveSavings } from "../hooks";

const MIN_PER_DAY_PAISE = 25000; // Plan 2's "tight" line

/** Monthly savings and "If I overspend" after setup (the Goals tab's control). */
export function SavingsSheet({ onDone, today = todayIst() }: { onDone: () => void; today?: string }) {
  const profile = useProfile();
  const costs = useFixedCosts();
  const goals = useGoals();
  const save = useSaveSavings();
  const [savings, setSavings] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<GoalMode | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (profile.isLoading || costs.isLoading || goals.isLoading) {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <Skeleton height={80} />
        <Skeleton height={120} />
      </Screen>
    );
  }
  if (!profile.data || !costs.data || !goals.data) {
    const failed = profile.error ?? costs.error ?? goals.error;
    return (
      <Screen>
        <ErrorState body={failed ? describeError(failed, messageFor) : "Finish setup first"} onRetry={() => void Promise.all([profile.refetch(), costs.refetch(), goals.refetch()])} />
      </Screen>
    );
  }

  const p = profile.data;
  const monthly = goals.data.monthly;
  const value = savings ?? p.savingsPaise;
  const chosenMode = mode ?? monthly.mode;
  const fixedPaise = costs.data.reduce((s, c) => s + c.amountPaise, 0);
  const next = perDayPreview({ freePaise: p.incomePaise - fixedPaise - value, today, payday: paydayRuleOf(p) });
  const check = realityCheck({ incomePaise: p.incomePaise, fixedPaise, savingsPaise: value, cycleDays: next.cycleDays });
  const suggested = wholeRupees(Math.max(0, Math.floor((p.incomePaise - fixedPaise - MIN_PER_DAY_PAISE * next.cycleDays) / 100000) * 100000));
  const floor = monthly.committedPaise;
  const belowFloor = value < floor;
  const notPossible = check.verdict === "notPossible";
  const title = belowFloor ? `Your goals need at least ${formatRupees(floor)}` : notPossible ? "Lower your savings to save" : "Save";

  async function submit() {
    setError(null);
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync({ profile: p, savingsPaise: value, monthlyId: monthly.id, mode: chosenMode !== monthly.mode ? chosenMode : null });
      onDone();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(describeError(e, messageFor));
    }
  }

  return (
    <Screen
      testID="savings-sheet"
      footer={
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
          {error ? (
            <Text variant="label" color={colors.bad} accessibilityRole="alert">
              {error}
            </Text>
          ) : null}
          <Button title={title} onPress={() => void submit()} disabled={belowFloor || notPossible} loading={save.isPending} />
          <Button title="Cancel" variant="ghost" onPress={onDone} />
        </View>
      }
    >
      <Text variant="heading" accessibilityRole="header">
        Monthly savings
      </Text>
      <Text variant="bodyMuted">This comes out first. Your daily budget is what's left.</Text>
      <SavingsStepper valuePaise={value} max={Math.max(0, p.incomePaise - fixedPaise)} onChange={setSavings} />
      <RealityCheck verdict={check.verdict} perDayPaise={check.perDayPaise} essentialsLeftPaise={p.incomePaise - fixedPaise} suggestedSavingsPaise={suggested} />
      {floor > 0 ? (
        <Text variant="label" color={belowFloor ? colors.bad : colors.muted}>
          {`Your goals need ${formatRupees(floor)} a month`}
        </Text>
      ) : null}
      <ModePicker variant="savings" value={chosenMode} onChange={setMode} />
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`app/app/(tabs)/goals.tsx` (replaces Plan 2's placeholder):

```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useFocusEffect, useRouter } from "expo-router";
import React, { useCallback } from "react";
import { goalKeys } from "@/features/goals/keys";
import { GoalsScreen } from "@/features/goals/screens/goals-screen";

export { RouteErrorFallback as ErrorBoundary } from "@/components/error-boundary";

export default function GoalsRoute() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  // Coming back to the tab (from a goal, a sheet or Home) shows fresh numbers.
  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: goalKeys.list });
    }, [qc]),
  );
  return (
    <GoalsScreen
      onNew={() => router.push("/goals/new")}
      onOpen={(id) => router.push({ pathname: "/goals/[id]", params: { id } })}
      onEditSavings={() => router.push("/goals/savings")}
    />
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider, onlineManager } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import React from "react";
import type { CatchUp, Today } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { NetworkError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { HomeScreen } from "./home-screen";

jest.mock("@/features/budget/api", () => ({ budgetApi: { getToday: jest.fn(), setFirstCycle: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/features/goals/api", () => ({ goalsApi: { catchUp: jest.fn(), undoCatchUp: jest.fn(), list: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/lib/kv", () => ({ kv: { get: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve("1")), set: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve()), remove: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve()) } }));
jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ Redirect: () => null }));
import { budgetApi } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { goalsApi } from "@/features/goals/api";

const behind: CatchUp = {
  situation: "BEHIND",
  goalId: "g",
  goalName: "Goa trip",
  strict: false,
  gapPaise: 180000,
  options: [
    { kind: "TIGHTEN", perDayPaise: 22500, days: 8, tomorrowPaise: 35200 },
    { kind: "MOVE", newDeadline: "2027-04", extraPaydays: 1 },
    { kind: "LOWER", newTargetPaise: 2820000 },
  ],
};

/** The HomeOver mockup: Fri 23 Oct, ₹720 spent of ₹593. */
const overDay: Today = {
  date: "2026-10-23",
  budgetPaise: 59300,
  spentPaise: 72000,
  leftPaise: 0,
  overPaise: 12700,
  poolOverPaise: 0,
  mood: "FAINTED",
  line: "Over by ₹127. Happens. Tomorrow’s a fresh start.",
  daysLeft: 9,
  cycleStart: "2026-10-01",
  cycleEnd: "2026-10-31",
  tomorrowPaise: 57700,
  catchUp: behind,
};

/** An ordinary day: ₹180 of ₹593 spent, nothing to catch up. */
const ordinary: Today = {
  date: "2026-10-23",
  budgetPaise: 59300,
  spentPaise: 18000,
  leftPaise: 41300,
  overPaise: 0,
  poolOverPaise: 0,
  mood: "HAPPY",
  line: "₹413 left for today. Nice and steady.",
  daysLeft: 9,
  cycleStart: "2026-10-01",
  cycleEnd: "2026-10-31",
  tomorrowPaise: 64000,
};

const tightened: Today = {
  ...overDay,
  tomorrowPaise: 35200,
  catchUp: { ...behind, situation: "APPLIED", options: [], applied: { actionId: "a", kind: "TIGHTEN", perDayPaise: 22500, days: 8, canUndo: true } },
};

function renderHome() {
  render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, networkMode: "offlineFirst" } } })}>
      <HomeScreen now={new Date("2026-10-23T14:00:00Z")} />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  jest.clearAllMocks();
  onlineManager.setOnline(true);
});

describe("Home over budget with the catch-up card (APP-23)", () => {
  it("shows how far over, tomorrow's lower budget and three ways to catch up", async () => {
    (budgetApi.getToday as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue(overDay);
    renderHome();
    expect(await screen.findByText("₹127 over")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("₹720 spent of ₹593 today. Tomorrow's budget drops to ₹577.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("progressbar", { name: "Budget used" })).toHaveAccessibilityValue({ now: 100 });
    expect(screen.getByText("Goa trip: ₹1,800 behind")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Earlier months came up short. Pick a way to catch up.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Spend ₹225 less a day for 8 days" })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Move the deadline to Apr 2027" })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Lower the target to ₹28,200" })).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("a pick updates tomorrow's budget and the card title; Undo restores both", async () => {
    (budgetApi.getToday as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue(overDay);
    (goalsApi.catchUp as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ goal: { id: "g" }, today: tightened });
    (goalsApi.undoCatchUp as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ goal: { id: "g" }, today: overDay });
    renderHome();
    fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Spend ₹225 less a day for 8 days" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Goa trip: catching up")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(goalsApi.catchUp).toHaveBeenCalledWith("g", "TIGHTEN");
    expect(screen.getByText("₹720 spent of ₹593 today. Tomorrow's budget drops to ₹352.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Done. Tomorrow's budget is ₹352, and Goa trip is back on track by Sat, 31 Oct.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Plan saved. You can change it any time in Goals.")).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Undo" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Goa trip: ₹1,800 behind")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("₹720 spent of ₹593 today. Tomorrow's budget drops to ₹577.")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("a failed pick keeps the options and says why (ERR-14)", async () => {
    (budgetApi.getToday as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue(overDay);
    (goalsApi.catchUp as jest.Mock).mockRejectedValue(new NetworkError("network"));
    renderHome();
    fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Lower the target to ₹28,200" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("No connection. Check your network and try again.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Lower the target to ₹28,200" })).toBeEnabled();
  });

  it("on an ordinary day a goal behind shows tomorrow's budget too", async () => {
    (budgetApi.getToday as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ ...ordinary, catchUp: behind });
    renderHome();
    expect(await screen.findByText("₹413")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByTestId("tomorrow-budget")).toHaveTextContent("Tomorrow's budget: ₹640");
    expect(screen.getByText("Goa trip: ₹1,800 behind")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("with nothing to catch up there is no card and no tomorrow line", async () => {
    (budgetApi.getToday as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue(ordinary);
    renderHome();
    expect(await screen.findByText("₹413")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("catch-up-card")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("tomorrow-budget")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("an empty pool: ₹0 until payday and the take option", async () => {
    (budgetApi.getToday as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({
      ...overDay, budgetPaise: 0, spentPaise: 0, leftPaise: 0, overPaise: 0, poolOverPaise: 40000, line: "Over by ₹400. Happens. Tomorrow’s a fresh start.",
```

`app/src/features/home/hero.tsx` (Plan 2's hero with the over-budget state and the tomorrow line):

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { View } from "react-native";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { ProgressBar } from "@/components/ui/progress-bar";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Gullak } from "@/features/gullak/gullak";
import { fromServerMood } from "@/features/gullak/moods";
import type { Today } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { formatDay } from "@/lib/dates";
import { formatRupees } from "@/lib/money";
import { colors, radius, spacing } from "@/theme";

const FIRST_RUN_LINE = "Hi, I'm your Gullak. Log a spend and I'll keep count.";

export function percentUsed(t: Today): number {
  if (t.budgetPaise <= 0) return t.spentPaise > 0 ? 100 : 0;
  return Math.min(100, Math.round((t.spentPaise * 100) / t.budgetPaise));
}

/** The HomeOver mockup's line under the red figure. One sentence has no full stop; two take one each. */
export function overCaption(t: Today): string {
  if (t.budgetPaise === 0 && t.spentPaise === 0) return "Your budget is ₹0 until payday";
  const spent = `${formatRupees(t.spentPaise)} spent of ${formatRupees(t.budgetPaise)} today`;
  if (t.tomorrowPaise === undefined) return spent;
  const verb = t.tomorrowPaise < t.budgetPaise ? "drops to" : "is";
  return `${spent}. Tomorrow's budget ${verb} ${formatRupees(t.tomorrowPaise)}.`;
}

export function Hero({ today, firstRun }: { today: Today; firstRun: boolean }) {
  const mood = fromServerMood(today.mood);
  const used = percentUsed(today);
  const overBy = today.overPaise + today.poolOverPaise;
  const tone = mood === "fainted" ? "bad" : mood === "nervous" ? "warn" : "ink";
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: spacing.md, padding: spacing.md, borderRadius: radius.lg, borderCurve: "continuous", backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.line }}>
      <View style={{ alignItems: "center", gap: spacing.sm }}>
        <Gullak mood={mood} stage="clay" size={160} />
        <Text variant="body" align="center">
          {firstRun ? FIRST_RUN_LINE : today.line}
        </Text>
      </View>
      {overBy > 0 ? (
        <View style={{ gap: spacing.sm }}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", gap: spacing.sm }}>
            <Icon name="alert" color={colors.bad} />
            <Text variant="hero" color={colors.bad} style={{ fontVariant: ["tabular-nums"] }}>
              {`${formatRupees(overBy)} over`}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <ProgressBar value={100} label="Budget used" tone="bad" height={12} />
          <Text variant="label" color={colors.muted} testID="tomorrow-budget">
            {overCaption(today)}
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <>
          <View style={{ alignItems: "center" }}>
            <Text variant="display" style={{ fontVariant: ["tabular-nums"] }}>
              {formatRupees(today.leftPaise)}
            </Text>
            <Text variant="bodyMuted">{`left of ${formatRupees(today.budgetPaise)} today`}</Text>
          </View>
          <ProgressBar value={used} label="Budget used" tone={tone} />
          <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
            <Text variant="label">{`${formatRupees(today.spentPaise)} spent`}</Text>
            <Text variant="labelMuted">{firstRun ? "Your streak starts tonight if you stay under" : ""}</Text>
          </View>
          {today.catchUp !== undefined && today.tomorrowPaise !== undefined ? (
            <Text variant="label" testID="tomorrow-budget">{`Tomorrow's budget: ${formatRupees(today.tomorrowPaise)}`}</Text>
          ) : null}
        </>
      )}
      <Text variant="caption">{`${today.daysLeft} day${today.daysLeft === 1 ? "" : "s"} left in this cycle, until ${formatDay(today.cycleEnd)}`}</Text>
    </View>
  );
}
```

If Plan 3 or Plan 6 changed `Hero` (the coin-drop event, the streak chip), keep those additions; the over-budget branch and the tomorrow line are the only changes here.

`app/src/features/home/home-catch-up.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useState } from "react";
import type { CatchUp, Today } from "@/features/budget/api";
import { CatchUpCard } from "@/features/goals/components/catch-up-card";
import { useCatchUp, useUndoCatchUp } from "@/features/goals/hooks";
import { describeError } from "@/lib/api-errors";
import { messageFor } from "@/lib/errors";

/** Home's catch-up card (spec 2.2). A pick or an undo returns the new day, which replaces Home's figures at once. */
export function HomeCatchUp({ catchUp, today }: { catchUp: CatchUp; today: Today }) {
  const pick = useCatchUp();
  const undo = useUndoCatchUp();
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const run = async (action: () => Promise<unknown>) => {
    setError(null);
    try {
      await action();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(describeError(e, messageFor));
    }
  };

  return (
    <CatchUpCard
      catchUp={catchUp}
      tomorrowPaise={today.tomorrowPaise}
      cycleEnd={today.cycleEnd}
      busy={pick.isPending || undo.isPending}
      error={error}
      onPick={(goalId, kind) => void run(() => pick.mutateAsync({ goalId, kind }))}
      onUndo={(goalId) => void run(() => undo.mutateAsync(goalId))}
    />
  );
}
```

`app/app/(tabs)/index.tsx` (keep anything Plan 3 added to the route):

```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useFocusEffect } from "expo-router";
import React, { useCallback } from "react";
import { todayKey } from "@/features/budget/hooks";
import { HomeScreen } from "@/features/home/home-screen";

export { RouteErrorFallback as ErrorBoundary } from "@/components/error-boundary";

export default function HomeRoute() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  // Back on Home after a goal change, a sheet or another tab: today's figures and catch-up card are fetched again.
  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: todayKey });
    }, [qc]),
  );
  return <HomeScreen />;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: The Maestro flow (E2E-06)**

`app/e2e/scripts/fall-behind.js`:

```js
// Signs in through the API with the local dev code, finds the user's goal and moves it two paydays behind
// (POST /dev/goals/{id}/fall-behind exists only under the server's local profile).
const base = typeof GULLAK_API !== "undefined" ? GULLAK_API : "http://10.0.2.2:8080/api/v1";
const jsonHeaders = { "Content-Type": "application/json" };

let res = http.post(base + "/auth/otp/request", { headers: jsonHeaders, body: JSON.stringify({ email: output.email }) });
if (!res.ok) throw new Error("otp request failed: " + res.status);
res = http.post(base + "/auth/dev/otp", { headers: jsonHeaders, body: JSON.stringify({ email: output.email }) });
if (!res.ok) throw new Error("dev otp failed: " + res.status);
const code = json(res.body).code;
res = http.post(base + "/auth/otp/verify", { headers: jsonHeaders, body: JSON.stringify({ email: output.email, code: code, device: "maestro" }) });
if (!res.ok) throw new Error("verify failed: " + res.status);
const auth = { Authorization: "Bearer " + json(res.body).accessToken };

res = http.get(base + "/goals", { headers: auth });
const goals = json(res.body).goals;
if (!goals || goals.length === 0) throw new Error("no goal to move behind");
res = http.post(base + "/dev/goals/" + goals[0].id + "/fall-behind", { headers: auth, body: "" });
if (!res.ok) throw new Error("fall-behind failed: " + res.status);
```

`app/e2e/goal-catch-up.yaml`:

```yaml
appId: in.gullak.app
---
# E2E-06: new goal → Goals → force behind (test data) → catch-up tighten → Home shows the lower budget → undo.
- runFlow: setup.yaml
- tapOn: "Goals"
- assertVisible: "No goals yet"
# Setup's flow keeps savings at ₹0, so give the goal something to come from.
- tapOn: "Edit savings"
- assertVisible: "Monthly savings"
- repeat:
    times: 5
    commands:
      - tapOn: "Save ₹1,000 more"
- tapOn: "Save"
- assertVisible: "This cycle's savings"
- tapOn:
    text: "New goal"
    index: 0
- assertVisible: "Name your goal to start"
- tapOn: "Goal name"
- inputText: "Goa trip"
- hideKeyboard
- tapOn: "Target, ₹60,000, tap to type an amount"
- eraseText: 10
- inputText: "5000"
- tapOn: "Done"
- assertVisible: "Doable"
- tapOn: "Start this quest"
- assertVisible: "Goa trip"
- assertVisible: "On track"
- runScript: scripts/fall-behind.js
- tapOn: "Home"
- assertVisible:
    text: "Goa trip: ₹.* behind"
- copyTextFrom:
    id: "tomorrow-budget"
- evalScript: ${output.before = Number(maestro.copiedText.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''))}
- tapOn:
    text: "Spend ₹.* less a day for .*"
- assertVisible: "Goa trip: catching up"
- assertVisible:
    text: "Done. Tomorrow's budget is ₹.*"
- copyTextFrom:
    id: "tomorrow-budget"
- evalScript: ${output.after = Number(maestro.copiedText.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''))}
- assertTrue: ${output.after < output.before}
- tapOn: "Undo"
- assertVisible:
    text: "Goa trip: ₹.* behind"
- copyTextFrom:
    id: "tomorrow-budget"
- assertTrue: ${Number(maestro.copiedText.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')) === output.before}
```

````markdown
## E2E-06: goal and catch-up

```bash
cd app && maestro test -e GULLAK_API=http://10.0.2.2:8080/api/v1 e2e/goal-catch-up.yaml
```

It runs `setup.yaml` first, sets savings to ₹5,000 from the Goals tab, creates "Goa trip" (₹5,000), moves it two paydays behind through the local-only `POST /api/v1/dev/goals/{id}/fall-behind`, picks "Spend … less", checks that tomorrow's budget on Home went down, and undoes it. Do not run it on the last day of a cycle: "Spend less" is not offered then.
````
